## TU1 blockers, round 6: copies across joins, and a declaration climb on `0204e5d4` (main, round mainpool12)

Nothing matched this round, so TU1 is still not merged. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/mainpool12/` (`d/` drafts, `v/` sweeps; `v/t524.sh` is the round mainpool10 tester pointed at this clone).

- **`func_0204e5d4` (40 to 30).** `hc.py` over the 31 declarations (round mainpool5 ran it before the type scan) fixed the `bx` and `fn` spill slots. Draft `d/e5d4_30.cpp`. A second 1500-round climb and the type scan (`ts.py`) from there found nothing. Left: `pitch` and `src` swap spill slots (`0xc`/`0x2c`), and `bp`/`w`/`sh` rotate in the pixel loop. Block-scoping any subset of `bp`, `w`, `sh` and `t`, the order and operand order of the three pixel statements, a separate variable for the end-of-glyph `bp`, and a 200-round climb over the order of the six per-glyph setup statements all gave exactly 30. So the pixel-loop registers follow the global numbering (declarations), not that block.
- **The zero copy crosses a join when its source is a copy.** In the `0204f524` tester, `u16 z; ... z = i; if ((u32)win & 3) { a = ...; c = ...; z = i; }` (with `long i`, after `i = 0; win->numBlocks = i;`) and `z` for every zero in the loop gives the target's instructions, including `mov r4, r5`. Only the registers rotate (16: `i` r4, `a` r5, `c` r6, `z` r7; the target has `z` r4, `i` r5, `a` r6, `c` r7). With a `u32 z` the backend also hoists `mov r4, r8`, a copy of `z` across the join. A two-definition constant (`z = 0; if (...) z = 0;`) gives `mov r4, #0` instead. So the backend's "register known to hold 0" survives a join for a two-definition copy of a multi-definition `long`, but not for a two-definition constant. A single `z = i` is propagated as 0 (back to 3 diffs). About 2300 type, declaration, statement and loop-form samples of this form (`v/zs.py`, `v/zt.py`, `v/zr.py`) never ranked `z` below `i`. The same holds for the 1440 declaration and copy orders of the round mainpool10 `n`/`z` form (`v/zc.sh`, best 6: `c` and `z` swapped). The best are still the old 3 (`0204f524`) and 1 (`0204f5bc`). In `0204f5bc`, putting the definitions and the `if` before `if (win->page >= win->numPages)` gives the copy, but the two `mov`s stay above the `cmp` (4).
- **`func_02051a34` (35).** Moving `Model *m` to function scope changes the result a lot with its position (98 to 35), but no position beats 35, and a 600-round declaration climb with it found nothing. A two-definition mask variable (`s32 mk`, `win->args[0] != (mk >> 16)`, the `textPos` store written as `(w & mk) | (((u32)(u16)pos << 16) >> 16)`) gives the target's instructions, but `mk` gets `fp` (the target puts the mask in `r6`) and its `mov`/`rsb` move into the entry block (44). Defining `mk` in the inner loop's preheader needs the inner `while` written as `if (...) { ...; do { } while (...); }` with an `if (c == 0)` second definition. That puts the `mov`/`rsb` in the target's place, but it turns the outer loop into a guarded form (102). `if ((u32)win & 3)` there is hoisted out of the loop and stays.
- **`func_0204ffdc` (52).** `v/dyins.py` put `if ((u32)box & 3) { dy = 0; }` before every statement, with `s32` and `long dy` (about 260 compiles): none was below 52. `long dy` keeps `dy` as a variable everywhere, but `busy` then gets its own `mov #0`. Copies from multi-definition locals (`dx = 0; dy = dx; if (...) dy = dx;`, also with `i`, `alpha`, `t` and `long` sources) behave like the constant. Function-scope `ln`/`j`, reusing `line`/`i` for the final loop (175) and function-scope `x`/`y` did not help either.
- **`func_020508f4` (8).** `tdv2.py` (259 placements), block-scoped `n`, `i`, `tag` and `c`, and reusing the `str` parameter instead of `s` (40) left the `fn`/`dst` swap.

## Scope moves on overlay near-misses (overlays under 0x500 bytes, round ovhi22)

No new matches. This round tested the round itcm5 finding (function-scope temporaries decide registers, declaration order decides stack slots) on 24 overlay near-misses with 2 to 15 diffs. Drafts, logs and tools are in `scratchpad/ovhi22/` (`d/<mod>_<addr>/base.*`, `scope*.out`, `tdv2_a.out`).

- **`t/scope.py src func mod`** builds every scope move for one function (or for an inline with `SCOPE_IN=<name>`) and scores each move alone, then pairs of the best `SCOPE_PAIRS` (default 24). `SCOPE_DRY=<dir>` writes the variants without compiling them. The moves are:
  - `hoist`: every block-scoped declaration of a name goes to an enclosing block, up to function scope. Initialisers become assignments in place. In `.c` files the assignment goes after the block's declarations.
  - `hoistgrp`: all block-scoped names of one type in the same blocks move together, plus every 3-name subset when a group has 4 to 6 names. This is the round itcm5 `cx, cy, cz` move.
  - `sink`: a declaration moves into the innermost block that holds all of its uses, or into any block in between.
  - `wrap`: a declaration's use spans (for example two loops that share an index) each get a new block with their own declaration. The tool tries each span alone and all spans together.
  - `merge`: two block-scoped names of one type whose line ranges do not overlap become one function-scope variable.
  - `uninit`: `T a = e;` becomes `T a;` plus `a = e;` after the declaration list.
  - `order`: each function-scope declaration moves to every other slot of the declaration list.
- **Sanity check:** on the pre-fix itcm5 draft (`itcm5/9f9c/g4.cpp`), `hoistgrp cx+cy+cz` takes the masked score from 197 to 153. That is the known lever, so the tool does find this kind of move.
- **Results on the overlay batch:** about 7,400 compiles across all runs. No move improved any of the 24 functions. Many moves changed nothing at all, which means MWCC gave the same code whatever the scope. In the drafts that use an inline (`021708ec` `Lo_FindMsg`, `021be5dc` `CalcDepth`, `02199180` `DistSq9180`), declaration order inside the inline does matter. Writing the same body by hand in the caller does not: `02199180` scores 10 for all 18 orders and scopes of `dz, dx, dy`.
- **Other tools on the same drafts:** `tdv2.py` (`TDV_MAXD=8`) on the ten ovmid2 drafts found `021d8d00` 13 to 12 (a second `chr = last->chr` right after its declaration, `best/d8d00_12.cpp`; still 7 with registers masked). It also found the known `021c4460` 10 to 9, which needs an extra `self->unk_a4 = n` store and so is not real.
- **Hand forms that did not move (all scored):**
  - `021e2690`: function-scope `8`/`3` variables in any declaration or assignment order, a scoped `t = 8` at every level with the swapped store order. Copy propagation removes them; the store order still sets the registers.
  - `02211b2c`: a `self + 0x14c` pointer local for any subset of the four calls, the name argument in a local, `char *` and index spellings.
  - `021e1204`: no entry pointer, an assignment inside the `if`, a table local, function-scope `k`, `k` reused for `unk_06`, `e` in its own block.
  - `021ec010`: every position of `p = *src`, a `self` copy, other types for `z` (`s32`/`int`/`u16`/`u32` give 32).
  - `021be5dc`: function-scope `z`, reusing `q`/`w`, `ToDepth2`/`ToDepth3`, operand order of every `FX_MUL`.
  - `021fff38`: all 24 orders of the `t` stores and the `home` load (best 7), temporaries for `x`/`y`/`z` at either scope (copy-propagated, 8), a setter inline (8).
  - `021c4460`: declaration order in the walk block matters (10 to 27) but never gives the target's `n`/`idx` registers. Reusing `key`/`kq` for the index or the counter gave 10 to 27.
  - `0220640c`: a wrapper inline around `func_0203cb78` that holds 0 to 5 of the preceding stores (6 everywhere).
  - `0218a5ec`: reusing the dead `s`/`e`/`x` for `t`/`k` (2 at best).
  - `021ffd0c`: field copies interleaved with the vector copy (27 to 29).
- **Pool-bound:** `021e1204` (2) and `021e6274` (8) did not change, so the ov003 pool merge is still blocked. `021cf420` (15) is register-only at its core (the entry zero is `r6`, the target has `r7`), but it is ov006-pool-bound with three far-off users.
## TU2 blockers, round 7: where a sunk global store goes (main, round mainpool13)

Nothing matched this round, so TU2 is still not merged. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/mainpool13/` (`d/`, `t/`).

- **The bare-block tools find nothing on TU2.** `blk.py`, `near.py` (`NEAR` up to 8) and `fscope.py` (with `FS_PAIRS`) left all five blockers unchanged: `0205e5c4` 2, `0205c234` 2, `0205e29c` 9, `02060a08` 13 and `02060b18` 16. `blk.py` finds no bare block next to a simple statement in the last three.
  - In `c234`, `t/c2gen.py` wrapped every contiguous run of the second set of stores in a bare block (760 variants, also with `start` in a nested block). The block never changes the code. Only the position of `start = data_02149efc.slotStart;` matters.
  - In `e5c4`, `t/e5gen.py` combined orders with `unk_14` before `unk_04`, the start of the `{ id ... }` block and an inner bare block (8616 variants). The best is 4, the round mainpool9 form (`res`, both stores, then `id`). `e5_g4.cpp` has the target's registers and store order but loads `id` after the stores.
- **A zero store to a global sinks or stays depending on its offset and the global's declared size.** Take `e5c4` with one store in place of the pair, written as `*(u32 *)((u8 *)&G + off) = 0`:
  - With `data_02149efc` (`ObjPoolGlobals`, 0x1c bytes), a store at `+0x10` or above (including offsets past the end, up to `+0x100`) is scheduled just after the two `slot` loads, with its address and zero.
  - A store below `+0x10` sinks, together with its address `ldr` and `mov #0`, to just before the `LightParam_Init` key store at `[sp, #0x10]`. That moves the registers (`id` goes to `sb`, and there is an extra push).
  - The threshold follows the size of the declared type, not the field types: a 4-byte type or a 0x1c-byte `u32[7]` struct keeps `+0x14` up, a 0x18-byte one sinks below `+0xc`, a 0x20-byte one below `+0x10`, and a `u32[16]` sinks up to `+0x2c`. `data_02fe1534` and `data_02149f18` sink at every offset tried.
  - The `slot` loads, the parameter struct's size, the field types, the base spelling (index, cast, member of `pool`) and the store's type (`u32`, `s32`, `StageRes **`) make no difference.

  So with the source order `unk_14`, `unk_04`, the `unk_04` store sinks (17). With `unk_04`, `unk_14`, the store order drags `unk_04` up with `unk_14` (2). Padding `ObjPoolGlobals` to sizes up to 0x504 bytes, or declaring it as `[1]` or `[]`, does not give the target's order (17 to 18). `__restrict` on `slot` lets the loads move above the stores, but it changes the whole schedule (20 to 33). An `unk_04 = unk_14` copy, `NULL`-returning inlines, setter inlines and local `o`/`l` copies all stay at 17.
- **`func_0205e29c` (9): the late `unk_14` store follows from the register swap.** In the target, the `Stage_FindObj3D` result (merged with the `unk_80` zero) is in `r3`, so its `str [r7, #0x14]` can sink to just before the `bl`. In ours it is in `r2`, which `and r2, r8, #0xff` overwrites, so the store has to come first. These all stay at 9 or worse:
  - redeclaring `data_ov000_021ccd64` with other types;
  - loading it into a local before the `unk_14` store (15, because this drops the inline's second `id == 0` test);
  - `Stage_FindLight1` and `u8` forms of the inline;
  - 72 type and declaration orders of `id`/`b`;
  - five spellings of the `GetArea` multiply.
- **`func_0205c234` (2).** The `ldr` of a constant address always lands one store before its load. These did not move it:
  - a two-definition `g` placed anywhere from the loop exit to `start`, with `start` after `args[3]` and five conditions (35 or worse);
  - a copy `h = g` of a `g` with two definitions at the top (42, because `g` lives in `ip` the whole time);
  - writing the function with `Slot_Alloc`/`Slot_Init` and a `Slot_Last` inline (45, because `slotEnd` is loaded again);
  - seven spellings of the `slotStart` read;
  - compiling it as C++;
  - other `-proc` values.
- **Tools.** In `scratchpad/mainpool13/t/`: the bigsweep1 tools pointed at this clone, plus `e5gen.py`, `e5dec.py` (scope and order of `id`/`type`/`lo`, 162 variants, all 17), `e5ty.py` (declared type of `data_02149efc`), `c2gen.py`, `e29v.py`, `e29ty.py`, and `hdr.sh` (compiles a draft against a copy of `StageCmd.h` with one extern redeclared).

## Pointer locals plus array indexing for operand order (round bigsweep2)

- **The trick.** MWCC chose the wrong evaluation order for `FX_Atan2(a.x - b.x, a.z - b.z)` on two globals: it loaded the z term first. Writing the operands as indexes through block-local pointers gives the target's x-first order and its registers:
  ```c
  {
      const s32 *b = (const s32 *)&data_02092a38;
      const s32 *a = (const s32 *)&data_02092a50;
      self->rot[1] = FX_Atan2(b[0] - a[0], b[2] - a[2]);
  }
  ```
- **Both parts are needed.** Pointer locals with `->x` still scored 6. Casts with indexes but no locals also scored 6. Our working theory is that an offset-0 field access counts as cheaper when MWCC orders the operands, and the index form hides that.
- **Matches from this trick:**
  - `ov011_022038ac`
  - `ov011_02204428` (a long-standing 6-diff)
  - `ov011_02203ef0` (0x40c bytes)
- **Where it did not help:**
  - `ov000_0219d9fc`: the `0x9ac < 0x9ae` u16 compare kept its load order. We tried index, swapped, casts, temporaries and `!(<=)`.
  - `ov000_021ffb90`: 7 against 8, with a different shape.
- **Partial gains, not yet matches:**
  - `ov013_021fc7e0`, 9 → 6: move the `+0x3c = 0xb33` store after the `v14(0x800,0x800,0x800)` call.
  - `ov000_02171478`: `u32 index = (u32)i < LEN ? i : i - LEN;` gives the target's `mov; subhs`. An end/index register swap remains.
  - `ov000_0219e998`: assigning `ok = GIdle...()` gives the target's structure. Only an r0/r1 swap remains.
  - `ov000_021a2670`: inlining `(u16 >> 12) != 5` and placing `anm` right after `lock` fixes the 0xb24 register.
- **Scratch tools:**
  - `vrf.py`: applies variants inside one function; an `@TOP@` prefix applies the edit to the whole file.
  - `fsn.py`: applies all neutral `fscope` moves together.
  - `pipe.sh`: runs fsn, then blk, then blk again.
- **No gain:**
  - `0220cbe8`: we could not stop `p+0x168` from being folded.
  - `021fc688`
  - `021e8ef8`: also 1300+ permuter iterations.
  - `02189b44`
  - `021d3e14`
  - `021b209c`
  - `021afaf8`
  - `021fc0b8`
  - `021ad8ac`

## Declaration-position grids, a product temporary, and main near-misses (round mainmid4)

- **`func_0203b12c` (now `src/Main/Gfx/GfxMgrText_2.cpp`).** It needed two changes.
  - The pixel offset is `bpp * pos`. With `db = (u16)bpp * db`, the destination is an operand, so the `mul` puts `db` first. Writing the product in both of its uses (`dst = buf + (((u16)bpp * pos) >> 3); db = ((u16)bpp * pos) & 7;`) keeps `bpp` first.
  - The callee-saved registers then rotated. Single scope moves did not fix this, and neither did pairs from `scope.py`. A grid over the declaration positions of two locals (`pos` and `b`, each at every top-level slot) found the order: `int pos;` first, and `u32 b;` after `u32 cur;`. If a pair search finds nothing, try every position of two variables, not just moves between block and function scope.
  - The function could not be appended to `GfxMgrText.cpp`, because that file's local `GfxNnsFont` prototypes conflict with `nnsys/g2d/g2d_Font.h`. So it went into a new file.
- **`func_0203c2bc` (4 diffs after register normalisation).** `sys` lands in r4 if any one of these statements is removed: `REG16(0x4000050)=0`, `func_02043904(...)`, the palette init, or some of the `REG32` writes. So the cause is register pressure, not the scope of a local. Moving `REG16(0x4000050)=0` earlier helps, but it reorders `volatile` writes, so it is not a valid fix.
- **`func_0206e858`.** Address constants are CSE'd across calls no matter how the code is written. These all failed: labels, blocks, constant conditions, inlines, `volatile` casts on the global, `static const` pointers and table forms. Putting the calls inside switch arms does give two loads of the same pool word, but the tail-merge shape is wrong.
- **`func_02015510`.** A fake `case 5` or `case 6` in `default` brings the switch pivot to `0x80`, leaving one diff: `cmp #5` or `cmp #6` where the target has `cmp #4`. No case set found so far gives an upper bound of 4.
- **Unchanged:**
  - `0206d43c` and `0206d99c`: these two share a pool. Over 240 variants of inline, manual expansion and declaration order were tried.
  - `0203d298`, `02049690`, `0203a984`, `0206f6d4`, `02064b54` and `0206e3a0`.
  - Permuter runs of an hour on `a984` and `9690` did not improve them.
- **Tools.** In `scratchpad/mainmid4/t/`:
  - `bo.sh func files...` scores many drafts in parallel, giving the raw and the register-normalised score.
  - `scope.py` now builds its pair stage from the best moves of both kinds: non-order moves and order moves (`SCOPE_ORD`, `SCOPE_DELTA`). Before, every pair conflicted. `SCOPE_NOORDER=1` turns order moves off.
  - `sq.sh` and `tq.sh` are job queues.
## Big main functions redrafted from scratch (main, round mainfresh1)

Two new matches: `func_02054bb4` (17 diffs before) in `SlidePanel.cpp`, and `func_020429a0` (never drafted before) in `TexObj_3.cpp`. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/mainfresh1/` (`d/`, `t/`).

- **`02054bb4`: one inlined copy that takes a reference.** The panel's edge width is read through `SlidePanel_GetEdge3(panel, res)`. That function uses `Model_GetWidth` for kinds 1 to 3, and `Model_GetWidthR` (which takes `ModelRes *&res`) only for kind 4. The function-scope `res` is passed by reference into exactly one inlined copy. This gives the target's register colouring. Every version that passed `res` by value, or by reference into every copy, stayed at 17.
- **`020429a0`: the tricks that matched it.**
  - **The `asm {}` identity matrix stops loop rotation.** The `TexObj_SetTransMtx` asm block (the same one as `Box_SetMtx` in `ObjPool.cpp`) turns off loop rotation for the whole function. All the loops become "branch to the test" loops and reload `count` on every pass, as in the target.
  - **A separate `int i` for each loop**, declared in the loop's own block, gives each index its own web (66 to 24).
  - **Bitfield reads are not shared between statements.** Each `FB(obj)->hflip`-style test loads the word again, which gives the target's repeated `ldr [sl, #0x6c]`. Write the flags as `if (FB(obj)->hflip ? TRUE : FALSE) hflip = TRUE; else hflip = FALSE;`.
  - **Read the OAM shape through `*(const u32 *)&oam[i]`.** With `*(u32 *)`, the load was shared with the bitfield store (11). The `const` read gives 3.
  - **The polygon attribute** is `G3_PolygonAttr(0, 0, 2, id, TexObj_GetAlpha(obj), TexObj_GetPolyMisc(obj))`. `misc` is built in its own inline (`u32 m = 0; if (..) m |= 0x4000; ...`). This gives the target's `mov r3, #0` / `orrne` registers.
  - **Stack slots follow declaration order.** A local declared later gets a lower address.
  - The new symbol `data_0208288c` was needed in `symbols.txt`.
- **No change:**
  - `0204b43c` stays at 17. FxMul reference temporaries and the `near`/`blk`/`scope`/`fscope` tools did not move it.
  - `02056230` stays at 42. The `sb`/`sl` swap did not move with fake reference variables.
  - In `02069b3c`, the target does not tail-merge the display-list stores, and a switch or early returns do not stop ours from doing it.
  - `02043100` is still `NONMATCHING`. A bitfield-struct draft (`d/3100/b.cpp`, 166 raw / 85 normalised, against 158 / 91 in the tree) gets closer on registers. The target loads `attr01` again for each bitfield and makes `~0xff` from `0xc000c000 asr 22`.
## Sweeping the pointer-local index trick over every near-miss (round opsweep1)

No new matches this round. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/opsweep1/`.

- **The rewriter.** `t/rw.py draft func module outdir` finds field reads in one function: `g.x`, `p->x`, `g.arr[2].x`, and the `LFLD`/`FLD`/`FIELD` macros. It routes them through block-local pointers indexed by the field offset, then scores every variant and greedily combines the ones that help. The variants are:
  - per statement: all reads, each base alone, and pairs of bases;
  - windows of 2–3 adjacent sibling statements that share a base, with reads only or with writes too;
  - one pointer per global or parameter at function scope;
  - each with and without `const`.

  The generated text is written so that it needs no type information:
  ```c
  const __typeof__(g.x) *_pp0 = (const __typeof__(g.x) *)&g;
  ... _pp0[((u32)&(*(__typeof__(g) *)0).x) / sizeof(__typeof__(g.x))] ...
  ```
  Fields of another type reuse the same pointer through a cast, `((const __typeof__(g.z) *)_pp0)[...]`. The cast is a no-op and does not lose the effect. On the old `ov011_022038ac` draft the rewriter rebuilds bigsweep2's fix by itself (6 → 0). The computed-offset form and the `__typeof__` form both score 0, the same as the hand-written `b[0] - a[0]`. A pointer to the first field read (`&g.x`, then `[0]` and `[2]`) also works. One pointer per field (`&g.z`, then `[0]`) does not.
- **Result.** The sweep covered 52 drafts with ≤ 15 differences: `ovhi22`, `ovmid2/best`, `bigsweep2`, `mainmid3`, and the TU1/TU2 drafts in `mainpool12`/`mainpool13`. It compiled about 11,000 variants, of which about 1,900 failed to compile, mostly on bit-fields. Nothing reached a match.
  - Almost every variant left the score unchanged.
  - The only reduction (`ov010_021ffd0c`, 13 → 12) is a scoring artefact: the pointer is hoisted to the top of the function.
  - The remaining near-misses are register choice or store/pool-load scheduling, not the evaluation order of two reads.
  - The trick is specific to the operands of one binary expression (`a.x - b.x`) on globals.
- **What was checked by hand:**
  - `ov003_021d883c` (2). The target copies the default vector as `ldr x; ldr y; str x; str y; ldr z; str z`. This is the only such sequence in the ROM (`t/pat_ll.py` scans every ARM function).
    - An 8-byte struct copy through a pointer, `*(Ov003XY *)&v = *(const Ov003XY *)&p[2];` with `const s32 *p = (const s32 *)&g`, gives the paired loads and stores with the right registers. With that copy, `z` read through `p` is hoisted above the stores (2). Read as `g.v.z` it stays below them, but then the base register is `r2` instead of `r0` (6).
    - An `s64` copy loads the high word first.
    - 25 minutes of permuter on the `g.v.z` form found nothing.
  - `ov000_0219d9fc` (3). The `0x9ac < 0x9ae` load order did not respond to pointers from `self` or `self + 0x900`, separate pointers, one-sided casts, `+ 0` or inlines with parameters. A `BOOL` inline materialises the compare.
  - `0205e5c4` and `0205c234` (TU2) are store and pool-reload scheduling. Pointer-index stores and moving the read to each position gave no fix.
  - `ov006_021e1bb4` is a register swap on `w - anim44` / `h - anim48` inside the inline. Index forms, swapped order and combined declarations did not change it.
- **Main functions owned by mainmid4:** no fix found for `0206e858`, `0203b12c`, `0206e3a0`, `0203d298`, `0203a984`, `02049690` or `0206f6d4`.

## Clean redrafts of the menu builders, and two-definition constants that fix a whole-function rotation (ov000, round bigfresh1)

No new matches. This round redrafted far-off big functions from the target's statement order, using the matched `func_ov000_0217fd70` (`Lo_0217fd70.cpp`) and `func_ov000_021790ac` (`Lo_02178fd8.cpp`) as templates. Drafts are in `scratchpad/bigfresh1/best/`.

- **`func_ov000_021782e0` and `func_ov000_02176714` (menu builders) are exact after register normalisation.** The old drafts (85 and 50 raw) had stores moved into the `{ u8 *s = ...; }` blocks to push registers around, which broke the instruction order (18 and 8 with registers masked). Writing every statement in the target's order, in the `Lo_0217fd70` style, gives 0 (`82e0_clean_146_sdr0.cpp`) and 2 (`6714_clean_68_sdr2.cpp`; the last 2 are a `mov #0x80` the scheduler places by register). What is left is one rotation of the long-lived values: the target has `g` (`data_02149ef8`) `r4`, `self` `r5`, the hoisted `1` `r6`, `0` `r7` and the base vtable `r8`; the clean draft has `self r4, 1 r5, 0 r6, vt r7, g r8`.
- **This rotation does not respond to naming.** Declaration order, an initialised `g`, a `self` copy, function-scope `s` instead of the block-local ones, inline constructors returning the new object, block-local `new` temporaries, position/target-setter inlines, bit-field forms of the `|=` flags, unused extra parameters and an inline wrapper around the whole body or its tail all compiled to the same registers (or broke the structure). Truncating the function shows the order flips exactly where the hoisted `1` becomes live across the rest of the function, so it follows the interference graph, not the names.
- **Two-definition constants on the first block move it.** `tdc.py` found that a two-definition zero used for the *first* zero store (`0x58` of the first window in `021782e0`, `0x5c` in `02176714`) puts the hoisted zero in the target's register (146 to 121, 68 to 43). In `021782e0` a second pass found a two-definition `1` for the first `0x2c` store (121 to 112), and the permuter then merged the two variables into one: a single `s32 k` defined `k = 0; if ((u32)self & 3) k = 0;` before the first window's zero store and `k = 1; if (...) k = 1;` before its `0x2c` store gives `self` `r5` and `0` `r7` (48 raw, 0 masked, `82e0_48_sdr0.cpp`). `g`, the `1` and the vtable are still rotated (`r8`/`r4`/`r6` against `r4`/`r6`/`r8`). Two-definition forms of `g` or of the vtable pointer, the merged `k` used for later `1` stores, and `tdc`/`tdv2` passes from there made it worse or changed nothing. The same merge does not apply to `02176714`, where the `1` store comes before the zero store; there `1` and `-0x1ccd` stay swapped (43).
- **Permuter:** 40 minutes on the clean `021782e0` draft found only the `o = self` copy noise (735 to 650); 40 minutes from the two-variable draft found the merged `k` (545 to 220, i.e. 112 to 48 raw); 40 more minutes from `82e0_48_sdr0.cpp` and 80 minutes on `6714_43_sdr2.cpp` found nothing.
- **`func_ov010_02201064` (76): the beam setters read the active byte again.** In the second loop the target reloads `b[0]` for the outer test of `SetA`/`SetB` but reuses the getter's load inside `Idle`. Writing the setters' outer test as `*b != 0` (the getter and `Idle` keep `FLD(u8, b, 0)`) gives that reload and removes the `0xffff` pool word (11 to 6 masked), but the loop registers move (190 raw, `1064_deref_190_sdr6.c`). The other masked diff is the zero for the `last` stores: the target builds a fresh `mov r1, #0` and keeps it for the `0x7a` store and both `func_ov000_02198360` zeros; ours stores the hoisted `r5` zero. `u16`/`FALSE` zeros, a chained store, a pointer copy and statements moved before the call did not change it.
- **Tools** (in `scratchpad/bigfresh1/t/`): `vv.py base func mod varfile` runs `(name, [(old, new)])` variants with raw and masked scores; `trunc.py src func a b` compiles the function cut after every `b`-th top-level statement and prints the prologue registers, to find where a register order flips; `tdall.py` replaces every use of one literal with a single two-definition variable; `td2j.py` is a joint two-literal `tdc`; `tdc.py` takes `TDWRITE=<variant>` to write one variant from a log; `split.py` moves the tail of a function into an inline at every statement.

## Big main functions, round 2: type-keyed CSE and a hoisted NULL (main, round mainfresh2)

There are no new matches this round. The drafts are in the scratchpad under `mainfresh2/d/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

- **Header fix:** In `GameScene.h`, `unk_vfb0` now takes `(int a, int b)` and `func_02043d84` returns `s32`. With this change, the old `02056e74` draft compiles again.
- **`020417e0` (TexObj init, new draft, now 24 / 2):** It would append to `TexObj.cpp`, ending at `0x02041b10`. Three findings:
  - **CSE is keyed by the access type.** Read the loop bound through a second struct view, `((const TexObjResB *)res)->bank->numSeq`, and pass `res->dict` as the argument. The target then reloads `[res,#0xc]` on every iteration. Through a single type the two loads are CSE'd.
  - **A `const` source pointer lets loads move above stores.** With a `const TexObjRes *res` parameter, the scheduler can hoist the `ldr [res,#0xc]` above the `obj->res` and `obj->entries` stores, as in the target.
  - **An address-taken counter changes allocation.** Pass the loop counter to an inline as `const u16 &idx` (the permuter found the same effect with `*&i`). This took the score from 87 to 24.
  - **Remaining diff:** The `NULL` stored in loop 2 is hoisted to the zero at function entry (`mov fp, r4`). The target copies it from loop 2's `i = 0` (`mov fp, sl`). Small test files show that any earlier loop causes this hoist, and an inner loop blocks it. No source form tried has fixed it.
- **`0204205c` (OAM cell copy, was 20, now 8 / 0):** The copy is written elementwise in the order `attr1, attr0, attr2`. Only an `r2`/`r3` swap of the attr0/attr1 loads remains. It would append to `TexObj_2.cpp`, ending at `0x02042628`.
- **No change:**
  - `02056e74` stays at 4 / 0. Only the colouring around `GameScene_FxMul(s, -0x80000)` differs.
  - `0204b43c` stays at 17 / 0, `02056230` at 42 / 0 (an `sb`/`sl` swap), and `02068e68` at 41 / 2. In `02068e68`, the target reloads `FX_SinCosTable_` for the x rotation only.
  - In `02069b3c` (66 / 21), an empty `asm {}` does not stop the tail merge.
- **Permuter:** Its C front end needs C structs instead of classes, `void *vtbl` casts for virtual calls, and `sizeof(TexObjEntry)` replaced with `0x28` in `base.c`.
## Overlay mid-size functions redrafted structure-first (round ovfresh1)

Four functions of 0x100 to 0x500 bytes matched after their old drafts sat at 26 to 62 diffs. Each fix was structural, not a register shuffle.

- **One member through a whole-struct local** (`ov003_021d6bac`). The target keeps a 16-byte frame with the sub-struct at `sp+8`. Write `Ov003Stat s; s.sub = p->sub;`, scale through `&s.sub.atk`, then copy back with `p->sub = s.sub;`. A local of just the sub-struct type gives the wrong offset. Copy the scalar fields 0 to 7 into separate scalar locals.
- **A block-local copy for subtraction operands** (`ov003_021daae4`). `{ Ov003Vec base = o->base; vp.x -= base.x; ... }` reproduces the target's three loads ahead of the subtracts. A block-local `Ov003VecP vp = data_...;` is hoisted out of the loop by MWCC, which matches.
- **A "dead" getter result is really an argument** (`ov000_021bb8dc`). The target keeps the getter's `r0` and passes it to three calls. Declare the getter as returning `void *` and pass the value through.
- **Duplicate the tail instead of `goto`** (`ov000_021bb8dc`). One case reached a shared statement through a `goto`. Write the statement plus `break` in both places and MWCC tail-merges it the target's way.
- **A `BOOL ok = TRUE; if (!f()) ok = FALSE; return ok;` inline** materialises the flag exactly as the target does.
- **`#pragma opt_common_subs off` for a global reloaded after every block** (`ov006_021d16a0`). Wrap only the function, then `#pragma opt_common_subs reset`. Make the min-search inline's index and result `u8`. This replaced a file-level `optimization_level 2` hack.
- **Near-misses, no match found:**
  - Reading bitfields into locals before the stores shares one load (`ov002_021dd9dc`: 57 raw to 28 raw, 0 masked). Only a register swap is left.
  - Inside an if/else, an early `return` in one arm keeps a predicated `strge`/`strlt` pair (`ov000_021c3158`: 38 to 4, 0 masked). Only an r4/r8 swap is left.
  - `ov011_021fb9b8` went from 27 to 16. `ov002_021d1f70` went from 56 to 39 after a 360-way statement-order sweep. `ov012_021fefc8` is still at 23 raw, with 2 masked diffs left (registers only).
  - Turning `opt_common_subs` off did not help any of these.

## Declarations between statements, and a register-only sweep (round gridsweep1)

`func_ov006_021e1bb4` matched this round, as `src/ov006/ov006_021e1bb4.cpp`. Drafts, logs and tools are in `scratchpad/gridsweep1/` (`d/<mod>_<addr>/`, `q*.log`, `t/`).

- **The match was one declaration moved between two statements.** The last 3 diffs (`ip`/`sl` swapped for `e->unk4` and `anim->unk48`) were inside the `ov006_Caption_Draw` inline. Moving `s32 h = e->unk4;` down from the declaration list to just after `w -= *(int *)anim->unk44;` (a C++ declaration in the middle of the block) matches. Earlier rounds tried all 720 orders of the declaration list, `scope.py` order moves, `hcm.py` and `combo2.py`, but none of them placed a declaration between statements. In C++ drafts, also try each declaration (with its initialiser) at every statement position before its first use, inside inlines too.
- **The sibling `func_ov006_021e0b80` did not follow.** It is the same caption code as a function. The 1bb4 body written as a function gives 32 diffs, while the old `ox`/`oy` draft gives 8. A grid over both drafts did not get below 8.
- **`func_0204f524` (TU1 blocker), 8 → 6.** The `scratchpad/mainpool12/d/f524_8_zcopy.c` form needs `u8 *c;` declared before `u8 *a;` (`d/0204f524/zl2.c`). The constants then take the target's `a`/`c` order. Only `z` and `c` are still swapped: `z` gets `r7`, where the target has `r4`. All 480 combinations of declaration order, `n` type (`long`, `u16`) and `z` type (`u16`, `u8`) give 6 or worse. So do block-scoped `i`, `z` or `c`, a `z = n` inside the `if`, and other types for `n`, `i` and `z`.
- **No other function moved.** These were swept:
  - Every draft in the round's score directories was rescored, raw and register-normalised (`scores.txt`). This gave 45 functions with 4 or fewer diffs after normalisation, plus about 15 with 5 to 10.
  - Each got a single-move sweep over every local, the function's own and each inline's: every declaration slot of every enclosing block, and the last 8 statement positions before the first use (`GRID_ONLY1=1 GRID_NS=8`).
  - The 0-diff ones also got the full pair grid: every pair of locals at every pair of positions, then `int`/`long`/`u32`/`s32` type pairs.
  - About 30,000 compiles in all. In most of these functions, nearly every move compiles to the same code. Examples: all 646 single moves in `021c4460`, all 124 moves of `h`/`d` in `0206d43c`, and 765 of 850 moves in `021be5dc`.
- **Hand work that did not match:**
  - `0206d43c` (18 → 12 raw, still 0 after normalisation). The permuter found it: compute the `tmpSize` argument into an `int` temporary before `s->baseOffset = 0` (`d/d43c/p75.cpp`). The same change does nothing in `0206d99c`. These also gave 18: the inline written as `(x + m) & ~m`, an extra copy, a `SysCard *` local, an `int` parameter, `SaveBase_BackupOffset`/`DataOffset`, and every operand order of the `h` argument.
  - `021e51f8`: the target keeps the constant `1` in `r1`. Loading `data_02097e40` into a local before the store gives `r1`, but then the global is not loaded again. These stay at 2: the full `func_ov006_021e4bec` body as the inline, a hoisted `menu` or `v`, and every parameter type.
  - `02049690`: the `seq`/`vol` registers inside `SndMgr_StartBgm` stay swapped with swapped parameters, caller locals in either order, a volume local, and a ternary.
  - `02189b44`: the `vx`/`vz` block stays at 5 with swapped orders, pointer indexing, cached `pos` fields, and no locals.
  - `021be5dc`: an hour of the permuter found nothing.
- **Tools** (in `scratchpad/gridsweep1/t/`):
  - `grid.py src func mod`. Stage 1 tries single moves of every local. Stage 2 is a pair grid, with locals whose single moves changed the code tried first. Stage 3 tries type pairs. Environment variables: `GRID_IN=<inline>`, `GRID_NS`, `GRID_MAX`, `GRID_ONLY1`, `GRID_NOTYPE`.
  - `sc.py` gives the raw and normalised score from one compile, plus a hash of the code.
  - `gather.py` and `scoreall.py` score every draft in a set of directories.
  - `inls.py` lists the inlines (with locals) that a function reaches.
  - `gq.sh` is a job queue.
  - `prun.sh` runs the permuter on main or overlay functions.

## Register colouring on the mainfresh2 near-misses (main, round mainreg1)

`func_0204b43c` (camera update, 17 raw / 0 masked before) matched and is appended to `src/Main/Gfx/Camera.cpp`. Drafts and tools are in the scratchpad under `mainreg1/` (`v/<addr>/`, `t/`).

- **Read a field into a local before the statement that would load it first.** In both arms of the `cam->mode` switch the target loads `cam->at.y` (or `cam->eye.y`) into the register after the hi/sum pair of the first `Cam_FxMul`, and our draft put it before them. Writing `s32 y = cam->at.y;` as the first statement of the arm, then the three stores in the old order, rotates the three registers into place (17 to 5). The loads still come out in the same scheduled order; only the colouring changes. When three values in a straight run are rotated, try reading the odd one into a local one or two statements earlier.
- **A second definition to split a result from its increment.** The last 5 diffs were the square root: the target loads `REG_SQRT_RESULT` into `r0`, computes `+ 1` into `r1` and loads `a.y` into `r2`; ours kept the result, the `+ 1` and the argument in one register. The form that matches splits the sqrt inline into a start-and-wait inline and writes the rest out:
  ```c
  Cam_SqrtStart(sq);
  by = b.y;
  root = (s32)REG_SQRT_RESULT;
  ay = a.y;
  q = root + 1;
  if (root & 0x40000000) {
      q = root + 1;
  }
  ang = FX_Atan2(by - ay, q >> 1);
  ```
  Without the second definition of `q` (or one of `by`/`ay` in the same place) it stays at 5. `(r + 1) / 2` also gives the target's registers, but adds the sign fix-up. A search over the statement orders, declaration orders and two-definition targets of these four locals (`v/b43c/v10.py`, 1152 variants) found it early (the run was stopped there): statements `by, root, ay, q`, declared `root, q, ay, by`.
- **`02068e68` (41 / 2 to 26 / 1, `d/8e68_26_1.cpp`): a two-definition table pointer gives the x rotation its own table load.** The target loads `FX_SinCosTable_` into a callee-saved register for the y and z rotations but loads it again for the x rotation. `if ((u32)p & 3) { tbl = FX_SinCosTable_; }` as the first statement of the `else` arm (before `u16 rzz = p->rot.z;`) stops the x rotation's `FX_SinCosTable_[...]` from reusing `tbl`, and gives the reload (37 / 1). Declaring `tbl` at function scope (found by `fscope.py`) fixes `alpha` and the `rotated` flag (26 / 1). Two diffs are left. `type` and the hoisted `1` are swapped (`r5`/`r4` against `r4`/`r5`). The target sets `rotated = TRUE` in the x arm with `mov r6, r5` (a copy of the hoisted `1`), and ours uses `mov r6, #1`. None of these changed it: types and positions of `rotated`, `rotated++`/`|= 1`/`= rx != 0`, a `BOOL` rotate-if inline, a local copy of `type`, the order of the three angle reads, `tdc`/`tdv2`/`near`/`pre`, the declaration grid and the permuter.
- **No change:** `02056e74` (4 / 0), `0204205c` (8 / 0), `020417e0` (24 / 2) and `02056230` (42 / 0). `tsw` and `pre` ran on all four. `tdc` ran on `6e74`, `205c` and `6230`, and `tdv2` on `6e74`, `205c` and `6230`. The declaration grid ran on `6e74`. For `020417e0`, second definitions of the first loop's zero, of its index, or of a `NULL` local all hoist the `NULL` to entry, and other loop forms or `NULL` spellings change nothing. `6e74` also had 64-bit temporaries in every declaration order with second definitions. `205c` had temporaries for the three attributes in every load, store and declaration order, plus inline copy helpers. `6230` had `i = 0` and `first` moved to every earlier statement. `trunc2.py` shows that the `6230` swap flips inside the big panel block (the `if` over `first` and the draw loop). The target colours `s` before the hoisted zero, and our build colours the zero first.
- **Tools.** `t/grid.py src func mod` tries every declaration order reachable by moving one or two declarations (`GRID_ONLY=a,b` limits the moved names, `GRID_ALL=1` tries every permutation). `t/tsw.py` tries other integer types for every declaration. `t/pre.py` reads each field expression of a statement into a block-local `__typeof__` temporary one to three statements earlier (`PRE_TD=1` adds a second definition). `t/trunc2.py src func regex` cuts the function after every top-level statement and prints the first matching instruction, to see where a register choice flips. `t/prun2.sh dir secs` runs the permuter on an existing directory (it sets `PERM_FUNC`, which `fakeobjdump.py` needs).
## One-element arrays as a register-colouring lever (overlays, round ovfresh2)

This round found no new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovfresh2/d/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

- **One-element and paired arrays change MWCC's colouring with no extra code.** Declaring a local as `T x[1]`, or two same-type locals as one `T xy[2]`, keeps the generated instructions the same but changes which registers they get. Results:
  - `ov000_0217ea30`: putting the second getter result into `void *ab[2]` fixed a whole b/o swap (33 to 3 / 2).
  - `ov002_021d1f70`: `z` as `z[1]` went from 39 to 28 / 0.
  - `ov002_021dfcc0`: `map` as `map[1]` went from 35 to 29 / 0.
  - `ov000_0214dd3c`: `k` as `k[1]` inside the `Key0214dd3c` inline went from 55 to 49 / 0.
- **`ov011_021fb9b8` (16 to 12 / 0):** In the lerp inline, store the end and start values, then compute the step by re-reading them: `(&e->unk_0c)[2] = ((&e->unk_0c)[1] - (&e->unk_0c)[0]) / frames;`. Keep the two-definition `to`. A head/owner register swap is left.
- **`ov000_021688c4` (35 to 20 / 0):** Move `unk_21 = 0` next to `unk_20 = 0`. Compute `a5 = alpha & 0x1f` after the first position call. Only the allocation of `a5` is left.
- **`ov002_021dfcc0` (38 to 29 / 0):** Assign the manager global after `unit`. Fetch the getter result in a block before reading `self+4`. Load the 0x44 and 0x48 fields into locals before the camera call.
- **`ov002_021e7ea8` (42 to 34 / 0):** Write the fixed-point multiply as `FxM(FX_Div(...), t)`, with the quotient as the first operand.
- **Remaining blocker, `ov000_0217ea30` (3 / 2):** The `-0x1666` pool constant is hoisted into `r5`, but the target loads it into `r1` at the `strh`. Without the two-definition constant the whole function rotates (70).
- **No change:** `021c3158` (4 / 0), `021dd9dc` (28 / 0), `021fefc8` (23 / 2), `021fee10` (44 / 22).
- **New scratch tools:**
  - `pairarr.py` tries every local as `[1]`, every same-type pair as `[2]`, and every parameter copied into `[1]`. Set `PA_IN=<inline>` to work inside an inline.
  - `pa_iter.sh` runs `pairarr.py` greedily.
  - `kqpos.py` moves a two-definition constant block to every statement position.
- **Permuter:** Its C front end rejects C++ inheritance (`struct A : public B`), and struct names used without `struct`.
## Unused parameters as locals fix a whole-function register rotation (ov000, round bigfresh2)

One new match: `func_ov000_021782e0` (0x77c bytes, menu builder), now `src/ov000/Lo_021782e0.cpp`. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/bigfresh2/` (`best/`, `t/`, `m/` for the small experiments).

- **The fix.** Start from the clean redraft (`82e0_clean_146_sdr0.cpp`: 146 raw, 0 masked, no two-definition tricks). Declare `g` (`data_02149ef8`) and the loop's `m` as parameters instead of locals: `void func_ov000_021782e0(VObj *self, void *g, VObj *m)`. The function still assigns both before use. `g` alone gives the target's whole prologue (`g` r4, `self` r5, `1` r6, `0` r7, vtable r8) and 18 raw diffs. What is left is an `id`/`m` swap in the loop, and `m` as a parameter fixes it (0). Turning any other local into a parameter changes nothing or makes it worse. Adding an unused parameter without moving a local into it also changes nothing.
- **Why it works.** These conclusions come from small test functions in `m/`. When the callee-saved registers are tight, MWCC assigns registers in this order:
  1. parameters, the last parameter first (it gets the lowest register);
  2. the hoisted constants, in reverse order of first use;
  3. long-lived locals, in declaration order.

  With few live values the order is simply reverse creation order. A local therefore never gets a lower register than a hoisted constant, whatever its name, initialiser or declaration position. That is why bigfresh1 could not move `g` out of r8. When the target puts a local-looking value (a loaded global) in r4, below the hoisted `1`/`0`, try making it a parameter.
- **Declaration order still matters between locals.** A long-lived local declared later is assigned later. On the 48-raw draft, a vtable-pointer local declared after `g` (with `g` declared first) moved the vtable to r8 (48 to 37). The parameter form made this unnecessary.
- **`func_ov000_02176714` (43 to 29 raw, 2 masked).** Making the loop's `tbl` a parameter fixes the `tbl`/`a` swap in the loop (`best/6714_29_sdr2_tblparam.cpp`). What is left is one r4/r6 swap across the whole function. The hoisted `1` is assigned before `-0x1ccd` and the loop's `base`; the target assigns `-0x1ccd`, `0`, `1` (reverse first use). A two-definition `0x130` in the loop gives exactly the target prologue (`-0x1ccd` r4, `0` r5, `1` r6), but only because it takes `1` out of the loop, and the frame shrinks (56). So the weight of `1`'s uses in the loop is what moves it ahead. These made no difference:
  - parameter, local, single-definition and two-definition forms of `-0x1ccd` and `1` (at one use and at all uses);
  - every pair and triple of locals as parameters;
  - a separate parameter for the object in each switch case;
  - a declaration grid over the loop block;
  - 15 minutes of two-definition annealing (`t/tdan.py`).
- **`func_ov010_02201064` (190 to 176 raw, 6 masked).** Making the block-local `all` a parameter gives the 14. The fresh `mov r1, #0` for the `last` stores is still missing. A two-definition zero after `func_02040528(2)` (`s32`/`int`/`u16`/`u8`, used for the three stores and/or the `0x7a` store and the call) merges with the hoisted r5 zero. The other attempts (inline helpers with a zero parameter, a parameter zero) changed nothing or made it worse.
- **Tools** (in `scratchpad/bigfresh2/t/`):
  - `pparam2.py src func outdir K [force]` writes every subset of up to K locals turned into parameters. It also handles initialised declarations.
  - `bo.sh func mod files...` gives the raw score and our prologue for many files in parallel.
  - `fam.py log` groups a `tdc` log by the registers of `g`/`self`/`1`/`0`/vtable.
  - `tdc.py` now prints our prologue after `##` and has these options:
    - `TDRX=<regex>` for non-numeric operands such as `g` or a vtable symbol;
    - `TDPARAM=1` makes the two-definition variable a parameter;
    - `TDSINGLE=1` gives it a single definition.
  - `td2j.py` specs take `LIT:N:NEAR:TYPE:SKIP`.
  - `tdan.py` anneals over sets of two-definition replacements.
  - `near2.py` and `tmpify.py` move statements and wrap a statement's `self` field base in a pointer local, both printing our prologue.
  - `m/m.sh` prints our code for a small test file that defines the target function's name.

## Inline-local stack order and mid-block initialised declarations (main, round mainfresh3)

- **`0206bab8` matched (310 / 163 to 0, `Effect_16.cpp`).** The particle-emit function had a wrong stack layout and the wrong order of its zero stores. These changes fixed it:
  - **Locals of an inlined function are placed after all of the caller's locals, so they get lower addresses.** The target's rotation matrices sit below the caller's vectors. Moving the rotation code into inlines that each own their `EffectMtx43 tmp` (`Effect_MtxRotXZ` and `Effect_MtxRotYXZ`) gave the target's frame. Inside `Effect_MtxRotYXZ` the order of the declarations (`tmp, ry, rx, rz, rotated`) also matters.
  - **A C++ declaration with an aggregate initialiser between statements** (`EffectVec c = {0,0,0};` after `p->origin = em->pos;`) emits its zero stores at that point. At function scope the stores move to the function entry.
  - **An inline that takes `const s32 &base`** (`Effect_RandScale`) loads `base` after the `rand` call in its body, as the target does. Taking `base` by value loads it before the call.
  - Some forms had to match the target exactly:
    - Use the existing `EffectPtcl_SetCmds` inline, not open-coded stores (it gives load-load-store-store).
    - Write `v.x`, `v.y = 0`, `v.z` in that order, with no radius local.
    - Write `out.x = c.x + rand(...)` (forward the zero vector).
    - The angle comes from `func_02040498`, not `func_02040528`.
  - The remaining `bl`/`blx` differences on the Thumb `MTX_*` calls are interworking, as in the already-matched `0206b81c`.
- **`02069b3c` (66 / 21 to 44 / 4, scratch `d/9b3c_44.cpp`).** The target only partly tail-merges the arms. Writing `p->unk_50 = idx;` in each arm, with a block-local `s32 i` in the second arm, merges only the `0x50` store, as the target does. Writing the palette address as `((u16)p->tex->plttVramKey + pltt[0]) << 3` loads the key before `pltt[0]`. One register swap is left: the target keeps param + key in `r3`, the register `data[0]` was loaded into, and ours uses `r1`.
- **`02053254` (95 / 60 to 79 / 20, `d/3254_79.cpp`; the permuter's version `d/3254_p84.c` is at 84 / 18).** Changes that helped:
  - In the else arm, write the direction flag as nested ifs: `if (mode == 0x1000) { if (flip == 1) dir = -1; } else { if (flip == 0) dir = -1; }`.
  - Statement order in the else arm is `sy`, `sx = FxMul(...) & ~0xfff`, `sz`.
  - The permuter read `scene->unk_b8_0` into a local before `if (b == 0)`.
  
  The remaining problem: our build hoists the `~0xfff` mask to the function entry, and the target rebuilds it in each block.
- **No change on the register-only near-misses:** `02068e68`, `02056e74`, `0204205c`, `020417e0` and `02056230`. Tried with the declaration grid, the between-statements grid, `tdv2`, typed constants, index and pointer forms, and split loop indices.
## Sweeping locals-as-parameters over the register-only near misses (round bigfresh3)

Four new matches, all found by turning one or two locals into extra parameters that the function assigns before use (`scratchpad/bigfresh3/t/pp3.py`, then a plain raw-score sort):

| Function | Before (raw/masked) | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov011_021fb9b8` | 12/0 | `owner` (the list's actor) as a parameter. Appended to `src/ov011/ov011_021fb66c.cpp`. |
| `func_ov002_021dd9dc` | 28/0 | `w` and `obj` as parameters, together. Either one alone makes it worse (`w` 50, `obj` 66). New file `src/ov002/ov002_021dd9dc.cpp` (keeps the draft's file-wide `#pragma opt_loop_invariants off`). |
| `func_02051a34` | 35/0 | `fn` (the font's split-char function pointer) as a parameter. New file `src/Main/Gfx/SlideWin_2.cpp`. |
| `func_02056230` | 42/0 | The panel loop index `i` as a parameter. New file `src/Main/Sys/SlideDrawScene.cpp`, plus the symbol `data_020829c8` (a `u16[3]` colour table that the function copies), which was missing from `symbols.txt`. |

- **When the lever fires.** In all four, the swap was between a long-lived local and another value: a hoisted constant, an inline's local or a second long-lived local. In each case one of the two locals needed a lower register than its declaration order gave it. Making it a parameter moved it ahead. In `021fb9b8` this swaps `head`/`owner` (r8/sb). None of the four needed a second change.
- **It is not a rule that parameters get the lowest registers.** In `func_ov002_021d1f70`, making `x` a parameter moved it from r4 up to sb. In `func_ov000_021688c4`, the extra `a5` parameter went to r4 and shifted everything else up. So treat the lever as a search dimension: try every local and every pair, and also both parameter orders for a pair (`PERM=1`).
- **Landing.** When a header or the same file declares the function with the old signature, C++ `extern "C"` refuses the overload. For scoring, `pp3.py` (`HDR=1`) wraps the old declarations in `#define f f_hdr` / `#undef f`. For landing, put the function in its own translation unit, which is what all four did. Callers in other files keep their old prototype. The extra parameter is never read before it is written, so the call site does not change.
- **No effect** (every single local and every pair as parameters, 0 gain):
  - `ov000_021c3158` (4), `021688c4` (20), `0214dd3c` (49), `021dfcc0` (29, both orders), `021d1f70` (28);
  - `func_02056e74` (4; the diff is the scratch register of a 64-bit multiply, and seven multiply spellings change nothing);
  - `func_0204205c` (8; scratch registers again), `func_02068e68` (26), `func_020417e0` (24).
  
  These are not callee-saved rotations, or not ones a parameter reaches. In `021c3158`, sharing `res` between the two blocks, a `name`/`res` reuse, and inline-parameter forms of the model constructor also changed nothing.
- **Partial:** `func_ov002_021e7ea8` 34 to 28 with `gx` and `ret` as parameters (`bigfresh3/d/e7ea8/gxret.cpp`).
- **Tools** (`scratchpad/bigfresh3/t/`):
  - `pp3.py src func outdir K` works for any return type. It renames block-scoped duplicate locals (`a_0`, `a_1`) so that each can be chosen separately, and takes `HDR=1` and `PERM=1` (see above).
  - `ppd.sh` / `ppdx.sh` drive it and score with `sc.sh` (raw score, parallel).
  - `scan.sh func mod` scores the newest scratchpad drafts of a function to find the best one.

## One-element array sweep over every near-miss (all modules, round arrsweep1)

This round ran `pairarr.py`/`pa_iter.sh` on the best draft of every unmatched function whose register-masked score is 4 or less: 55 functions, from 1 to 146 raw diffs. It then ran `pairarr.py` with `PA_IN` inside each `static inline` the function uses (39 functions, about 140 inlines), and ran it again on a second draft for 8 functions. No function went into the build. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `arrsweep1/` (`w/`, `w3/` and `wi/<func>/<inline>/`, plus `x/` for hand work).

- **The lever helps on big rotations, not on 1 or 2 stray registers.** Every function at 2 to 6 raw diffs stayed where it was, inline arrays included. The hits were all in functions where a whole callee-saved rotation was wrong:
  - `func_ov006_021cec68` went from 38 to 12 with `void *res[1]` (the message resource), then to 1 with `u8 n[1]`. That draft still has the old uninitialised `kq` trick (`g + kq`, where `kq` is set only on the early-return path). It is not valid code, and it leaves `add r0, r4, r6` against `#0x8b0`. Without the trick the draft is at 7 / 0 (`arrsweep1/x/cec/plain.cpp`). Only a g/res swap between `r4` and `r5` is left. Pairs, mixed `u8 *gr[2]`, an inline and block moves did not fix it. The function is still blocked by the ov006 string pool.
  - `func_ov000_02176714` went from 43 to 29 / 2 with `tbl[1]` (`w/func_ov000_02176714/base.cpp.it1.cpp`). The clean draft went from 68 to 56 / 2 (`w3/.../base.cpp.it1.cpp`).
  - `func_0206e858` went from 5 / 0 to 0 / 0 with `LangState *st[1]` (`w3/func_0206e858/base.cpp.it1.cpp`).
- **A 0 from `sd.py`/`sc.py` is not a match.** These scripts mask `[pc, #...]`, so they hide an extra literal-pool word. Check every 0 with `tools/match.py`. `func_0206e858` at 0 still differs in one pool offset. The target loads one pool word (`data_0214b105`) for both `func_0206e900` calls. The draft names `data_0214b104.name` once and `data_0214b105` once, which gives two pool words. The object is 4 bytes too big, and every module then failed the ROM check. If both calls name the same address, in any spelling, MWCC keeps the address in `r5` (11 diffs). These forms also failed: a two-iteration loop, a helper inline, `#pragma opt_common_subs off`, and `u32` casts of the address.
- **No change from the lever (best draft, raw / masked):** `021e51f8` (2 / 0; the `mov #1` for `Ov006_Begin` is in `r0`, but the target has `r1`), `0206e3a0`, `0217a1f8`, `0218a5ec`, `021e2690`, `021ec010`, `02211b2c`, `021be5dc` (3 / 0; an FX temporary in `CalcDepth`), `0219d9fc`, `021d883c`, `021e8ef8`, `02189b44`, `021fb02c`, `0220640c`, `021fc688`, `0203d298`, `021c4460`, `02199180`, `021708ec`, `02171478`, `021fc0b8`, `0206f6d4`, `021e1204`, `02056e74`, `020508f4`, `0204205c`, `0203a984`, `0206d43c`, `02060a08`, `021d3e14`, `0219e998`, `021fb1b4`, `02051a34`, `0206d99c`, `02056230`, `021782e0`, `0204f5bc`, `02068e68`, `0204f524`, `0205c234`, `0205e5c4`, `021fff38`, `0205e29c`, `021d36fc`, `021afaf8`, `021e6274`, `021fce2c`, `021cf420`, `02064b54`, `021d4118` and `0203c2bc`. `021ffb90` went from 8 to 7 / 4 with the `q` parameter of `Ov015_Ang` copied into an array. `02060b18` went from 16 / 0 to 15 / 4, which is a worse result.
- **Tools** (scratchpad `arrsweep1/t/`):
  - `pairarr.py` now scores each variant with one compile (`sc.py`).
  - `pa_iter.sh` keeps a `.c` draft as `.c`.
  - `drive.sh list` runs `pa_iter.sh` over a list of `path func module` lines.
  - `drive_inl.sh list` runs `pairarr.py` inside every inline the function uses. `inls.py file func` lists those inlines.
  - `gather2.py` finds drafts and picks the function from the file name or the parent directory name.
## Clean redrafts of small near-misses, and a dead store that orders hoisted constants (round nearfresh1)

- **New match: `func_ov002_021e2690`** (2 raw diffs before).
  - The redraft uses the sibling `Caption` struct layout (see `ov002_021e0c28.cpp`), with `tx`/`ty` block locals and the stores in the target's order (`mode`, then `timer`).
  - The last 2 diffs were the registers of two constants hoisted out of the loop (8 and 3).
  - MWCC gives loop-hoisted constants registers in the order of their first use in IR. A dead store that comes first, `c->timer = 8; c->mode = 3; c->timer = 8;`, gives 8 the earlier register. The target's store order stays the same, because the dead store is removed.
- **A new local can replace the comma hack.** `lx = x + 0x4c` stops the `(x+0x4c)-0x80 → x-0x34` fold just as `x = (0, x + 0x4c)` does (`ov000_0217e560`).
  - In that function the fold is not the last problem: the remaining diffs move between the 0x84/0x8c/0x94 groups and the 0x98 group.
- **`ov011_021fff38`: the data view sets the alias behaviour.** A cast view `((s32 *)&struct)` keeps loads after stack stores; an `extern s32[]` view lets them be hoisted.
  - With the cast view and `y = D58[9]; t.x = D58[8]; t.y = y - 0x2000;`, the top of the function matches.
  - The middle still has the `d[1]` load hoisted above the copy of `p` (best: 6 raw / 2 normalised).
- **`ov015_021ffb90`:** pointer forms of `Ov015Vec` get the right load order, but they bring in an `add r0,r2,#0x114` / `[r0,#0xc08]` artifact (best: 7/4).
- **`ov009_02208b24`:** the draft in src has a semantic error. The target is `cmp; bgt` with pool constant 0x19a, so the condition is `<= 0x19a`, not `< 0x19b`. Fixing only that makes the score worse (21/7 → 30/13), so the draft needs to be redrafted further.
- **Did not move from their base score:**
  - `ov000_0217a1f8` (2): the target's `mvn r2,#0` appears nowhere else in the ROM.
  - `ov000_0218a5ec` (2), `ov015_02211b2c` (2), `ov002_021ec010` (2), `ov011_021fb02c` (needs the comma hack).
  - `ov010_0220640c` (6/2), `ov006_021e0b80` (8/7; 600 statement orders tried).
- **Dead-store sweep:** inserting a duplicate assignment at earlier positions, run over 25 overlay candidates, found no new matches. The permuter only accepts C drafts; C++ drafts fail at setup with a syntax error.

## Two-definition constants and a hand-rotated loop (main, round mainfresh4)

- **`020572f8` (605 / 203 to 6 / 6, scratch draft only).** The function sits in the `main_84` gap with `02056e74`, so it was not placed. The changes that helped, in order of size:
  - **Keep VRAM bases out of constant propagation.** The target selects between registers (`mov r10, r11; movne r10, r5`), not between immediates. Writing each base twice, `v4 = 0x6400000; if ((u32)win & 3) v4 = 0x6400000;`, keeps it in a register. Pass `v4` and `v6` into the helper inlines as parameters.
  - **Rotate the loop by hand.** `if ((c = fn(&win->str)) != 0) { setup; do { ... } while ((c = fn(&win->str)) != 0); }` puts the setup (here the two VRAM bases) in the preheader, after the first test, as the target does.
  - **Turn an unused parameter into the local that needs the low register.** The second parameter (`void *p` in the caller) became `SlideMsg *msg`. The body still assigns `msg = win->msgs;`. This fixed a whole-function register rotation.
  - **Merge the early returns into one `if (a || b || ...) return 0;`.** Separate `if`s give separate return blocks.
  - **Statement and declaration order inside blocks.** Stores through `msg` (a known offset from `win`) can be reordered by the scheduler. The order of the source statements inside each block still decides most of the schedule.
  - The last three diffs did not move:
    - two prologue stores to the same zero, swapped
    - one store scheduled across an address computation
    - two pool loads swapped
  - I tried every position of the `stop`/`nameChanged` initialisers, chained assignments and type swaps.
- **`02043100` (158 / 91 to 141 / 78, the `NONMATCHING` draft in `Gfx3D.cpp` is updated).** Changes that helped:
  - Declare `trans[3]` and `scale[3]` at function scope.
  - Assign `shape`, `name` and `ox` as statements, not as initialisers.
  - Flatten the nested inlines. The nesting depth of inline locals changes the stack slots.
  - Read the OAM attributes through a bitfield struct (`GfxOamBits`).
  - The permuter found nothing better (40 min).
- **`02053254` (79 / 20 to 76 / 16).** Writing the `y` mask with the same two-definition form helped (`kq = 0xfff; if ((u32)scene & 3) kq = 0xfff; y &= ~kq;`). Using it for `x` as well gives 74 / 20. Most of the `~0xfff` masks are still hoisted to the function entry.
- **`02069b3c`:** no change from 44 / 4.
## A string literal in a local, and merging a string-pool file (overlays, round ovmid3)

- **New matches: `func_ov006_021d5b98` and `func_ov006_021d5e68`** (a destructor and its deleting form, 0x2d0 and 0x2d8 bytes, both 2 raw diffs before). They are now in `src/ov006/ov006_021d5b98.cpp`, together with the already-matched `021d6140`, `021d630c` and `021d6724`, because all five load strings from one pool (`0x021ebfe0..0x021ec034`). The old three files are deleted, and `strrefs.py --claim` rewrote the pool.
  - The last 2 diffs were the order of the two loads before a `func_02036838(data_02097cdc, "ef/effect3darc", 7, 0)` call. The target loads the string address before `data_02097cdc`. Passing the literal through a block local gives the target's order: `{ const char *s = "ef/effect3darc"; func_02036838(data_02097cdc, s, 7, 0); }`. The next call (`"ef/effect3dtri"`) is written directly and loads in the opposite order, as the target does. With `extern` string arrays the same local trick still gives 2 diffs, so the literal is needed, and with it the pool merge.
  - When a pool merge turns a matched `extern` string into a literal, check for null tests that only existed for the `extern`. `021d6724` had `const char *name = data_...; if (name) { ... }`. MWCC folds the test for an array address but not for a literal, so the merged file emits a `cmp`. Keep the local and drop the `if`: `const char *name = "cefly1pic00";` followed by the three calls.
  - When the merged files declare the same global with different types (`data_ov000_021ccd54` as `u8 *` and as a struct pointer), declare it once and cast at the uses. The cast does not change the code.
- **Finding these:** `strrefs.py <mod> <func>` on every near-miss shows which ones load strings. A draft that is 2 or 3 diffs off only around string loads is a candidate for literals. Check that every function sharing the pool is matched (or in your file) before merging.
- **`func_ov000_0218e12c` (16 / 3 to 28 / 0, structure only).** The draft hand-inlined the manager teardown. The matched sibling file `ActorMgr_0218e0f0.cpp` already has `ActorMgr_Delete`, `ActorMgr_RemoveGroup` and `ActorGroup_Delete`. Using them, `if (data_ov000_021ccd68 != NULL) { ActorMgr *mgr = data_ov000_021ccd68; if (mgr != NULL) ActorMgr_Delete(mgr); data_ov000_021ccd68 = NULL; }`, gives the target's instructions (the separate `ldr`s instead of an `ldm`, and the branch layout). Turning `saved` into a parameter fixes `mgr`. What is left is a reversed allocation: the target gives the innermost inline's locals (`ActorGroup_Remove`'s `prev`/`next`) `r4`/`r5`, and `saved` gets `r8`. Ours runs outer to inner. Neither local inline copies with other declaration orders nor `pairarr` inside each inline nor `inline_bottom_up`/`inline_depth` changed it. The draft is `scratchpad/ovmid3/best/e12c_28_0_clean.cpp`. It belongs at the end of `ActorMgr_0218e0f0.cpp` (it fills the gap up to `0218e234`).
- **No change** from locals-as-parameters (`pp3.py`, K=2, both orders), the one-element array sweep (`pairarr.py`), the dead-store sweep (`dup.py`) or the two-definition sweep (`td1.py`) on these:
  - Leftovers: `0217a1f8` (2), `0218a5ec` (2), `021ec010` (2), `0217e560` (6/6), `021e0b80` (8/7; 420 more statement orders with separate `w`/`h` locals) and `021d36fc` (20/2).
  - Others: `02153804` (17/0; also all 120 declaration orders and 100 statement orders of `z`/`sx`/`sy`), `0219b808`, `0215e6f0`, `021dedf0`, `021e0280`, `02161994`, `0214bcc4`, `02152f8c` and `021e2490`.
  - Details:
    - `0217a1f8`: the target's argument order `r0, r1, r2` with `mvn r2, #0` needs `-1` not derived from `0x78`. A `u8` third parameter gives the right order but `mov r2, #0xff`. Other parameter types, inlines, `~0`/`0xffffffff` and pragmas do not.
    - `0218a5ec`: the target keeps the `0x8000` stack argument in `ip` from before the two `ldrh` until the `str`. 252 orderings of `t`/`k`/`a`/`b` and seven inline wrappers do not reproduce it.
    - `021e96cc` (13/4 to 13/2): calling `ov006_MidR(&self->look.y, a.y, b.y)` (operands swapped) fixes the load order. What is left is one block's registers.
    - `021eca14` (5/5 to 7/2): reading `FIELD(p, 0xc, u16)` into a local before the `0x49` store gets the target's load order. A register swap is left.
- **Blocked:** `func_ov006_021d3f04` matches exactly (`scratchpad/ovlow1/d3f04.cpp`, checked with `match.py`), but its strings share the `0x021ebc04` pool object with the unmatched `021d4118`.
- Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/ovmid3/` (`best/`, `d/`, `r/`, `t/`). `t/lev.sh dir file func mod` runs pp3, pairarr, dup and td1 on one draft. `t/unm.py` lists the unmatched functions in a size range. `t/asm.sh func` prints the target asm from `build/usa/asm`.

## Several locals as trailing parameters at once (overlays, round ovmid4)

- **New match: `func_ov002_021df2ec`** (0x4c8 bytes, `src/ov002/ov002_021df2ec.cpp`). Four of its locals (`y`, `x`, `id`, `w`) are declared as extra trailing parameters: `void func_ov002_021df2ec(void *self, u8 *own, s32 y, s32 x, u16 id, u8 *w)`. Two other changes were also needed:
  - `id` must be `u16`.
  - In the final block, `py` must be assigned before `px`.
  - What is left in `match.py` is one `bl`/`blx` interworking difference, which the linker fixes.
  - One local as a parameter (the pp3 K=1 or K=2 sweeps) was not enough. The order of the extra parameters matters, because the last parameter gets the lowest callee-saved register. `t/pps.py` writes random subsets and orders of locals as parameters, and `t/greedy.sh` repeats K=1 and keeps the best each time. Of about a dozen functions in the queue, only `021df2ec` matched this way.
- **Gains from the same lever, not matched yet** (raw / masked):
  - `0218867c`: 80/27 to 22/6. The draft takes the parameters `(CmdObj *self, u32 n_1, void *ents, void *blk)`. The case-1 global goes in a block local, and the player struct is read with `map` at `0x772`. What is left is two things:
    - An `arg`/`done` register swap. Two-definition constants on `one` and `done` make it worse.
    - The case-1 `g` load is hoisted.
  - `02160534`: 73/17 to 17/17 with the parameters `..., s32 d, s32 y, s32 x`. Only the spill slots of two locals are left.
  - `0216c310`: 106/11 to 67/11 with the parameters `(u8 *self, s32 y, u8 *next, u8 *n)`. The middle multiply/BOOL block still differs structurally.
- **`021e96cc`: the ovmid3 13/2 draft is wrong.** Swapping `MidR`'s operands changes what the function does. The best draft that does the same as the target is 14/2. It computes `u16 d = b.y; d = d - a.y;` in each block.
- **`0218e12c`: 28/0 to 17/0.** The teardown is hand-inlined, `saved` is a parameter, and the locals are declared in the order `aprev, anext, gnext, gprev, saved, mgr, group`. What is left is a `saved`/`group` register swap. I tried every order of the five inner locals and about 400 random sets of parameters. None of them gained anything.
- **`021998ac`** needs `#pragma optimization_level 2`. Without it, loop-invariant code motion hoists the sin/cos table loads. The `SetRot` tail merge is still unsolved.
- **`0215b2e4` and `0215b598`** load small constants from the literal pool. No matched code does this, so I skipped them.
- **No change** on `021eca14` (best 5/5 or 6/3). The target loads `0x1f` into `r0`, and loads `p` first.
- The drafts are in `scratchpad/ovmid4/d/`. The tools are in `scratchpad/ovmid4/t/`, and `t/unm.py` now covers ov000 to ov008.

## Spill order by type, a scale local before calls, and cached OAM words (main, round mainfresh5)

- **`020572f8` (still 6 / 6, scratch draft only).** Spill stores in the prologue follow the type of the local: a narrower local is stored first. Within one type class, `nameChanged` is always stored first. Changing types fixes the order of the two zero stores, but MWCC then stops if-converting `case 36`. Net result: no gain.
- **`02053254` (76 / 16 to 36 raw).** Two changes helped:
  - Assign a function-scope `scale = dir << 12;` before the `SetPos`/`func_ov000_02190758` calls. This stops MWCC from sinking the shift past the `bl`.
  - Use one two-definition mask `kq = ~0xfff; if ((u32)scene & 3) kq = ~0xfff;` for both `x &= kq` and `y &= kq`. The mask is then materialised in the tail block, not at function entry.
  - After those two changes, a hill-climb over locals-as-parameters, one-element arrays and the declaration grid did the rest.
  - The diffs left are register colouring in the middle block and the order of the `0x78`/`0x7c` store tail.
- **`02024730` (`NNS_G2dMakeCellToOams`, new draft, 85 raw).** Changes that helped:
  - **Give the shape mask a signed type.** The target uses `asr` for the `(shape & 0xc000) >> 14` index and `lsr` for the `0xc0000000` part. Declare the shape as `s32`, as an `int` enum would be.
  - **Read `attr01` once.** Load it into a local and pass the value to the `G2_GetOBJEffect` inline. The target reuses that load for the shape mask. Reading through the pointer twice reloads it.
  - **Write `G2_SetOBJEffect` as `if (effect != 0x100 && effect != 0x300) plain; else with rsParam;`.** This matches the branch layout.
  - **Open issue: the frame is `0x18`, not `0x20`.** In the target, `_01` and `_11` are spilled and the centre correction is written as `-hw*4096 + _00*hw + _10*hh`. That expression form gives the right `rsb`/`mla` chain. It also makes MWCC spill `isDouble` instead of the loop counter, which costs 143 against 85 for the other form.

Two new matches. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/parsweep2/` (`d/<tag>/`, `t/`). Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
