This swaps `seq`/`vol` (`r7`/`r6`) into place. New file `src/Main/Snd/SndMgr_LoadStep.cpp` (taken out of the `NONMATCHING` block in `SndMgr_2.cpp`). The file renames the header prototype with `#define func_02049690 func_02049690_hdr` around the `#include`, because `SndMgr_RequestLoad` in the header still calls the one-argument form. |
| `func_ov000_021afaf8` (robo state update, 0x8d0 bytes) | 45 / 2 | `anm` and `def` as parameters, both assigned `NULL` at the top (45 to 2). Then, in `case 3` of the mode switch, `big = TRUE;` goes first, before the `if (...a != 0) ofs = 0x1000;` and the `0x9ac`/`0x9ae` stores (2 to 0). New file `src/ov000/Robo_021afaf8.cpp`. |

- **Inline arguments as parameters (`t/argpar.py`).** This is a new form of the lever. For each argument of a `static inline` call that is not a plain name or constant, a variant assigns that argument to a new parameter (or local) on the line before the call, then passes the variable. In `02049690` it matched. In `func_ov002_021dfcc0` it took 29 / 0 to 10 / 0: the `ov002_CamMove` unit argument (`self->4->4`) becomes a parameter (`d/gr_dfcc0/g0.cpp`). One `mgr` / inline-zero swap (`r6`/`r8`) is left. Parameter and local sweeps, the single-move declaration grid, one-element arrays and inline-local parameters did not change it. In `func_02060a08`, the `slot->args[1] >> 8` argument as a `u8` local gives 13 to 10 (`d/a08t/b.c`). What is left is inside `Flags_AddSlot`: the slot pointer register and the operand order of the `orr`s. All 72 orderings and groupings of the four OR terms leave it at 10.
- **Whole-statement permutations (`t/sib.py`).** These come after the lever, because the lever can leave a pure scheduling diff. A unit is one statement, or one compound statement such as an `if`/`else` chain, a loop or a block. The tool writes every permutation of every window of 2 and 3 sibling units. It is not semantics-preserving, so check each hit by hand. It found the `afaf8` fix. It also took `func_ov012_021fc688` from 6 / 2 to 2 / 2 (moving `self->0x110 = 0` after the three flag toggles in case `0x1e`; `d/fc688/b.cpp`). The 2 left there are the `add r6, sp, #0` for `&r` in `Ov012_Fx2`, which ours schedules before the `Rot3` copy. Five other copy forms did not help.
- **Other results from the sweep:**
  - `func_ov006_021cec68` (pool blocker) went from 38 / 1 to 1 / 1 with `n` and `res` as parameters. That draft still depends on the old uninitialised-`kq` trick (`g + kq`), which is the one diff left. Without the trick it is 7 (`g` and the hoisted pool address swapped, `d/cec68/a.cpp`). `g` as a parameter in any order, every declaration slot, a block-scoped `g`, and dead `kq` forms all stay at 7.
  - `func_ov003_021e6274` (pool blocker) went from 51 to 45 with `m` as a parameter. A `self` / `t` swap through the whole function is left. All 64 permutations of up to 4 parameters, and dropping `me`, stay at 45.
  - `func_ov000_0217a1f8`: `kq` and `-1` as parameters (single and two-definition, 80 variants) stay at 2.
- **No gain from the lever** on the other pool blockers: `0204f524` (6, 26 variants), `0204f5bc` (1; even `z = i` as a parameter is constant-propagated back to `mov r4, #0`), `020508f4` (8), `0204e5d4` (30, 529 variants), `0205e5c4` / `0205c234` (2, scheduling only), `0205e29c` (9), `02060b18` (16), `ov003_021e1204` (2; bitfield views of `unk_16` give 3), `ov006_021cf420` (15), `ov006_021d4118` (26). There was also no gain on the other 55 functions with masked ≤ 10 (list in `scratchpad/parsweep2/cands0.txt` and `scanbest.txt`, logs `m.log`, `mA.log`, `n.log`, `nA.log`, `s.log`). Triples of locals as parameters (`K=3`) on `ov002_021d3e14` (18) and `ov000_0219e998` (26) changed nothing either.
- **Tools** (`scratchpad/parsweep2/t/`):
  - `norm.py src func out` splits `T a, b;` and `T a; T b;` declaration lines so that `pp3.py` sees every local. Before this, `pp3.py` found no locals at all in some drafts (for example `02060a08`).
  - `psw.sh list` runs norm, then `pp3.py` (with `PERM` when there are 9 or fewer locals), then scores.
  - `argpar.py src func out K` and `apw.sh list` are the inline-argument lever.
  - `ppi.py src inline out K` turns an inline's locals into extra inline parameters, and passes `0` at every call.
  - `sib.py src func out [W]` and `sibw.sh list` are the statement-unit permutations.
  - `greedy.sh dir func mod file` alternates `pp3` and `argpar` from the best result until nothing improves.
  - `unm.py` lists the unmatched functions from the delinks ranges.

## Inline local declaration form, and assignment moves after the parameter lever (ov000, round bigfresh4)

Two new matches in ov000. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/bigfresh4/` (`d/`, `lev/`, `q/`, `t/`). Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_021bd8ac` (0x8fc bytes) | 107 / 0 | The model-allocation inline declares its name pointer uninitialised at the top, before the initialised allocation: `char *nm; u8 *m = func_0203dd9c(0x160); if (m != NULL) { nm = *name; ... }`. Writing `char *nm = *name;` inside the `if`, or putting `nm` after `m`, leaves the pool/argument registers rotated. New file `src/ov000/ov000_021bd8ac.cpp`. |
| `func_ov000_021c194c` (0x52c bytes) | 11 / 2 | `flag` and `p` as parameters (`pp3`), then the `flag = FALSE;` that `pp3` puts at the top moves down to just before `if (!self->flags.b1) { self->unk_44 -= 0x1000; ...`. The parameter lever fixes the registers; the move fixes the scheduling. New file `src/ov000/ov000_021c194c.cpp`. |

- `func_ov000_021afaf8` was found independently with the same fix (`anm`/`def` as parameters, then `big = TRUE;` first in case 3). It landed as `Robo_021afaf8.cpp` from round parsweep2.
- **After `pp3`, move the assignments it adds.** `pp3` turns `T x = v;` into `x = v;` at the declaration point. That point is often wrong for scheduling. `t/amove.py src func mod out` moves each one-line assignment up and down by up to `AM_MAX` statements. `t/swapadj.py` moves whole statement units within a block by up to `SW_K=2`. `t/splitdecl.py` splits an initialised nested declaration into a top declaration plus an assignment at every slot, and also does the reverse. `t/qrun.sh queue log` runs lines of the form `tool src func mod tag` one after another.
- **Partial gains (not landed):**
  - `ov012_021fbec8`: 76 / 6 to 40 / 2 (two swapadj passes, `d/fbec8_s2.cpp`).
  - `ov010_0220c758`: 61 / 0 to 41 / 2 (`t` as a parameter).
  - `ov000_021a2670`: 9 / 10 to 13 / 5 (`anm` assigned before the `if`).
  - `ov012_021fc688` and `ov012_021fdfa0` are both at 2 / 2. They share one cause: `Ov012_Fx2` schedules `add r6, sp, #0` before the struct-copy stores, and the target schedules it after the `y` store.
- **No gain:**
  - `ov000_02176714` (29 / 2): 80 forms of the hoisted `1` / `-0x1ccd`, plus amove and swapadj.
  - `ov010_02201064` (176 / 6): splitdecl, amove and swapadj.
  - `ov000_021ad8ac`, `ov013_021fc7e0` and `ov015_0220cbe8` all stay at 6 / 6. In `cbe8` the target keeps `add r7, r4, #0x168`, and every sub-pointer form folds it.
  - `ov013_021fb1b4` (26 / 0): a single-move declaration grid of 5329 variants.
- **`func_0206e858` (main) still has two pool words for `data_0214b105`.** The target shares one literal-pool word with `LangData_3.cpp`. Every spelling either emits a second word or keeps the address in a register through CSE. That covers over 200 pairs, `volatile`, `static const` pointers, loop and `goto` forms, and the pragmas `opt_common_subs`, `opt_propagation`, `optimization_level` and `optimize_for_size`. It probably needs the function to sit in the same TU as the other user of the word (`d/e858/`).
## An unused register parameter as a spilled local, a pragma swap, and a two-definition scale constant (overlays, round ovmid5)

Three new matches:

| Function | Before (raw/masked) | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_02160534` (0x4b8) | 17/17 | New file `src/ov000/ov000_02160534.cpp`. |
| `func_ov002_021d98ac` (0x358) | 43/36 | New file `src/ov002/ov002_021d98ac.cpp`. |
| `func_ov000_02191c24` (0x2a0) | 4/4 | Appended to `src/ov000/Shadow_0219196c.cpp`. |

- **Use an unused register parameter for a local that spills (`02160534`).** The only diffs were the stack slots of two spills: `pid` and the 64-bit copy of `d`. The target has `pid` at the lower slot. Declaration order, statement order, types, one-element arrays and trailing stack parameters did not change the slots. A trailing parameter is spilled to its own home slot, which gives a different frame. The second argument `a` (in `r1`) is never read, so the fix was to rename it `pid` and assign it in the body. A parameter's virtual register is created before every local, which gives it the target's slot order. When a function has an unused `r0` to `r3` parameter, try each local in it before adding trailing parameters.
- **`match.py` does not compare `bl` targets.** With 0 diffs in `match.py`, the ROM check still failed for `02160534`. The draft called `_ffltu` (from `(float)(u32)`), and the target calls `_fflt`. Casting the operand to `s32` fixed it. When the check fails after a 0, compare the bytes (`cmp -l extract/usa/arm9_overlays/ovNNN.bin build/usa/build/arm9_ovNNN.bin`) and read the target's call names with `t/asm.sh`.
- **`#pragma opt_loop_invariants off`, not `optimization_level 2` (`021d98ac`).** Level 2 stops the sin/cos table loads from being hoisted, but it also turns off the tail merge of the three `MTX_RotY33_` calls in the `SetRot` switch. `opt_loop_invariants off` keeps the merge (43 to 22).
- **Read two bitfields of one halfword into locals before both stores (`021d98ac`).** `s32 x = m->bits.x; s32 z = m->bits.z; n->a = x; n->b = z;` loads the halfword once and extracts both fields before the stores, as the target does. Writing the fields straight into the stores loads the halfword twice. A struct copy (`Size98ac sz = ...`) goes through the stack. Hand-written shifts on a loaded `u16` get the order wrong. With `u8` locals the code is worse. `s32`, `u32` and `u16` all match. The only diff left in `match.py` is the `bl`/`blx` to the Thumb `MTX_RotY33_`, which the linker fixes.
- **A two-definition constant for one store of a vector (`02191c24`).** The target loads `unk_14` into `r1` and the constant `0x1000` into `r2`. Every store order, a dead first store, the `SetScaleXZ`/`VEC_Set` inlines, a block local, `u32` and one-element arrays all gave the reverse (5/0) or worse. `s32 one = 0x1000; if ((u32)self & 3) one = 0x1000;`, used only for `scale.y`, matches.
- **No change:**
  - `0218e12c` (17/0): every subset and order of up to three of its locals as parameters (139 variants), and block-scoped `group`, `gprev`/`gnext` and `aprev`/`anext`.
  - `0218867c` (22/6): all 24 orders of `kind`/`arg`/`one`/`done`, and `arg`, `done` or `one` as a parameter in each position.
  - `0219badc` (5/4): an `|= 0x40` store, and an inline or split `if` for the event-table tail (`strne` before `popeq` in the target).
  - `0218a670` (12/6): every order of the case 20 to 22 statements, `code++`, `pairarr` and `dup`.
  - `021512b4` (35/3): a widget-draw inline for the six `+0x70` store / `vfunc_20` / `vfunc_90` triples, the unused `a` as `x`/`y`/`h`, and a constant local. The first `x` is still folded into the add, and the target keeps it in `r5`.
  - `021e83d0` (6/5): `r`/`i` as parameters, and a two-definition `0x1666`.
- Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/ovmid5/` (`d/`, `t/`). `t/best.sh mod addr` scores every scratchpad draft named after an address and prints the best three.
## Open-coded searches, a u16 setter inline, and the remaining main triage (main, round mainfresh6)

Two new matches: `func_0203bc20` (VRAM range allocator, new file `src/Main/Gfx/GfxVramAlloc.cpp`, never drafted before) and `func_02048bfc` (play-time normalise, in `PlayTime.cpp`, 56 raw before). Drafts are in the scratchpad under `mainfresh6/d/`.

- **`func_0203bc20`, from 74 raw to 0.**
  - A first draft with a `GfxVramHeap_NewNode` inline for the free-node search (used twice) was right after register normalisation.
  - Writing the search out at both places, with `node` and `i` at function scope, and trying all 720 declaration orders gave 12. Only a `cur`/`i` swap was left.
  - `pp3.py` then found the fix: `cur` and `node` as extra parameters (`u32 func_0203bc20(GfxSys *sys, u32 size, BOOL sub, GfxVramNode *cur, GfxVramNode *node)`). The callers keep their 3-argument prototypes.
  - Separate inlines for the two searches gave 15, and two parameters on that version did not help. Open-coding was what made the parameter lever reach 0.
- **`func_02048bfc`, from 56 to 0.** The old drafts had the right operations but the wrong narrowing and schedule. Three changes fixed it:
  - **Store through an inline with `u16` parameters.** `PlayTime_Set(t, h, min, sec, ms)` narrows every value with `lsl`/`lsr` before its `strh`, as the target does. A plain `t->seconds = sec;` drops the narrowing.
  - **Order the work as quotient, then remainder.** Compute `ms % 1000` and `s % 60` before the next carry: `s = (u16)(ms / 1000) + t->seconds; ms %= 1000; sec = s % 60; m = s / 60 + t->minutes; min = m % 60; h = m / 60 + t->hours;`.
  - **Put the quotient first in each sum** (`s / 60 + t->minutes`, `(u16)(ms / 1000) + t->seconds`). The field-first form costs 5 to 9 register diffs.
- **The same inline in `func_02048d58` (add) and `func_02048e74` (subtract).**
  - `func_02048d58` is now right after register normalisation (66 to 29 raw). The reciprocal of 1000 and one quotient swap `r4`/`r5`. No order of the four sums, operand order or inline declaration order (720 tried) changed this.
  - In `func_02048e74` (161 to 124 raw), compute all four fields into `int` locals and store them together, so that the loads from `ToMillis` are reused. Clear with `PlayTime_Set(dst, 0, 0, 0, 0)` (the target inlines the clear) and write the test as `ToMillis(a) < ToMillis(b)`.
  - Both are in the `NONMATCHING` block of `PlayTime.cpp`.
- **`func_02049c80` (53 to 22 raw).** Draft: `d/c80/h/best_22.cpp`.
  - The target keeps `-1` in `r4` from the entry, for the first return, for the three `!= -1` tests and for `-1 - 0x7f`. A two-definition local (`s32 none = -1; if ((u32)mgr & 3) none = -1;`) gives this (53 to 36).
  - `result` as a parameter and `handle` as a one-element array give 22.
  - What is left: two `mov r0, r6` before the first two calls, because the target loads `useSlot` and `slot` into `r2` and keeps `mgr` in `r0`. Locals as parameters with K=3, a `self` copy and an inline for the start path did not change it.
- **`func_0203b70c`: the old draft stored the OAM words in the wrong order.** `oam->attr23 = 0` comes first, then `attr01`, then `attr2` (31 raw, 4 masked; `d/b70/a.cpp`). The old order cleared `attr2` after writing it. What is left is the zero/address register pair in the six-array clear loop.
- **SDK functions blocked by linker-defined constants.** These sit in pool words with no reloc, so a C literal folds into an immediate:
  - `OS_GetInitArenaLo` (`0xd000000`, which fits an immediate, is a pool load in the target);
  - `OS_GetInitArenaHi` (`0x1000` and `0x800` are compared with 0 at run time);
  - `FS_LoadOverlayInfo` (the overlay count `0x10` comes from the pool).
  
  They need an absolute symbol in the link, so they stay in the gaps.
- **`RtcCommonCallback` and other blocked functions.** `RtcCommonCallback` has a `bl` into TWL-only code (`module:none`), as do the GX load functions, `func_0200d85c` and the rest of the mainmid1 list. The main functions that are not blocked and not owned by other workers all have drafts now.
- **No change:**
  - `func_0200d36c` (card ROM read, 5): `word`/`data` use `r1`/`r0` instead of `r3`/`r1`. The draft needs `#pragma optimization_level 2` plus `opt_propagation off` to get the target's loop shape at all. No other pragma pair, no compiler version and no loop form moved the 5.
  - `func_0203c2bc` (93 / 4): parameter, `register`, `const`, alias and one-element-array forms of `sys` all stay at 93.
  - `func_0206e3a0` (2): five forms of the fog-colour write.
  - `func_02015510` and `getScaleData_` were not changed.
  - `hill.sh` over `0203d298`, `0203a984`, `020459cc`, `0206f85c`, `02047724`, `0206f6d4` and `02064b54` found nothing.
- **Tool fixes** (scratchpad `mainfresh6/t/`):
  - `hill.sh` no longer takes `pairarr2.py`'s "N variants" line as a score.
  - `hillc.sh` is a copy of `hill.sh` that keeps `.c` drafts as `.c`.
  - `scanall.sh func` scores the newest scratchpad drafts that define a function.
  - `grid.py` needs `t/tmp` to exist.
## Third locals-as-parameters sweep: a statement move plus a narrower flag type (round parsweep3)

Drafts and logs are in `scratchpad/parsweep3/` (`d/`, `drive.log`, `sib4.log`, `scan.log`). Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

- **New match: `func_020572f8`** (0x1858 bytes, the slide-window text renderer, 6 / 6 before). It is in the new file `src/Main/Gfx/SlideWin_3.cpp`, which has its own `.text` range `0x020572f8..0x02058b50`. The range leaves `02056e74` in the gap before it. Two changes:
  - **Statement move (`sib.py`, 6 to 2).** Call `SlideWin_ResetWait(win)` (`win->unk_b5c = 0; win->unk_7c = 0;`) before `if (!msg->active) SlideMsg_Open(msg);`, not after. This is safe: `msg` is `win + 0xc0` (size 0x364), so its stores cannot reach `0x7c` or `0xb5c`.
  - **The two zero spill stores in the prologue (2 to 0).** Declare `u8 stop; u8 nameChanged;` (`stop` was `BOOL`) and assign `stop = adv; nameChanged = adv;` in that order. mainfresh5 found that narrower locals are spilled first, but on the old draft the type change broke the if-conversion of `case 36`. After the statement move it no longer does.
  - A 120-variant grid shows what matters: any 8- or 16-bit type for `stop`, `stop` declared first, and `stop` assigned first. `BOOL`/`s32`/`u32` for `stop` leave the 2 diffs, and a 32-bit `nameChanged` gives 19 or more. `tools/match.py` confirms the match.

- **The full sweep** (`norm.py`, then `greedy.sh`, which alternates `pp3` K=2 with `PERM` and `argpar`, then `sib.py` W=3) ran on the best draft of every unmatched function that has a draft in `ovmid4/d`, `mainfresh5/d`, `mainfresh4/d`, `ovmid3/best` or `nearfresh1/d` and was not in parsweep2's list. `t/cands.py` builds that list and `t/drive.sh` runs it.
  - Gains, none of them close to a match:
    - `func_02043100`: 140 to 124. `addr`, `o`, `name` and `shape` become parameters (`d/g_3100/g2.cpp`).
    - `func_02024730`: 85 to 84 (one `argpar` site).
  - No change: `ov000_02153804` (17), `ov002_021eca14` (5), `ov006_021e96cc` (14), `0203b70c` (28), `02053254` (36), `02069b3c` (44), `ov000_0219763c` (93). For `020572f8`, the `sib` step after greedy gave the match above.
  - The `nearfresh1` drafts and the other `ovmid3` drafts were all in parsweep2's list already. The current best drafts of those functions are the same files parsweep2 used, or other workers' newer drafts.
- **`sib.py` hits need a semantic check.** Besides the `020572f8` move, it gave three false hits this round:
  - `ov000_0216c310` went from 67 to 25 by moving the `n->0x14` store after the `vfunc_1c` call. That call reads `0x14`, and the target stores `0x14` before `0x18`, so the move is wrong.
  - `02043100` went from 124 to 110 by moving `t0 = t1 - 0x1000` in the `flipV` swap. That turns the swap into a double subtract.
  - `ov006_021cec68` went from 7 to 6 (W=4) by moving `g = data_02097d48;` after its uses.

  A large `sib` gain on a function with structural diffs is usually alignment luck. Read the target asm around the moved statement before you trust it.
- **parsweep2 leftovers, still unsolved:**
  - `ov002_021dfcc0` (10 / 0): the `mgr` / `TryMove`-result swap (`r6`/`r8`). None of these changed it:
    - the result as a caller local (every declaration slot, by reference) or as a parameter (worse);
    - `mgr` as a parameter (22);
    - other types and shapes of the inline's result;
    - all 298 K=3 parameter sets (`pp3`);
    - `sib` W=4 (492 variants).
  - `ov012_021fc688` (2 / 2): `add r6, sp, #0` is still scheduled before the `Rot3` copy. None of these moved it:
    - a pointer local for `&r`;
    - a one-element array;
    - `base` passed by value or by reference;
    - `ry` as an inline parameter;
    - a declaration swap.
  - `02060a08` (10 / 0):
    - All 24 parameter orders of `Flags_AddSlot` change nothing.
    - So do 48 parameter-type and cast combinations for `a`/`b`.
    - Caller locals or parameters for the inline's arguments make it worse (192 variants, best 18).
    - The diff is an `a`/`b` colouring swap (`lr`/`r4`). The OR operand order is already right.
  - `ov006_021cec68` (7 / 0, hack-free): putting the `0x8b0` block in an inline (six forms), `g` as a parameter in every position, and a pointer-to-global parameter for `data_ov006_021ec884` all leave the hoisted pool address below `g`.
  - `ov003_021e6274` (45 / 2): `self`/`t` are swapped through the whole function. Re-parameterising `self`, dropping `me`, and making `t` a parameter or a local in another position all stay at exactly 45.
  - `ov000_02153804` (17 / 0): `sy` and `t` are swapped (`r8`/`sb`). None of these changed it:
    - all 120 declaration orders;
    - the 20 valid statement orders of the prologue;
    - other types for `t`, `q`, `sy` and `n`;
    - block-scoping `t`, `sy` or `n`;
    - reusing `sy` for `n`;
    - 250 random parameter sets (`pps.py`);
    - `sib` W=4 (676 variants).
- **Lesson from `020572f8`:** a fix that failed on an older draft (here the spill-type change) can work once another diff is gone. Re-run old single-lever ideas after each structural gain.
- **`ov006_021e96cc`** goes from 14 to 13 with `u16 d = b.y - a.y;` in one statement (`d/96cc/f_d1.cpp`). The 3 `bl`/`blx` diffs are interworking. In the target, `d` reuses `b.y`'s register and the result reuses `a.y`'s. The target also loads `b.x` before the `look.y` store. Forms that store into a local or into `a` change the x block's branch layout and score 33.

## Hoisting block locals and statement order (overlays, round ovmid6)

- `func_ov003_021f24ec` (43/14 before) matched after three changes together:
  - Move the block-scoped loop locals (`m`, `idx`) to function scope, declared after `map` and before `i`.
  - In the loop body, order the statements as `idx = map[i]`, then the `idx < 0` clamp, then `m = &self->models[i]`.
  - Read `def->res[idx].x` directly instead of caching `def->res` in a local.

  No one of these changes was enough alone; the register allocation only matched with all three.
- `smulbb` for a constant power of ten (`div = 10`) comes from `div = 1; for (k = 1; k < 2; k++) div *= 10;`. The loop is unrolled late, after the multiply has been chosen. This helped 021e0488 (76/47 to 65/32) and its twin `func_ov002_021d8d00`.
- Before hand-writing inlines, reuse ones that already match:
  - Caption drawing is in `src/ov006/ov006_021e1bb4.cpp`.
  - SaveData nibble get/set is in `src/ov000/Save_02197d80.c`.

  With these, 021971c4 is down to a register rotation only (masked 15 to 6).
- An exhaustive grid that hoists block declarations and moves declarations one at a time gave no gains on about 20 near-miss functions. When the remaining difference is a register rotation, declaration position alone rarely fixes it.

No new matches. The drafts are in `scratchpad/bigfresh5/best/` and `scratchpad/bigfresh5/d/`, and the tools are in `scratchpad/bigfresh5/t/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | After | What helped |
|---|---|---|---|
| `func_ov014_022153e0` | 203 / 32 | 6 / 6 | A two-definition `kq = 1` used in case 0 (`tdc.py`). The `SIN` store moved before the `f0` increment in cases 10 and 11. `v1.y = 0; v1.y -= 0x2000;` for the subtract from the zero register. The `0x14` case written as `FxMul(...) + 0x1000` with a separate `0xf33` store. |
| `func_ov012_021fbec8` | 40 / 2 | 24 / 0 | The `Bec8_S1` inline's local `t` became an inline parameter called with `0` (`ppi.py`), with the inline locals reordered to `z, y, x, lim, s`. A `u16 w = ent->unk_04;` before `timer++`. A two-definition `kz = 0` in `S1e`. |
| `func_ov015_0220182c` | 262 | 190 / 33 | Two-definition `0x7fff`. |
| `func_ov015_021fc40c` | 107 | 90 / 22 | Two-definition `4` for `blinkIdx` (`tdc.py`). |
| `func_ov010_0220c758` | 41 / 2 | 36 | Two-definition `0xffff`. |
| `func_ov010_02206974` | 152 | 150 | Two-definition constant. |
| `func_ov000_021a0d4c` | 78 / 32 | 112 / 14 | The masked score improved. The low-byte range check is written `(u16)(lo + 0xfffd) <= 1`, and each flag check as `if (ok) { ok = TRUE; if (x != 0) ok = FALSE; }`. The busy check is still if-converted where the target keeps a branch. |

- **Two-definition constant (`tdc.py`).** `T kq = LIT; if ((u32)self & 3) kq = LIT;` keeps a constant in its own register instead of an immediate or a CSE'd copy. `t/tdchain.sh` applies the best variant and runs again. Rename the earlier `kq` before each round, or every variant of the next round fails to compile on a duplicate declaration.
- **MWCC skips part of the scheduling in a basic block with 7 or more calls.** In `53e0` case 2, the second effect's virtual calls stay in source order. Removing any one call from the block makes the scheduler interleave them as the target does. Labels, `do { } while (0)`, `goto` and inline wrappers do not split the block. The last 6 / 6 of `53e0` is this, and the cause is probably different source structure for that case.
- **Inline locals as inline parameters (`ppi.py`).** This works the same way as the locals-as-parameters lever, but inside an inline. Permuting the order of the inline's own local declarations then moved the remaining registers.
- **No gain:**
  - `ov000_021a2670` stays at 13 / 5. Tried: the register of `lock`, permutations, struct forms, and an inlined case body.
  - `ov000_021a4a10` stays at 169. With stack offsets masked (`t/sdrs.py`) the diff is about 40, mostly one spill of `self + 0x10c`.
  - `ov000_021a7af0` stays at 160 / 66. Most of the diff is in how the constants `0x200` and `0x52` are built, and in scheduling order.

## A split carry, `int` locals for the sums, a late declaration, and a linker-script constant (main, round mainfresh7)

Three new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `mainfresh7/d/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_02048d58` (play time add) | 29 / 0 | Two changes, both needed. In `PlayTime_Normalize`, the millis carry is split around the remainder: `s = (u16)(ms / 1000); ms = ms % 1000; s += t->seconds;`. In the function, the millis and seconds sums go through `int` locals before their stores (`int ms = a->millis + b->millis; int sec = ...; dst->millis = ms; dst->seconds = sec;`). Each change alone stays at 25 or 29; together they give 0. `func_02048bfc` still matches with the changed inline. `PlayTime_1.cpp` (`func_02048cf0`) is merged into `PlayTime.cpp`, so the file now covers `0x02048be4`..`0x02048e74`. |
| `func_0203b70c` (graphics init) | 31 / 4 | In the OAM loop, `int y = (i >> 2) << 6;` moves below `int x = ...;` and the `oam->attr23 = 0;` store (a declaration after a statement, which C++ allows). This fixes both the zero/address registers of the earlier six-array clear loop and the second loop. `t/swapadj.py` found it; it was the only one of 163 unit moves that helped. Appended to `src/Main/Gfx/GfxSys.cpp`, which now ends at `0x0203b924`. |
| `FS_LoadOverlayInfo` (`0200f274`) | gap | The overlay count is `__OVERLAY_COUNT`, which the `dsd` linker script already defines as an absolute symbol (`__OVERLAY_COUNT = 16;`). `extern char __OVERLAY_COUNT[];` compared as `(u32)__OVERLAY_COUNT` gives a relocated pool word, as in the SDK build. The rest is the TWL SDK source (the in-memory table first, then the ROM-header table through `CARD_GetOwnRomHeader() + 0x50/0x58`). Appended to `libs/nitro/fs/fs_overlay.c`, which now ends at `0x0200f3f0`. |

- **Move initialised declarations past statements.** Swapping the two declarations (`x` before `y`) changes nothing in `0203b70c`; only moving `y` below the first store does. Run `swapadj.py` (which treats a declaration as a movable unit) before the parameter and type levers.
- **Split an expression into two statements.** The `d58` fix came from writing `s = q; ...; s += field;` instead of `s = q + field;`. `t/splitx.py src func mod out` tries this on every `x = L op R;` in a function and its inlines (`x = L; x op= R;`, and `x = R; x op= L;` for commutative operators). It found nothing on the other near-misses below, but it is cheap to run.
- **`OS_GetInitArenaHi` / `OS_GetInitArenaLo` stay in the gaps** (matched in round twl2, see the last section). Their pool words with no reloc are `0x023e0000`, `0x0d000000`, `0x02fff000`, `0x02fff680`, `0x02fe15c0` (end of DTCM), `0x01ffcac0` (end of ITCM), `0x02298140` and `0x022183c0` (main arena starts), `0x1000` and `0x800` (stack sizes). `dsd`'s linker script defines only `__ITCM_HI`, `__DTCM_LO`, `__CODE_HI` and `__OVERLAY_COUNT`, and none of those is the DTCM end or the TWL arena start. Matching them needs either a `dsd` patch that writes the SDK's `SDK_SECTION_ARENA_*`, `SDK_SYS_STACKSIZE` and `SDK_IRQ_STACKSIZE` symbols into the `.lcf`, or a post-processing step that appends them.
- **No change:**
  - `func_02049c80` (22): an `SndMgr *self` copy (plain or two-definition) for the calls or for the rest, a slot-load inline returning `-1`, and `return none` or `(u32)-1` for the slot test. The target keeps `mgr` live in `r0` up to the first calls, which none of these forms gives.
  - `func_02048e74` (126 / 44 with the new inline): `u16` field locals with `u32` borrows give 93 raw but 61 masked (`d/e74/k.cpp`), and `hill.sh` on that took it to 89 / 60 (`d/e74/h/best_89.cpp`, `sec` as a parameter). Our build still spills a dead `smull` low word to `[sp]`.
  - `func_02046070` (6): local copies, parameters and a block local for the `WH_ChangeSysState` value and pointer.
  - `splitx`, `amove`, `swapadj` and `sib` over `02056e74` (4), `02046070` (6), `0203d298` (7), `0204205c` (8), `020459cc` (9), `0203a984` (10), `02006e48` (13), `0206f85c` (14), `02047724` (15), `0206f6d4` (15) and `02049c80` (22) found nothing, and neither did `swapadj` with `SW_K=5` (`q/q.log`). Moving the inlined multiply into an `FxMul` inline (four forms, both operand orders) leaves `02056e74` at 4.
- **The remaining main gaps** (`mainfresh7/gaps.txt`, ranked in `mainfresh7/scan.out`): apart from the skip list, the functions without drafts all have a `module:none` reloc (TWL-only code) or are hand-written asm (`02004f94`, `02075810`, `02075d4c`, the `02009508` epilogue fragment). `02058d10`, `02058f2c` and `0204c158` are at 0 with literals but are TU1/TU2 string-pool blockers.
## Bitfield stores as a register lever, and string-pool blockers redrafted (round poolfresh1)

Drafts are in `scratchpad/poolfresh1/` (`best/`, `d/<func>/`). Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

- **New match: `func_ov006_021cec68`** (0x6e8 bytes, the network menu task's init), now `src/ov006/ov006_021cec68.cpp` with its own `.text` range `0x021cec68..0x021cf350`. Its two strings are `extern` arrays, so it lands without the ov006 pool (which still needs `021cf420` and `021d4118`). The fix is one statement. The draft had `g[0x8b0] |= 0x30;` on the byte at `data_02097d48 + 0x8b0`; writing it as two one-bit bitfield stores matched it (7 to 0):

  ```c
  ((ov006_Bits8b0 *)(g + 0x8b0))->b4 = 1;
  ((ov006_Bits8b0 *)(g + 0x8b0))->b5 = 1;
  ```

  MWCC merges the two read-modify-writes into the same `ldrb; orr #0x30; strb`, so the instructions do not change. The register allocation does: `g` moves to `r4` and the hoisted `&data_ov006_021ec884` to `r5`, as in the target. Matched code in ov002 (`021ccda0`, `021e779c`) already writes this byte as bitfields. `n` and `res` stay parameters (the parsweep lever).
- **Try it on other register-only near misses.** If a draft ORs or clears several bits of one field at once and only registers differ, write the bits as separate bitfield stores, and the reverse. It did nothing on `ov006_021cf420` (`flags |= 6` as `f.b1`/`f.b2`) or `02060a08` (the packed slot word as a bitfield or `u8` struct is 42).
- **`func_ov003_021e6274`: 45 to 9 without hacks** (`best/e6274_9_u16c.cpp`, no `(u32)self & 3`). In case 2, read the slot into a separate `u16 c` and keep `n` for the decremented value:

  ```c
  c = self->unk_18;
  if (c == self->unk_93_1 || c == 0) return;
  func_ov000_02197dec(self, c, -1);
  self->unk_93_1 = c;
  n = c - 1;
  ```

  The `self`/`t` swap through the whole function is gone, and `n` takes `r5` with the bitfield value in `ip`, as in the target. What is left is one extra `and r1, r5, #0xff`, because a `u16` (or `s32`/`u32`) `c` is converted to the `u8` bit-field type. With `u8 c` the `and` goes, but the store's value computation is sunk past `n = c - 1`, `n` gets a new register and `self`/`t` rotate again (46). Every `u8 c` form tried stays at 46: 30 declaration orders, block scope, manual read-modify-write of the byte, a separate index variable, a `PlayAnim(self, idx)` inline (40 variants), other container types for the bit-field.
- **No change:**
  - `ov003_021e1204` (2). About 700 variants: `k` types and declaration orders, `switch`, `Kind` inlines on the entry or the value, a dispatch inline, a variable reused for `unk_06` and the kind, a `lo:12, kind:4` bitfield view (3), `goto`. The load of `unk_16` always takes `r0`; the target puts it in `r1` (the dead entry pointer).
  - `ov006_021cf420`. Without the two-definition `kq`, case 5's tail merges into case 4's (60). An inline with a state parameter, a local `st`, and the case-4 locals as parameters (80 sets) change nothing; `state + 2` breaks the merge but costs 32.
  - `0204f524` / `0204f5bc`. The natural form (`win->numBlocks = 0;` and a constant ternary, no two-definition `a`/`c`) gives the target's instructions with a rotation (12: `i` `r7`, `0x6600000` `r5`). Parameters for `i`, `a` or `c` do not move it.
  - `02060a08` (10 / 0): all 54 orders and groupings of the four OR terms.

Neither of these changed it: statement orders in either branch, a two-definition constant (`TDLIT`, 212 variants), or the shared call written once with variable arguments (24).

## String-pool blockers, round 2: where the remaining diffs come from (round poolfresh2)

No new matches, so no pool is unblocked. The drafts are in `scratchpad/poolfresh2/best/`, and the variant sets are in `d/<func>/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

- **The ov006 pool `0x021ebc04..0x021ebc65` depends only on `021d4118`.** Every string from `"nut00"` on is loaded by functions in `0x021d3498..0x021d5b98`. The only gaps there are `021d3f04`, which matches (`scratchpad/ovlow1/d3f04.cpp`), and `021d4118`. The string before it, `" "`, ends exactly at `0x021ebc04`. So the pool can probably be split there, and `021cf420` (in the `0x021ebbb0` part) is not needed for it.
- **`func_ov006_021d4118` is still 26 / 4.** Only the camera block differs. In the target, `fp` holds the `data_ov006_021eb6fc` base from `rot.x`/`rot.y` (loaded before the two `ldm`/`stm` vector copies) until `rot.z` (loaded after them), and `0x4400` is in `r3`. In our build the three `ldrh` stay together and `0x4400` gets `fp`. Unchanged at 26 or worse, about 600 variants:
  - declaration and store orders;
  - `rot` copied field by field, or with `rot.z` read after the vector copies;
  - a table pointer local, in the block or at function scope;
  - inlines that take each argument by value or by pointer, or that copy inside the inline;
  - direct field stores, and `0x4400` written inside the inline;
  - comma expressions, a C++ reference, one-element arrays.
  The permuter cannot parse the draft as it is (the `VCALL(x, 08)` octal token), so it was not run.
- **`func_ov003_021e6274` (9):** `int c = (u8)self->unk_18;` gives the target's `ldrsh` plus `and #0xff`. The extra `and r1, r5, #0xff` for the bit-field store stays with an `int`, `u16`, `u32` or `s16` `c`, and with an explicit cast, `c & 7`, a `u8` setter inline or a bit-field view in another container. Every form in which the `and` goes away (a `u8` `c`, or a `u32` bit-field view) brings back the `self`/`t` rotation (46). The cause: `n = c - 1` gets its `and #0xff` scheduled before the bit-field insert, so `n` cannot share `r5` with `c`. Writing `c - 1` directly in both uses with a `u16 c` (`best/e6274_6_nolocal_n.cpp`) scores 6 / 4, but the instructions do not line up any better.
- **`func_0204f5bc` (12 to 1, the round-mainpool8 floor):** the two-definition bases from `SlideWin.cpp` (`b4`/`b6` with `if ((u32)win & 3)`) plus a function-scope `long k` declared before them (`best/f5bc_1_k0.cpp`). What is left is the round-mainpool8 problem: `mov r4, #0` instead of `mov r4, r5`, or (DUIS order) the `strb` after the base `mov`s. Bit-field views of `numBlocks` make it a read-modify-write (6 or more), so this lever does not fix it.
- **`func_0205e5c4` (2):** the target stores `unk_14` before `unk_04`. Every form that stores in that order needs one more callee-saved register (17): a chained assignment, a pointer local, a zero local, an inline, all 80 orders of the block and 216 type and declaration combinations.
- **Bit-field lever, no gain:** `ov003_021e1204` (the `unk_a2_0`/`unk_cc` clears in 8 forms, 2 or worse), `020508f4` (`box->flag4 = 1` for `unk_000 |= 0x10`, still 8 / 0), `0204f5bc` (above).
- **Also unchanged:** `ov003_021e1204`, where reusing the index variable for `unk_16` (40 variants) keeps the load in `r0`. `ov006_021cf420`, where the `r6`/`r7` swap of the zero and `blk` survives moving the case-4 locals to function scope (93 variants). Without `kq`, storing `state` right after `unk1C` in case 5 scores 34 / 7.

## Locals as parameters, then parameter order, then statement moves (overlays, round ovmid7)

Four new matches, all from one recipe. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov002_021e5974` (0x9c, model-setup vfunc) | 12 / 0 | The loop pointer `p` becomes an unused second parameter. New file `src/ov002/ov002_021e5974.cpp`. |
| `func_ov002_021ddf18` (0x62c, menu/cursor setup) | 131 / 2 | `cam` and `w` as parameters (36 / 2). Then `h = ...n1;` before `w = ...n0;` (a `sib.py` swap, 32 / 0). Then the parameter order `(self, owner, s32 h, u8 *cam, s32 w)`, with `h` as a third extra parameter (0). New file `src/ov002/ov002_021ddf18.cpp`. |
| `func_ov008_021cfa38` (0x4e4) | 89 / 0 | `ents` and `i` as parameters (29 / 2), then `res` (20 / 2), then the order `(m, u16 i, Ov008SaveEnt *ents, void *res)` (0). New file `src/ov008/ov008_021cfa38.cpp`. |
| `func_ov002_021d8d00` (0x148, two-digit OAM number) | 20 / 4 | `div`, `x`, `chr` and `k` as trailing parameters (6 / 4). Then `y = m->0x100;` as a plain `s32`, with `(u16)y & 0x1ff` in the loop's store instead of before the loop. Then the `y`/`x` loads move above `if (val >= 100) val = 99;` (`sib.py`, 0). New file `src/ov002/ov002_021d8d00.cpp`. |

- **The recipe (`scratchpad/ovmid7/t/chain.sh src func mod tag`):**
  1. `pp3.py` with `K=2`, `PERM=1` and `HDR=1`: every one or two locals as trailing parameters, in every order.
  2. `pperm.py src func nkeep out` on the best result: every order of the added parameters, and every order with one more top-level local added. In `021ddf18` and `021cfa38` this step went from 32 or 20 to 0. In `ov002_021dedf0` it went from 9 to 5.
  3. `sib.py` (statement-unit permutations, W=3) on that best result, then check each hit by hand.

  Earlier rounds ran step 1 without `PERM` (new parameters only in one order) and never ran step 2. They also picked candidates by masked score ≤ 10, so drafts like `021e5974` (12 / 0) and `021cfa38` (89 / 0) were never swept in their current form. Run the chain on every draft whose masked score is 0, whatever the raw score.
- **Write a loop-invariant mask in the loop body (`021d8d00`).** The target computes `(u16)m->0x100 & 0x1ff & 0xff` before the loop, after the clamp and the other preheader code. With the mask written before the loop, its longer dependence chain is scheduled first in the block. What matches is a plain `s32 y = m->0x100;` (placed with the `x` load, above the clamp) and `oam[n].y = (u16)y & 0x1ff;` in the loop: LICM hoists the mask to the end of the preheader.
- **Per-branch tails for a jump to a jump (`ov000_0217ea30`).** In the target, two of the three `if` arms end their null-`new` test with `beq` to a lone `b` at the end of that arm. Only the last arm jumps straight to the shared code. This is a tail merge done after branch optimisation: the arms first ended with the same instructions, and those were merged later. Writing `o = new ...; self->0x3c = o; o->vfunc_10(b);` at the end of each arm, not once after the `if`, gives the target's whole branch layout (70 / 0 to 58 / 0). Putting all the setup code that follows into each arm is too much (108).
  - With `a` and `b` as locals instead of parameters, `self` gets the target's `r4` (18 / 9). But then `b` and `o` swap, and one arm's tail no longer merges.
  - Drafts: `scratchpad/ovmid7/d/ea30/w2.cpp` (58 / 0) and `g4/v002.cpp` (18 / 9).
- **`ov002_021dedf0`: 19 / 0 to 5 / 0** (`d/dedf0/w2.cpp`). The remaining diff swaps `cols` and the hoisted `i * cols` product. A hand-written `row = i * cols` gets the target's register, but it is scheduled before the inner loop's zero-trip test (6 / 2). This is the same trade-off as the `func_ov002_021e0ff8` note: a hand-written loop invariant gets a late register, and an LICM-hoisted one is placed right but gets an early register. K=3 with every parameter order (1112 variants) also stays at 5.
- **No change, not worth repeating:**
  - `021971c4` (87 / 6) is a pure three-way rotation of `self`, `tgt` and the zero register. The masked 6 is an alignment artefact of `difflib`. Nothing changed it at all: `a` with or without `= NULL`, `a` hoisted to every top position, a two-definition zero passed as the `func_ov000_02190cb0` argument, and every K=2 parameter set.
  - `ov006_021e6eb0` (23 / 10 to 10 / 8). The target computes and stores `trig` before `release`, with both `(u16)~x` casts. 600 forms gave at best 10 / 8 (`d/6eb0/g1/v030.cpp`): statement orders, casts, local types, explicit temporaries and inline helpers.
  - `ov003_021d883c` (3 / 2). An 8-byte struct copy of `x`/`y` gives the old order. An `s64` copy loads the high word first. None of these pragmas changed it: `optimization_level 0..3`, `opt_*`, `peephole`, `schedule`, `scheduling`.
  - `ov000_0219d9fc` (3 / 2). `0x9ae > 0x9ac` loads in the target's order, but puts the values in `r1`/`r0` instead of `r2`/`r1`. Locals, inline compares and casts did not fix it.
  - `ov002_021e0488` (65 / 32). `s32 out[3]` gives the target's 0x78 frame, but puts `tmpl` below `out` (56 / 23).
  - `ov000_0215b2e4`, `0215b598`, `0215d4b4` and `021535a8` are still the pooled-G3-constant blocker. An empty `asm {}` does not change the code; `asm { nop }` breaks it.

## Constant hoisting threshold, materialised flags, and `const` inline params (largest functions, round bigfresh7)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `bigfresh7/d/<tag>/`. Scores are raw / register- and stack-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | After | Change |
|---|---|---|---|
| `func_ov003_021d8fc0` | 457 / 184 | 325 / 81 (`d/8fc0/b3.cpp`) | Three changes. (1) `Seq_IsValid(const Ov003Sequence *self) { BOOL r = FALSE; if (self->active) { r = self->entries != NULL; } return r; }` gives the target's two separate checks with a reload in between. (2) Empty `case 0: case 1: case 2: break;` in `Seq_Run` turns the compare chain into the target's jump table. (3) Cases 3/4 use `Seq_SetPair(self, Seq_Cur(self))`, and the entry test is `Seq_IsCtl(const Ov003SeqEntry &e)` (by reference, so there is no copy). Still different: the loop makes three struct copies where the target makes two, and the final `e = Seq_Cur(self)` is CSE'd from the loop copy, where the target reloads it. |
| `func_ov000_021743bc` | 129 / 2 | 121 / 4 (`d/43bc/n4.cpp`) | `new` expressions on small classes with a `static operator new` that calls `func_0203dd9c` and a ctor (the pattern from `Menu_0215429c.cpp`) give the `movs rX, r0; beq` allocation tests. Passing `g` as a parameter, and reordering the `func_0205976c`/`func_0205975c` locals, gets to 121. The target colours 1→r4, 0→r5, o→r6, g→r7. We put `g` in sb (as a parameter) or r4 (as a local). Declaration permutations and greedy found nothing better. |
| `func_ov000_02191f18` | 220 / 130 | 393 / 78 (`d/1f18/best_393_78m.cpp`) | Each of cases 8–13 writes the scale out in full: `h = m->unk_cc; sc = (s16)m->unk_ce; k = FALSE; if (h < 0) h = 0x64; if (sc != 0 && sc != 0x1000) k = TRUE; c = p[N]; if (k) c = ...;`. This gives the target's materialised flag. Raw is worse because we hoist 0, 0x1000 and 1, and the target hoists only 1. Keep `a0.cpp` (220 raw) as the raw base. |
| `func_ov008_021ce518` | 1 / 1 | 1 / 1 | The only diff is the switch root, `cmp #0x15` against `#0x14`. Dummy cases 18 or 19 mapped to default move the root to 0x14 but leave a dead `cmp #0x13`. Not solved. |

- **Constant hoisting threshold, micro-tested (`bigfresh7/x/`).** MWCC hoists a constant into a callee-saved register at function entry when it has **5 or more register uses**; 4 uses are not hoisted. Compare-immediate uses do not count. The threshold does not change with:
  - the literal's form: `0`, `0L`, `0u`, `(u16)0`, `static const`, `enum`, or `FX32_CONST`;
  - the parameter type (s16, u16 or u32);
  - wrapping the use in an inline.

  Zero stores inside a loop, together with other zero stores, also push the zero to entry. Under high register pressure only some constants are hoisted. When the target keeps a constant local and we hoist it (1f18, 8fc0), the fix is to have fewer uses, not to write the literal differently.
- **Materialised bool (`mov rX,#0 ... movne rX,#1; cmp rX,#0`).** Two forms produce it:
  - `BOOL k = 0;` and `if (cond) k = 1;` with other statements between them, and the `k` test after a load;
  - `r = 0; if (a) { r = b != NULL; }` inside an inline.

  A plain `k = FALSE; if (a && b) k = TRUE;` is jump-threaded away, or comes out as `movne`/`moveq`.
- **A `const T *self` inline parameter keeps a bitfield byte reload.** With it, `if (!self->active) return; if (!IsValid(self)) return;` stay as two loads. With a non-const pointer, the second load is CSE'd.
- Passing a struct by value to an inline, or returning one, makes a stack temporary. Use `const &` to avoid the copy.

## How MWCC lowers a sparse `switch`, and permuter runs on the largest drafts (round bigfresh8)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `bigfresh8/d/<tag>/`. Scores are cxxperm scores (aligned + register-masked), except where noted.

| Function | Before | After | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|

The target loads `t` before the first call. What is left is `g` in `sb` (target `r7`), plus the order of `mov r0, rT` / `mov r1, #0x1a` before `func_ov000_02154b58`. Writing it with a `u16` id variable, a `?:`, an inline or a cast on the literal does not change that order. |
| `func_ov000_02166180` | 621 | 402 (`d/6180/perm/out-402-*.cpp`) | In the second loop, pass `pos[j].x + ex` straight to the inline (s32). The draft's `s16 x, y` locals add `lsl`/`asr` pairs. The rest came from cxxperm, mostly locals turned into trailing parameters. That result is too noisy to land as it is. |
| `func_ov000_021652e4` | 333 | 258 (`d/52e4/perm/out-258-*.cpp`) | cxxperm only. Spill slots are the main difference: the target's order from `sp` up is `i`, `tbl`, `y0`, `x0`, `row`, then the template copy. Reordering the declarations changes almost nothing (328). |
| `func_ov003_021d8fc0` | 610 | 488 (`d/8fc0/perm/out-488-*.cpp`) | cxxperm on bigfresh7's `b3.cpp`, 13,000 compiles. The struct-copy count is unchanged. |
| `func_ov008_021ce518` | 1 / 1 | 1 / 1 | Switch root, not solved. The rules below show why. |

How MWCC lowers a sparse `switch` (worked out with one-line test files):

- **3 or fewer cases** become a linear `cmp`/`beq` chain. **4 or more** become a binary tree. A node is the sorted case at index `(n-1)/2` and is emitted as `cmp k; bgt right; bge k_body; left...`.
- **Cases close together form a cluster** when `hi - lo + 1 <= 2 * count`, for example `{30,31}`, `{30,33}` or `{16,18,20}`, but not `{30,34}` or `{16,20}`. A cluster is one node: `cmp hi; bgt right; cmp lo; blt left; beq lo_body; cmp v; beq ...` for each other member, in ascending order.
- **Cases that go to `default`** stay in the tree and keep their compares. The only exception is the last compare of a chain (`beq D; b D` becomes `b D`). That is how `case 21: default:` in `ce518` gives the cluster `{16,20,21}` with root `cmp #0x15` and no compare for 21.
- The target's `cmp #0x14; bgt; cmp #0x10; blt; beq; cmp #0x14; beq` is a cluster `{16,20}` with nothing between them. Under these rules that cannot happen without a dead compare inside the chain. These all fail: dummy cases 0–45 (alone, sharing each real body, or as an empty `case X: break;`), pairs of dummies from -100 to 1000, every source order of the case blocks, case ranges (MWCC has no `case a ... b`), and the pragmas `optimize_for_size`, `optimization_level 1-4`, `switch_tables`, `opt_*` and `peephole`. Dense sets such as 16–20 become a jump table.

Here `sh` stays a plain local; `sh[1]` gives 29. New file `src/ov002/ov002_021dedf0.cpp`. |
| `func_ov000_0217ea30` (0x364) | 18 / 9 and 58 / 0 | From the ovmid7 `g4/v002.cpp` draft (`a` and `b` as locals, per-arm tails): `b` as a third parameter and `void *a[1]`. The 58 / 0 draft (`a` and `b` as parameters) also reaches 0 when `a` is copied into `void *a_p[1] = {a};` at the top. New file `src/ov000/Lo_0217ea30.cpp`. |

- **The combined lever (`scratchpad/ovmid8/t/ppa.sh src func mod tag`).** `pp3.py` with K=1 makes each local a trailing parameter in turn. Then `pairarr.py` with `PA_ONE=1` runs on the base and on each result: every local becomes `T x[1]`, and every parameter is copied into a one-element array. Each result is scored. On the ovmid7 base of `021e0280` (20 / 0) it finds the match in about 25 seconds. Neither lever alone matched: ovmid7's K=2 parameter sweep stopped at 7, and the ovfresh2 array sweep had never been run after the parameter step. `K=2` sweeps pairs of parameters first, which is much slower.
- **What it changes.** All three fixes are a swap of two callee-saved registers between a pointer local and another long-lived value (the hoisted `i * cols` product in `021e0280`, `self` and `o` in `0217ea30`). A one-element array is assigned at a different point in the colouring order than a scalar local, so it can trade places with a value that no declaration order moves.
- **Port a matched sibling.** `021dedf0` and `021e0280` do the same loop on different shapes. Once `021e0280` matched, copying its body into `021dedf0` (with only the shape lookup changed) matched at once, where 1112 K=3 parameter variants had stayed at 5.
- **Partial gains (drafts in `scratchpad/ovmid8/`):**
  - `ov006_021dacb0`: 41 / 7 to 24 / 7 with the `unk14A` loop counter as `int i_1[1]` (`d/dacb0/i1.cpp`). What is left is the inlined `SlotManager_Reset` loop: the target keeps `i * 0x4c` in `sl` and adds it to the base again after the `MI_CpuFill8` call. Ours keeps the slot pointer. Local copies of the inline with `u8`/`u16`/`int`/`u32` counters and pointer forms do not change it.
  - `ov000_0218867c`: 57 / 6 to 22 / 6 in two `ppa.sh` passes (`d/867c/a2.cpp`: `blk` and `n_1` as parameters, `blk` copied into `void *blk_p[1]`, `ents` as `[1]`).

  - `ov000_02199554`: 19 / 2 to 13 / 0 (`d/9554/c.c`). In the first loop, test `part == NULL` and `break` before `out[n] = part;`, because the target does not store the NULL. In the second loop, put the `a != NULL` arm first. `ov000_02199180` reaches 14 / 0 with the `DistSq9180` locals declared and summed in x, y, z order (`d/9180/v0.cpp`). Both now differ only in the scratch registers of the shared loop: the target puts `a` in `r0` and the `found` base in `r2`, ours puts them in `r2` and `r1`. Loop shapes, `if` orientation, a ternary, the inline written out, local types and K=2 parameters do not move them.
- **No change from `ppa.sh`** (K=1 on all of these; K=2 as well on `021e51f8`, `02189b44`, `02199180`, `02199554`, `02161994`, `021708ec`, `02153804` and `021d3e14`): `ov006_021e51f8` (2 / 0; a local copy of `func_ov006_021e4bec` inlined at the `unk18 = 1` store changes nothing), `ov000_02189b44` (5 / 0), `0215e6f0` (9 / 0), `0219b808` (4 / 0; also all 120 parameter orders of the `Send` inline), `02161994` (10 / 0), `021708ec`, `02153804`, `ov002_021d3e14`, `0219e998`, `ov003_021cd744`, `02165a20`, `0214dd3c`, `ov008_021ce518`, `0219d9fc`, `02171478`, `ov003_021d112c`, `021597dc`, `0214bcc4`, `02176714`, `0218cfc8`, `ov003_021d6a3c`, `021743bc`, `02181c4c`, `ov006_021e8ef8`, `02196258`, `ov006_021e6eb0`, `ov002_021e7ea8`, `ov006_021de6bc`, `ov006_021e2490`, `0218a670`, `0217e560`, `02152f8c`, `021512b4`, `0219badc`, `ov003_021e83d0`, `ov002_021eca14`, `ov006_021e0b80`, `0218e12c`, `ov003_021d36fc`, `ov006_021e7004`, `ov006_021e96cc`, `ov003_021d883c`, `ov003_021e6274`. `0217558c` (67 to 64) and `0219763c` (99 to 93) moved a little.

## Fresh redrafts with sibling idioms, `const` tables, and the permuter for register order (ov000 0x021a0000+ and ov009–ov015, round ovhimid2)

Five new matches, each confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build whose ROM check passes. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovhimid2/d/<tag>/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff; a `bl`/`blx` diff on a thumb SDK call is a linker artefact and is not counted.

- **`func_ov010_02206974` (148 to 0) and `func_ov014_0220a67c` (103 to 0): declare the table `const`.** Both drafts were exact apart from the order of a table load and a store. Declaring the table as `extern void *const data_...[]` (6974) or `extern const Ov014Vec data_...[]` (a67c) lets the scheduler hoist the load above the store, as in the target. When the file you merge into already declares the symbol with another type, put the `const` declaration in a namespace, `namespace Ov010T6974 { extern "C" void *const data_ov010_0220f024[]; }`, and use `(const char *)Ov010T6974::data_ov010_0220f024[i]`. `extern "C"` makes it the same symbol.
- **`func_ov010_022000d8` (68 / 40 to 0), the fx-object update in `ov010_021fff80.cpp`.** What fixed it:
  - an aggregate initialiser for the rotation, `Ov010FxRot rot = {0, 0, 0};`, then the two field stores;
  - the idle test as a `const Ov010FxObj *` inline that returns a `BOOL r = TRUE; if (o->b0 && o->sw.mode != 0) r = FALSE;`;
  - mode 2's cases 0 and 1 as one `case 0: case 1:` with `if (o->sw.state == 0) { ... }` inside;
  - the two handles copied into a block-local `s32 h[2]` with a `u32 i` loop at the end.
- **`func_ov000_021b4130` (no draft before; first draft 428, then 0).** The function is new in `src/ov000/Robo_021b4130.cpp`, a gap of its own. The steps that mattered:
  - The actor lookup as the usual `FindActor(u16 id)` inline. The target loads the id before the manager's null test, so the argument has to be evaluated before the inline body.
  - The target places `switch` cases 0 and 1 after the common tail. Write `default:` first, and put the tail (the matrix translation and the `MI_Copy48B`) inside it, ending with `return`.
  - A `VecSet(&v, x, y, z)` inline for the three vector stores whose loads all come before the stores.
  - The bitfield-looking `(x << 25) >> 16` is `(u16)(LFLD(u16, g, 0xa) << 9)`, a plain halfword shifted and truncated, not a 7-bit field.
  - The sin/cos block: `sn`, `cs` and `d` as locals, then `cs = FX_Mul(d, cs); sn = FX_Mul(d, sn); v.x += sn; v.z += cs; v.y -= 0x800;`. A statement-order search over that block found it.
- **`func_ov009_0220a128` (90 to 0), modelled on `func_ov009_0220aeb8`.** The first draft had 0 masked diffs, but every long-lived value was in the reverse register (target `f` r4, zero r5, `pl` r6, `st` r7, `self` r8; draft `self` r4 up to `st` r8). Declaration order, types, inlines for the repeated blocks and two-definition tricks did not change it. Taking `f` out of the locals moved `self` to r8, which showed that `f` was being coloured as a local. The permuter (`--inlines`, 232 compiles) then found the target's colouring. Cleaned up, the form is:
  - `st` as a parameter, `void func_ov009_0220a128(Ov009Actor *self, u8 st)`, assigned in the first test. This is the locals-as-parameters trick from round ovmid7; making it a local again costs 12.
  - `u32 f = ...`, `BOOL flag = ...` and `Ov009Actor *pl = ...` declared where they are first assigned, not at the top of the function.
  - In the respawn case, `z` and `y` declared at function level, and `s32 t = self->pos.z; z = t;` inside the block. `z = self->pos.z` directly costs 4.
- **`func_ov009_02208b24` (fresh draft, 94).** The sqrt sequence copies the ov014 idiom (`OV014E_SQRTCNT`). The second stack vector and the `ldm`/`stm` copy come from a `Toward(self, const Vec *t, s32 k)` inline with `Vec d; Vec q = *t;`: the last-declared local sits highest. Swapping the `state` and `p` declarations is worth 16. The `0x19a` compare is still derived from the `0x66` (`add r1, r4, #0x134`). `< 0x19b` gives the pool load but the wrong constant. A two-definition `k` inside `Toward` fixes the compare (61) but is not clean (`x/b.cpp`).
- **`func_ov014_0220f3f0` (fresh draft, 89 / 12), the box-distance helper called by `func_ov014_0220f20c`.** The draft uses `RotYXZ` from `Robo_021b3738.cpp` and an axis inline that takes `const s32 *ext`. The extent is read once into `e` for the test and the `FX_Div`, and reread for the `VEC_MultAdd` scale. The dot product is `dot = x + y + z` in one expression; that gives the target's y, x, z multiply order, and three `+=` statements do not. What is left is `&ax`: the target keeps it in `r6` across each `if (d > 0x1000)` block.
- **Near misses, with the best drafts:**
  - `ov012_021ffd34` (`d/fd34/c.cpp`, 30, register-only);
  - `ov011_021fd638` (`d/d638/k3.cpp`, 32 / 5);
  - `ov000_021bb33c` (`d/b33c/p5.cpp`, 19);
  - `ov010_0220c758` (`d/c758/s1.cpp`, 40: a `u8 *m` parameter and a `u16 t[1]` array local, both hacks; the clean `a.cpp` is 91 / 2);
  - `ov015_022113e8` (`d/13e8/d1.cpp`, 104 / 7; the permuter reached 36 only with junk parameters);
  - `ov015_0220b1b8` (`d/b1b8/b.cpp`, 100).

## A size-guarded fill splits the slot address across a call (overlays, round ovmid9)

One new match. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov006_021dacb0` (0x184, `SlotManager` create) | 24 / 7 | The inlined slot clear writes the name fill as `{ u32 n = sizeof(self->name); if (n != 0) { MI_CpuFill8(self->name, 0, n); } }`. The one-element `i_1[1]` counter from ovmid8 is no longer needed. New file `src/ov006/ov006_021dacb0.cpp`, with a local copy of the inline (`ov006_Slot_ClearGuarded`). |

- **What the guard changes.** In the target, the slot loop keeps only the offset `i * 0x4c` in a callee-saved register. It computes `p + off` once for the stores before the `MI_CpuFill8` call and again for the stores after it. With a plain call, MWCC CSEs `p + off` across the call and keeps the slot pointer in the callee-saved register instead. The `if` puts a block boundary around the call, so the two address computations stay separate. Every other form changed nothing: `p->slots[i].field` written out, a re-taken `&p->slots[i]` after the call, the sub-struct clears as separate inlines, a `goto` label after the call. `if (sizeof(...) != 0)` (a constant condition) and `if (self->name)` do not work either; only the `u32 n` local with `if (n != 0)` does.
- **The header inline cannot take the guard.** `func_ov006_021daea0` (already matched, same `ov006_SlotManager_Reset`) goes from 0 to 23 with the guarded fill, while `func_ov006_021dafb4` stays at 0. The game had two versions of the slot clear, so the guarded one is a local inline in the new file.
- **Try this when** the target recomputes `base + offset` after a call and keeps only the offset in a callee-saved register, while our build keeps the summed pointer.
- **Partial gains (drafts in `scratchpad/ovmid9/d/`):**
  - `ov000_021688c4`: 28 / 8 to 20 / 0 (`88c4/b1.cpp`) by writing `unk_21 = 0` right after `unk_20 = 0`. The target does the two flag stores before the alpha multiply. What is left is a three-way rotation: the target puts `a5` (`alpha & 0x1f`) in `sb` and the `Model_PlayPart` model and resource pointers in `r7`/`r8`; ours has `a5` in `r7`. `ppa.sh`, a local copy of `Model_PlayPart` in four forms, moving or removing `a5`, and 20 minutes of cxxperm (19,930 compiles) do not fix it.
  - `ov000_0214b1a0` (G3 quad draw, no earlier ovmid draft): 16 / 16 to 12 / 12 (`b1a0/c.cpp`). The third vertex is `0x08000800`, not `0x07ff0800` (the target computes it as `rsb r0, sb, #0x10000`). The scale's `y` is not masked with `0xff`. The vector commands copy `pos` and `scale` through `s32 x = p->x; s32 y = p->y; s32 z = p->z;` locals into an `s32 v[3]`. The target loads `y` and `z` together (`ldmib`), stores `z`, then loads `x`. An 8-byte copy of `y`/`z` (`*(s64 *)&v[1] = *(const s64 *)&p->y`) gives the `ldmib` but not the register order (16 / 8, `b1a0/k6/v006.cpp`). 72 declaration and store orders, struct copies, loops, two-level inlines, `ppa.sh` and 20 minutes of cxxperm with `--inlines` (29,040 compiles) stay at 12 or above.
- **No change:**
  - `ov000_02199554` / `02199180` (13 / 0, 14 / 0): the scratch-register rotation in the distance loop does not move with an early-return or store-both inline for the distance, `a` at function level, a `VecFx32` difference struct, an `s64` accumulator, a `dist` pointer, reloading `found[i]`, or an `out` reload. Putting the three arrays into one struct moves the registers, but in another wrong pattern (46). cxxperm with `--inlines` found nothing in 20 minutes (19,466 compiles).
  - `ov000_0218867c` (22 / 6): the target loads `data_02fe1534` at the store in case 1, after the loop. Ours loads it at the top of the case into `sb`, which shifts `arg` and `done` (`r6`/`r8` swapped). Writing the global directly, a found-`n`-th inline, `while`, `if`/`else` stores and a `goto` all hoist it too, and are worse (28 to 50). All 24 orders of the `kind`/`arg`/`one`/`done` declarations stay at 22.
  - `ov003_021ea588` (50 / 30): the target uses `sl` and gets all of `func_ov000_0219aae8`'s stack arguments into registers before it stores them. An inline wrapper for the call and all 120 orders of the `a`/`b`/`v.x`/`v.y`/`v.z` statements stay at 50 or worse.
  - `ov006_021e309c` (51 / 19): five forms of the `Caption_Draw` argument expressions (separate `dx`/`dy` locals, operand order, a cached anim pointer) compile the same.
- Tools are in `scratchpad/ovmid9/t/` (the ovmid8 tools pointed at this clone, plus `tdis9.py`).

No new matches. Every function below was redrafted from the target asm without opening the old draft. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Old draft | Fresh draft | What moved it |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ov000_02196258` (0x608) | — | 34 / 0 (`d/6258/best34.cpp`) | `s32 zero = 0;` local for the `adc rX, rY, r4` and the `umull`/`mla`/`mla` s64 multiply; scale loads written as `y = FX(sc->y); z = FX(sc->z); x = FX(sc->x);` then stores `x, y, z`; nibble update as `u8 val = 0xf; val \|= Get(row, idx) & 8; Set(row, idx, val);` |
| `ov003_021cd744` (0x1d8) | — | 28 / 0 (`d/d744/z1.cpp`) | sibling-style `(u16)(s16)self->unk_69` loop bound |
| `ov000_02165a20` (0x358) | 45 / 0 | 68 / 0 clean (`d/5a20/e1.cpp`); 45 / 0 with `ppa.sh` parameters | `head = tail = 0` (one chained assignment) fixes the store order |
| `ov002_021e0488` (0x23c) | 65 / 32 | 41 / 30 (`d/0488/r1.cpp`); cxxperm 37 / 28 | locals-as-parameters like the matched twin `ov002_021d8d00`, `n = 0` before `MTX_Copy43To44_`, `y` computed after the clamp |
| `ov000_0216715c` (0x490) | 135 / 89 | 147 / 45 (`d/715c/n1.cpp`) | endpoints kept pre-shifted (`<< 12`), lerp as `LO_FX_MUL(b - a, t) + a` |
| `ov000_021971c4` (0x2ec) | 87 / 6 | 85 / 4 (`d/71c4/a.cpp`) | `u32 none = 0xffff;` and `s32 zero = 0;` locals for the constants the target keeps in callee-saved registers |

- **Constants in callee-saved registers.** When the target keeps `0` or `0xffff` in `r4`–`r7` across calls (`mov r4, #0` in the prologue, then `str r4, [sp]`, `adc ..., r4`, `cmp r0, r5`), declare a named local (`s32 zero = 0;`, `u32 none = 0xffff;`) and use it at every site. MWCC then keeps it in one register instead of rematerialising it at each use.
- **Inline arguments appear to be evaluated right to left**, which matters when matching the load order at an inlined call site.
- **What is left (no source form moved it):**
  - 6258 and 71c4: in the inlined `GetNibble`, the target puts `mod = q + ror(t)` in `q`'s register, while ours lands in `t`'s. Every Get/Set spelling, `idx`/`row` type, the matched `Save_02197d80.c` inlines (`Obj_StoreValue` with a `value` parameter), O2/O3 pragmas and all compiler versions compile the same.
  - 71c4 also has a four-way rotation (target `self`=`r7`, `zero`=`r4`, `none`=`r5`, `tgt`=`r6`; ours `r4`/`r5`/`r6`/`r7`). All six declaration orders, `u8 *self` as the parameter, separate `tgt` assignment and `int`/`u16` constants do not change it; neither does `ppa.sh`.
  - d744: `self` is `r6` instead of `r4`. Inline parameter types, `continue` instead of a nested `if`, function-level loop locals, and moving the draws or the loop into inlines all stay at 28 / 0.
  - 0488: one extra 4-byte stack slot (the target frame is 0x78 with an unused slot at `sp+0x18`) and the spill-slot order.
- Drafts are in `scratchpad/ovmid10/d/`, and tools (`sc.py`, `sc.sh`, `tdis9.py`, `ppa.sh`) are in `scratchpad/ovmid10/t/`.
## Two cameras through one base pointer, case order, and inline accessors (ov000 0x021a0000+ and ov009–ov015, round ovhimid3)

- **`func_ov015_021fce94` (no source before; first draft 127 / 81, now 0).** This function sets up the two cameras of an `Ov015CamPair`. Write one `Ov015Cam_Init(Ov015Cam *c, s32 ay, s32 az)` inline and call it on `&self->cam0` and `&self->cam1`. The scheduler then interleaves the two cameras' stores the way the target does. If the cameras are reached through `FLD` offsets or through a second pointer, the stores come out one camera after the other. The fovy update inside the inline is the usual `(s64)deg * 0xb60b60b60b + 0x80000000000 >> 44` sin/cos lookup, and the `pos`, `at` and `up` vectors go through a `V3Set` inline.
- **`func_ov015_02200d50` (first draft 47 / 40, now 0).** Three things were needed:
  - The `switch` lists `case 3` before `case 2`. MWCC keeps source case order in the compare chain.
  - The BOOL chain uses separate flags (`BOOL c; BOOL b; BOOL a; a = FALSE; ok = TRUE; c = TRUE; b = TRUE;`), not one accumulator.
  - The first field is read through a one-line inline accessor (`Ov015Stat_GetUnk14(g)`). This renames a temporary the same way as the permuter's `(0, x)` comma trick, without the hack.
- **What helped on the near misses:**
  - An inline helper for repeated circle-point code stops the sin/cos table address from being hoisted, and the frame then matches (`ov010_02204030`).
  - Inline G3 command wrappers (`Cmd1`, `Vtx`, `VtxXY`, `Vec`) give stack temporaries the target's layout (`ov010_02200a00`).
  - For a table lookup with an unsigned bound, use `k < sizeof(table)` inside an inline; that gives `blo` (`ov010_0220441c`, 8 / 0).
  - Copy table entries through a `const` pointer to `tbl[i]` (`ov012_021fefc8`, 26 / 2).
- **What did not help:**
  - Passing an argument-order setter inline (`Set(s, x, y)`) instead of two stores does not change the `ldm` merge of `pos->x` and `pos->y` in `ov000_021c6d64`.
  - Neither do `y`-before-`x` statement orders or precomputed `scroll * 6` and `width / 2` locals.
  - In `ov000_021bd474` (2 / 0), only two `asr #31` sign extensions are swapped. Swapping the final updates, the declaration order, the `FxMul` form and the branch shape all leave it at 2 or make it worse.

## Split-then-modify locals fix register coalescing, and three codegen blockers that stay open (overlays, round shared1)

Five new matches. Each puzzle was first reproduced in a small standalone test file compiled with the project flags, and the source forms were then searched in that file or straight in the draft. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff. Drafts and test files are in `scratchpad/shared1/d/` and `scratchpad/shared1/p1`–`p5/`. `t/cc.py file [func]` compiles a test file and prints its disassembly, `t/ccq.py` is the same as a library, and `t/rsw.py file func mod spec.py` scores the text variants listed in a spec file.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_02196258` (new `Robo_02196258.cpp`) | 34 / 0 | `SetNibble` computes the shift in two steps: `s32 sh = idx % 8; u32 *p; sh *= 4; p = &...flags[row][idx / 8];`. `GetNibble` stays `(v >> ((idx % 8) * 4)) & 0xf`. |
| `func_ov000_021971c4` (new `Robo_021971c4.cpp`) | 85 / 4 | The same `SetNibble` (85 to 2; the four-way `self`/`zero`/`none`/`tgt` rotation went too). The last 2 came from a `FindActor(u16 id)` inline (`mgr = data_ov000_021ccd68; if (mgr == NULL) return NULL; return func_ov000_0218e640(mgr, id);`) in place of the `?:`. The inline's argument `self->0x90` is then loaded before the `mgr` NULL test, as in the target. |
| `func_ov003_021d6a3c` (new `ov003_021d6a3c.cpp`) | 16 / 3 | The same `SetNibble` and `GetNibble` pair as 6258. |
| `func_ov000_02199554` (appended to `Body_021992b4.c`) | 13 / 0 | The distance inline as `s32 x = a->x; s32 y = a->y; s32 z = a->z; x -= b->x; y -= b->y; z -= b->z;` instead of `s32 dx = a->x - b->x;`. That fixes the loop's scratch registers but rotates `n`/`q`/`out` (26 / 0). Declaring `s32 i;` before `s32 n;` fixes the rotation (0 / 0 in every order where `i` comes before `n`). |
| `func_ov000_02199180` (appended to `Body_02198c8c.c`) | 14 / 0 | The same two changes as 9554. |

- **Split-then-modify locals (the shared fix for puzzles 1 and 2).** When the target puts the result of `a op b` in the register of the other operand, or keeps a value in a register of its own where ours coalesces it, initialise a local from one operand and apply the operation as a separate statement (`s32 sh = idx % 8; sh *= 4;`, or `s32 x = a->x; x -= b->x;`). MWCC then gives the result a different live range, and the coalescing changes. In the standalone test the nibble register moved only with this form or with a whole-expression call (`Set(row, idx, 0xf | (Get(row, idx) & 8))`), and the second form changes the instruction count in the real function. In 6a3c the working pairs are a split `SetNibble` with a plain `GetNibble`, or a plain `SetNibble` with a split `GetNibble` (`s32 sh = idx; u32 w; sh %= 8; sh *= 4;`). Try both inlines.
- **Puzzle 3: `add rX, sp, #0` scheduled too early in `Ov012_Fx2` (still 2 / 2 in `fc688` and `fdfa0`; `ab7c` not retried).**
  - A standalone `Fx2` gives the target order when it is the first thing in its block. Put a call and a store in front of it in the same block (`p3/gen2.py`, context `c2`), and the `add` follows `mov r0, #0x68` before the copy stores, as in our build.
  - In every variant the `add` was scheduled right after `mov r0, #id`. The target's `add` fills the load slot after `ldrh z` instead, so the `&r` address is created later in the target's source.
  - None of these moved it in the test file or in fc688: `Rot3 r; r = *base;`, member-wise copies, a `u16 *` copy, `base[0]`, `const` temporary copy, `volatile` source, a copy inline, a one-element array, `r` as a caller local passed by pointer, `r` by value, a pointer local before or after the calls, the order of the `o`/`r` declarations, `id` typed `u16`/`s32`, and a returned versus discarded result (432 test variants and 16 in fc688). The member-wise, array and assignment forms stop keeping `&r` in a callee-saved register (47 / 34 and worse).
- **Puzzle 4: the `ov008_021ce518` switch root (still 1 / 1).** Once clusters are formed the target tree is ordinary: the elements are `{11}`, `{16,20}`, `{30}`, `{40}`, and the root is element `(4-1)/2`. So the open question is only how `{16,20}` became a cluster. `p4/sw.py` and `p4/sw2.py` add one or two extra labels with values 8–44 (pairs from 12–25). Each label goes to default, to its own empty `break`, or to one of the four real bodies (2,568 switches). No combination gives the target compares. An extra label that shares the 16/30 body (`case 18:`) removes its own `beq`, but the compare becomes `cmpne r0, #0x12`. The target has no conditional compare, so this is not the source either.
- **Puzzle 5: the 7-call block in `ov014_022153e0` (still 6 / 6).** The limit is not a fixed number of calls before a region. In a test file (`p5/reg.py`) with effects of three calls each, the region after the 7th call is left in source order. With N plain calls followed by one effect, the effect's first region is scheduled up to N = 4 and left in source order from N = 5. In the `53e0` shape (`p5/a7.cpp`), the region after the 4th call is already left in source order, and the region after the 6th call is scheduled again. Any real branch inside the block (for example a two-definition `id2`) splits it and restores the scheduling. That adds instructions the target does not have. Eleven other forms change nothing: block-scoped or array effects, effect, sound and flag inlines, a cached `pos` pointer, a chained call, and a cached sound global.
- `ppa`-style or permuter searches were not needed for the five matches. Every one came from the two source idioms above, plus the inline for `FindActor`.

## Sweeping the params + one-element-array lever over every module (round ppasweep1)

Two new matches. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov002_021dfcc0` (0x3fc) | 8 / 0 | One `ppa.sh` pass on the `permtool1` draft. The `mgr` global becomes an unused third parameter, copied at the top into `void *mgr_p[1] = {mgr};` and then overwritten with `data_02149ef8`. The `(0, ...)` comma on `unit` is still needed (14 without it). New file `src/ov002/ov002_021dfcc0.cpp`. |
| `func_ov013_021fb1b4` (0xb6c) | 26 / 0 | `ppa.sh` gave 23 / 0 (`prev` as a parameter, `cnt` as `u8 cnt_1[1]`). Two `ppaiter.sh` rounds then gave 2 / 2 (`pos`, `prev`, `cur` as parameters and `Ov013Vec v_1[1]`). The last swap, `mov r6, r4` scheduled after `sub r5, r1, #0x7800`, went away when the loop header was written `for (cnt_1[0] = 0, i = 0; ...)` instead of `i = 0, cnt_1[0] = 0`. New file `src/ov013/ov013_021fb1b4.cpp`. |

- **Candidates.** I took every unmatched function (not in ov004) whose best draft scored 10 or less masked in `permsweep2/best.txt` and `bigfresh7/best_scan*.txt`, and skipped the functions ovmid8 had already run. That left 92 functions with a raw score above 0. The list is in `scratchpad/ppasweep1/cand.txt`.
- **The lever only helps when the masked score is already 0.** Of the 27 masked-0 drafts, 3 improved (the two above, and `ov013_02206764`, 22 to 2 / 0). None of the 44 drafts with a masked score of 1 to 6 that the sweep reached improved. I stopped the run before the drafts with masked 6 to 10. K=2 (pairs of parameters) gave nothing that K=1 had missed, and it costs too much once a function has more than about 12 locals: `0204205c` ran 40 minutes without finishing. Run `ppaiter.sh` only on drafts that the first pass improved; on the others its first round just repeats the K=1 pass.
- **Comma order in a `for` initialiser is a scheduling lever.** `for (a = 0, b = 0; ...)` and `for (b = 0, a = 0; ...)` emit the same instructions in a different order. Try it whenever the only diff left is one swapped pair just before a loop.
- **Left over:** `ov013_02206764` is at 2 / 0 (`scratchpad/ppasweep1/d/6764/base.cpp`). The `t->pos.z` and `self->pos.z` loads in the distance check are swapped. Ten minutes of `cxxperm.py` (6943 compiles), an inline distance helper with either argument order, a separate load into a local and `ppaiter.sh` did not change it.
- **Tool fix:** `pp3.py` always wrote `.cpp` outputs, so `ppa.sh` skipped the parameter step for `.c` drafts. It now keeps the source's extension, and `ppa.sh` appends each result to `raw.part` as it runs, so a run cut off by a timeout keeps what it scored (`scratchpad/ppasweep1/t/`).

## A `volatile` pointer local reorders two loads without changing registers (round shared2)

One new match. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff. Drafts and test files are in `scratchpad/shared2/` (`best/`, `p1/` for the load-order test, `t/` for the tools).

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov013_02206764` (appended to `ov013_022065ac.cpp`) | 2 / 0 | Declare the target pointer `volatile Ov013Actor *t;`. The `ppasweep1` parameter `b` stays a trailing parameter; the one-element `a_1[1]` is not needed. |

- **What it fixes.** In `dz = self->pos.z - t->pos.z` the target loads `t->pos.z` first, but keeps the registers of the plain form (`sub r1, r2, r1`, result in `t`'s register). In a one-function test (`p1/gen.py`, `p1/gen2.py`), every non-volatile spelling loads the left operand first: operand swaps, `-t + self`, casts, pointer locals, struct copies, value or pointer inlines, `opt_propagation`/`opt_common_subs` off. The split form `s32 dz = t->pos.z; dz = self->pos.z - dz;` gives the target's load order, but the result then shares the register of `self->pos.z`, not `t->pos.z` (3 / 0). Reading `t` through a `volatile` pointer gives both the order and the registers. Volatile loads are emitted in the order the expression is evaluated, and the scheduler does not move the plain `self` load above them.
- **Try it when** the only diff is a pair of independent loads in the wrong order and the registers are already right. Cast the one access, `((volatile T *)p)->f`, to test it, then declare the pointer `volatile` if every access through it needs the same order. In `6764` only volatile on both `t->pos.z` and `t->pos.x` worked, which is the same as the `volatile` declaration.
- **Partial gains with the same lever:**
  - `ov003_021e83d0`: 6 / 5 to 3 / 3 (`best/e83d0_3_3.cpp`). With the `offset.x`/`offset.y` temporaries read through a `volatile Ov003VecC *`, both loads now come before the stores, in the target's registers. What is left is `add r4, sl, #0x100` and `mov sb, #0` (the loop's `self + 0x100` base and `i = 0`), which the target schedules after the `offset.z` load. All 720 orders of the six statements before the loop stay at 3 or worse.
  - `ov000_021c6d64`: 24 / 10 to 23 / 8 (`best/6d64_23_8.cpp`). Reading `pos->x` and `pos->y` through a volatile cast in the first block stops the `ldm` merge, but the target loads `y` before `x`, and the volatile loads keep source evaluation order (`x` first).
- **No change from `volatile`:** `ov002_021eca14` (the `u8` store still blocks the load, 5 / 5), `ov000_0218867c` (the `data_02fe1534` address is still loaded at the top of the case, 22 / 6 or worse), and the `add rX, sp, #0` blocker in `ov012_021fc688` (a volatile `self->rot[1]` read, volatile copy source and a volatile id all stay at 2 / 2).
- **The `add rX, sp, #0` blocker and `e83d0` look like one problem.** In both, an instruction with no inputs in the block (`&r`, `self + 0x100`, `i = 0`) that feeds a callee-saved register is scheduled right after the first loads in our build and in a later load-delay slot in the target. Since MWCC schedules after allocation and both builds have the same registers there, the codegen order of that instruction must differ. No source form found so far moves it.
