- **Register-only near misses retried without a match:** `ov003_021e1204` (2 / 0: the target loads `unk_16` into the dead `e` register; 25 spellings of the `>> 12` extraction, bitfields and split forms), `02056e74` (4 / 0: `asr` of the sign word into the source register; 20 forms of the fixed-point multiply, inline helpers in both argument orders), `0204205c` (8 / 0: the two `ldrh` of the attribute copy swap registers; member, struct, pointer and volatile forms).
## Two functions that need dsi/1.3, not dsi/1.3p1 (ov000 0x021a0000+ and ov009–ov015, round ovhimid4)

Two new matches, each confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build whose ROM check passes. Both drafts were already right; they only needed the compiler before dsi/1.3p1. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovhimid4/d/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_021b2b08` (0x240, the robo camera pitch clamp) | 17 / 6 (`ovhimid3/d/2b08/b.cpp`) | Unchanged source, compiled with dsi/1.3. New file `src/ov000/Cam_021b2b08.cpp`. |
| `func_ov011_021fce2c` (0x678, a tutorial step) | 24 / 0 (`bigsweep2/d/ov011_021fce2c/v_isc.cpp`) | Unchanged source, compiled with dsi/1.3. Appended to `src/ov011/ov011_021fc94c.cpp`, whose three matched functions also match under dsi/1.3. |

- **How to spot it.** dsi/1.2, 1.2p1, 1.2p2 and 1.3 fold a `u16` truncation into the operand of the next instruction: `lsl r1, r1, #16` then `rsb r2, r0, r1, lsr #16`, with a later `lsr r3, r1, #16` for the stored value. dsi/1.3p1 writes the truncated value first (`lsr r1, r1, #16`, then `sub r2, r1, r0`) and colours the registers around it differently. No source change reproduces the folded form under dsi/1.3p1: `u16`/`u32`/`s32` types for every operand, separate `t`/`d` locals, an `else` assignment, an inline clamp and writing the field directly all compile to the same code. When a near-miss has an `ALU ..., rX, lsr #16` in the target that our build splits, run `tools/match.py ... --all-compilers`.
- **How it is set up.** Each file has an entry in `tools/mwcc_versions.py`, which both `configure.py` and `match.py` read. (Round cc13 made dsi/1.3 the default and removed both entries; see the next section.)
- **The whole ROM also matches with dsi/1.3.** Setting `MWCC_VERSION = "dsi/1.3"` in `configure.py` gives 0 FAILED and every ROM check OK. So no matched file needs dsi/1.3p1, and the game itself may have been built with dsi/1.3 (the earlier note narrowed it to dsi/1.2 to dsi/1.3p1). The default is unchanged here.
- **Sweep.** I compiled the latest drafts of every unmatched function in scope (up to 48 each from earlier rounds' `d/` folders, 1,978 drafts) under both compilers. Only these two changed. A `rsb/sub/cmp ..., lsr #16` in the target also shows up in `ov000_021a7af0`, `ov000_021aa978`, `ov010_021fe2a4`, `ov011_021fb02c` and `ov011_021ff24c`, but their current drafts score the same under both compilers.
- **Near-misses without a match (best drafts in `ovhimid4/d/` or as listed):**
  - `ov000_021bd474` (2 / 0): the two `asr #31` stay swapped. `s64` `dx`/`dz`, `s64 k`, a `Sel` inline for the step, an `Upd(p, dx, dz, k)` inline, an array for `dx`/`dz` and dropping the redundant `k = 0x1000` all compile the same or worse.
  - `ov000_021bb33c` (2 / 2, `ovhimid3/d/b33c/q1.cpp`): the target stores a freshly built zero (`moveq r0, #0; streq r0, [r4, #0x10]`) on the `keep == 0` path. `self->fx = NULL`, a `Clr` inline, `(Fx33c *)FALSE`, `self->fx = fx = NULL` and an `s32 k` all store the hoisted zero in `r7` (3 / 2). A `BOOL k = keep ? TRUE : FALSE` gives the `moveq` but keeps the `movne`/`cmp` (9 / 9). cxxperm `--inlines` ran 16,445 compiles and found nothing.
  - `ov010_0220441c` (8 / 0): `r1`/`r2` swapped in the `(u64)1 << st` OR. `1ULL`, a `u64 bit` local, a `Bit(n)` inline, a pointer to the field, a struct with a `u64 flags` member, `(s32)st` and all six orders of the three statements around it compile the same or worse.
  - `ov015_021ffb90` (7 / 4): the target loads `d->x`, `t->x`, `d->z`, `t->z` from two base registers. 48 forms of the two pointers (`Vec *`, `const Vec *`, `s32 *`, `u8 *` with `FLD`, both declaration orders, through `d0`/`q0` locals), a `VEC_Subtract` inline, `dx`/`dz` locals and look-at inlines stay at 7 / 4 or worse. The `FLD` forms (`ovhimid4/d/fb90/a.cpp`, 8 / 4) remove the stray `add r0, r2, #0x114` but load `self->unk_10` first.
  - `ov012_021ffd34` (23 / 0) and `ov012_021fefc8` (26 / 2) are the same case-2 block: the target keeps the table index in a callee-saved register (`sb`/`sl`) and the `0xc` stride in `r0`. Moving `idx` to function scope, `u32`/`s32` types and reusing it for the case-`0xa` byte do not change it.
  - `ov015_02201344` (0x2b0, no draft before; first draft 157 / 46, now 154 / 25, `ovhimid4/d/1344/c.cpp`). This is the stage set-up with three model/animation pairs. An `Ov015MdlSet { vt; Ov015MdlPair p[3]; }` struct with the name copies written out against `s->p[i].mdl` gives the target's `o + const` addressing. With a `SetMdl(m, ...)` inline the compiler shares `o + 0x44` across the third pair instead. What is left: the target loads each pair's directory and model-name strings into `r4`/`r5` before the first copy, and the 0x30 object and the two 0x160 objects use swapped registers.
  - `ov014_02205634` (11 to 3 / 3, `ovhimid4/d/5634/b3.cpp`, clean): the target-distance block is ordered by hand. Declare `dz`/`dx` without initialisers, then write `self->unk_84.z = gz;`, the two differences, `sq = dx * dx; sq += dz * dz;`, `self->unk_84.y = gy + 0x1000;`, the two sqrt register writes, and only then `self->unk_84.x = gx;`. MWCC keeps the stores roughly in source order around the multiplies. A grid over all store, `SQRTCNT` and `SQRT_PARAM` orders (60 + 72 + 486 forms), an `Ov014_VecSet` inline (6) and struct copies of `g->pos` (17 or worse) found nothing better. What is left is the `z` store, which the target places after the sqrt base load. cxxperm (26,400 compiles) stays at 3.
- `ov010_02200a00` and `ov011_021fd638` hit the shared `add rX, sp, #0` pattern.

## An automated `volatile` sweep over the near-miss drafts (round vol1)

Two new matches. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff. Tools and results are in `scratchpad/vol1/` (`t/`, `r/<func>/`).

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov009_0220b8a0` (appended to `ov009_0220b70c.cpp`) | 6 / 0 | `Ov009_FxMul(*(volatile s32 *)&v.y + 0x1800 - self->pos.y, 0x19a)`: the stack copy `v.y` is read back after `func_ov000_0219a2f4(&v, ...)`. The volatile read gives the target's load order and register allocation. |
| `func_ov011_021fff38` (appended to `ov011_021ffb58.cpp`) | 6 / 2 | `func_02066900((const char *)((volatile s32 *)D58)[2], (const char *)((volatile s32 *)D58)[1], ...)`: both arguments of the call are read as `volatile s32`. Only the pair works; neither read alone helps. |

- **The tools.** `t/volsweep.py draft func module outdir` puts `#define VOLR(e) (*(volatile __typeof__(e) *)&(e))` at the top of the draft. For each member, array or `FLD` access in the function, and for each `data_` global, it tries `VOLR(...)` on that one access. It also tries `volatile` on each pointer declaration (local or parameter). It keeps the best change and repeats (greedy, up to four rounds). `t/volpair.py` tries every pair of `VOLR` wraps, for misses that need two volatile reads. MWCC accepts `__typeof__` in both C and C++. In the final source, write the `VOLR` as a plain cast.
- **The sweep.** 130 unmatched drafts with a masked score of 10 or less (from `permsweep2`, `bigfresh7` and `ppasweep1`; ov004 skipped) got the single-wrap sweep. The 41 drafts with masked 1–6 and raw 12 or less also got the pair sweep (35 or 22 wraps per draft). Besides the two matches, these drafts improved:
  - `func_ov000_021c4460`: 10 / 0 to 5 / 2 (`r/func_ov000_021c4460/best.cpp`). A volatile `self->unk_e4` read in the `if` fixes the loop registers. But it reloads the callback for the call, and the target loads it once into the dead `i` register. A callback local in every declaration position, `if ((cb = ...) != NULL)` and reusing `i`/`idx` all go back to 10 / 0. 9400 `cxxperm` iterations on the 5 / 2 draft found nothing.
  - `func_ov014_0220f3f0`: 40 / 10 to 12 / 10 (`r/func_ov014_0220f3f0/best.cpp`). The three inlined axis blocks read `ext[1]` and `ext[2]` through `volatile`. What is left is `&acc` held in `r6` in two of the blocks (the target is 2 instructions longer). 11000 `cxxperm` iterations found nothing.
  - `func_ov006_021e2490`: 17 / 0 to 13 / 0 (volatile `m->unk14`). `func_ov015_0220cbe8`: 6 / 6 to 5 / 5 (volatile `v2.y`). The rest of `cbe8` is the `self + 0x168` base register.
  - `func_ov000_021c6d64`: the `parsweep2` draft `sbase.c` (11 / 6) is better than the round shared2 one. Writing the second block's `x` as `(pos->x >> 12) + self->unk_b4 / 2 + ...` makes it 11 / 4 (`w/6d64b.c`). All 72 term orders and groupings of that sum were tried.
- **No change on register-only misses.** Of the 47 drafts at masked 0, only `0b8a0` moved. Most masked-0 misses are allocation swaps that load order does not reach.
- **A volatile member of pointer type is not the same as a volatile `s32` read.** In `021fff38`, `((volatile Ov011FxDesc *)&g)->arc` (a `const char *` member) gave 9 diffs, while `((volatile s32 *)&g)[2]` matched. With the pointer-typed member, the second argument was loaded first. When a volatile cast works in the `VOLR` form but not as a struct member, cast to the scalar type of the original expression.
- **`e83d0` (3 / 3) is unchanged.** Making `offset.z` volatile too, a `const volatile` pointer to the whole offset, or putting the `z` copy inside the block all give 3 / 3 or worse.
## dsi/1.3 is now the default compiler (round cc13)

- **The switch.** `MWCC_VERSION` in `tools/configure.py` is now `dsi/1.3` (mwccarm 4.0 build 1034, `mwcc_40_1034` on decomp.me), and `tools/match.py` and `tools/permuter/cxxperm.py` default to it. The two per-file `dsi/1.3` entries for `src/ov000/Cam_021b2b08.cpp` and `src/ov011/ov011_021fc94c.cpp` are gone from `tools/mwcc_versions.py`, which now lists only the two NitroSystem files that need `dsi/1.1`. `tools/permuter/cc.sh` and `tools/asm2mw/asm2mw.py`, which named the compiler directly, also use `dsi/1.3` now. The full build gives 0 FAILED with all 19 module checks OK, and no matched file needed `dsi/1.3p1`. The linker also comes from `dsi/1.3` now, and its output is identical. To check a draft against the old compiler, pass `--compiler dsi/1.3p1`, or use `--all-compilers`.
- **Why.** dsi/1.2 to dsi/1.3 fold a `u16` truncation into the next instruction's operand (see the previous section), and dsi/1.3p1 does not. With the stack-frame evidence, the game was built with something from dsi/1.2 to dsi/1.3. Every function matched so far also matches under dsi/1.3.
- **New match: `func_0206f6d4`** (0xb4, item lookup by part ID). The draft `mainmid4/d/f6d4/u16a.cpp` scored 14 under dsi/1.3p1 and matches under dsi/1.2 to dsi/1.3, unchanged. It is appended to `src/Main/Lang/ItemGive.cpp`, whose `.text` range now ends at `0x0206f788`.
- **Sweep.** Every unmatched function in main, ITCM, DTCM, ov000 below `0x021a0000` and ov001–ov008 (ov004 skipped, `__sinit_*` left out): 209 functions, 123 of them with drafts in the scratchpad. I compiled up to 11 drafts per function (the best-scored ones from earlier rounds' `best*`/`res.txt` lists plus the newest), and 20 more for each of the 35 whose target has an ALU instruction with a `, lsr #16` operand. That came to 1,979 drafts, each under dsi/1.3, and also under dsi/1.3p1 when it scored 40 or less. Only `0206f6d4` scored differently. Lists and logs are in the scratchpad under `cc13/` (`unm.txt`, `cands*.txt`, `vsweep*.log`).
- **The five `lsr #16` functions from round ovhimid4.** Their best drafts compile to the same code under both compilers. In `ov011_021fb02c`, `ov010_021fe2a4`, `ov011_021ff24c` and `ov000_021a7af0` the `cmp rX, rY, lsr #16` is a `u16` loop counter compared against its bound, which dsi/1.3p1 also folds. Only `ov000_021aa978` has the real `rsb ..., lsr #16` fold, and the drafts already reproduce it.
  - `ov011_021fb02c` stays at 6 / 0 (`bigfresh3/d/xb02c/pp/p_a_i.cpp`): `j` and `dz` have swapped registers (`r5`/`r8`). cxxperm under dsi/1.3 ran 20 minutes without an improvement.
  - `ov000_021aa978` went from 34 / 19 to 59 / 10 (`cc13/pa/a978_it1/base.cpp.pa/one_sub.cpp`). Moving `hit = FALSE;` after `eff = 0;` (`cc13/d/a978/v1.cpp`, 143 / 10) fixes the switch head: the target's `addls pc, pc, r1, lsl #2` jump table and `mov r7, r6` (`eff` copied from `hit`). Then `u8 *sub_1[1]` cuts the raw score to 59. What is left: the target builds the constant `1` once at entry (`mov sl, #1`). It uses it for the `1` argument of case 2's `func_ov000_02191418` and for that case's `eff = 1`, and so takes one more callee-saved register. Ours builds the `1` inside case 2. A `one` local (`s32`/`BOOL`/`u32`, set at the top, after `sub`, or before the `switch`) is constant-propagated away. So are the eight types tried for `hit` and six for `eff`, and the statement orders tried around the case-2 call.
  - `ov010_021fe2a4` (25 / 15, `big11/d/e2a4_af0.cpp`) and `ov011_021ff24c` (96 / 10, `big13/best/f24c_s10.cpp`): same code under both compilers. The blockers are the ones described in earlier rounds.

## Null-checked string locals, a split subtraction from the right operand, and the `NNS_G3dGlb` fields in a projection (ov000 0x021a0000+ and ov009–ov015, round ovhimid5)

Two new matches, each confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build whose ROM checks pass. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovhimid5/d/<tag>/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov015_02201344` (0x2b0, stage set-up with three model/animation pairs; new file `src/ov015/ov015_02201344.cpp`) | 154 / 25 (`ovhimid4/d/1344/c.cpp`) | Each pair is written like the matched sibling `func_ov015_02209364`: `{ const char *b = name; const char *a = dir; if (a) { copy dir, copy name, flags \|= 2; { const char *c = anim; if (c) { copy dir, copy anim, flags \|= 4; } } } } unk_00 = 0;`. The fields are written out against `s->p[i].mdl` (no pointer inline). |
| `func_ov015_021ffb90` (0x244; appended to `ov015_021ff968.cpp`) | 7 / 4 (`ovhimid4/d/fb90/base.cpp`) | The look-at differences as `s32 dx = d->x; s32 dz = d->z; dx = t->x - dx; dz = t->z - dz;`, with `d` declared before `t`. |

- **A null-checked local turns a string constant into a register value.** MWCC propagates a string address (or any global address) into each use, so a constant passed to an inline or held in a plain local is reloaded from the pool right before each call. When the target loads two string addresses into callee-saved registers before the first `STD_CopyString` and reuses them, assign them to locals and test one of them (`if (a)`). The test is folded away (the address is never NULL), but the locals stay real values. The declaration order sets the registers (`b` before `a` here gave `a` in `r4` and `b` in `r5`; the other order is 101 / 0). The nested `const char *c = ...; if (c)` places the third string's load after the second copy, where the target has it. With the inner block but no `if` it is 6 / 5, and with `c` declared at the top of the pair it is 5 / 4.
- **Split from the right operand.** Round shared1's split form `s32 x = a; x -= b;` keeps `x` in `a`'s register. The other split, `s32 x = b; x = a - x;`, loads `b` first and keeps the result in `b`'s register. In `021ffb90` the target loads `d->x`, `t->x`, `d->z`, `t->z` and computes into `t`'s registers. This form matched outright. All 16 `volatile` combinations of the four reads, with plain or `-=` splits, stay at 7 / 2 or worse (`d/fb90/g/`, 288 variants). Try both split directions on any subtraction whose operand loads are swapped.
- **`func_ov000_021c14f4` (39 / 11 to 64 / 6, `d/14f4/g1.cpp`).** The projection block reads `NNS_G3dGlb.projMtx` and `NNS_G3dGlb.cameraMtx` (the round mainmid3 struct). Writing `proj = (u8 *)&NNS_G3dGlb.projMtx; MTX_MultVec43(&v, &NNS_G3dGlb.cameraMtx, &out);` gives the target's `ldr r4, =projMtx` before the call, with the pointer kept across it. With `data_02092800`, MWCC reloads it after the call. What is left:
  - a whole-function rotation (`self` is `r4` where the target has `r6`, and the zero is `r6` where the target has `r5`);
  - the target's `add r1, r1, #0x1ec` for `&data_02097d48->cam`, which we fold into the load offsets. A local, a cast, a struct local and a `volatile` source all fold the same way;
  - `q - 0x1000` in `r4` (the register that held `0x800`), which is the same 3 / 0 diff as `func_ov000_021be5dc` (`splitsweep1/sw/func_ov000_021be5dc_6.cpp`). Eleven split and operand forms of `ToDepth` and its argument compile the same.
- **No change (best drafts as listed):**
  - `ov014_02205634` (3 / 3), `ov015_0220cbe8` (5 / 5): for `cbe8`, `p + 0x168` held as a pointer local, a struct member, an inline argument, split forms and an offset local are all folded into the `0x580` loads. The target keeps `add r7, r4, #0x168` across the call.
  - `ov015_02211b2c` (4 / 4 to 2 / 2, `d/1b2c/a.cpp`): write the `b5 = 0` bitfield store before `b8 = 1`. What is left is `add r0, r6, #0x14c`, which our build schedules before the `ldr` of `data_ov015_02213368`. This is the "no-input instruction scheduled early" pattern from round shared2. A `const` table, a `volatile` read, locals and 48 statement orders do not move it.
  - `ov009_021ff908`: a clean redraft (`d/f908/b2.cpp`, 38 / 4) replaces the `parsweep2` junk-parameter draft, which scores 38 / 15 under dsi/1.3. The target computes `p.y + 0x3000 - pos.y` into the zero register `r5`, so the zero is a variable coalesced with `d`. Every form of a `d = 0` local is constant-propagated away, and `ppa.sh` found nothing.
  - `ov009_0220acb8` (15 / 11): the argument registers for `func_ov000_0219aae8` (`y + 0x1333` into `lr`). Eight inline and argument forms compile to the same code.
  - `ov015_0220b1b8` (20 / 4): the target builds the zero used by the `abs > 0x4cd` multiply as a fresh `mov r0, #0`, and builds the zero for the later blocks (`r8`) after `cmp r4, #0x99a`. We share one zero register from the first block. Four forms of the multiply do not change it.
  - `ov010_021ffd0c` (15 / 3), `ov010_02204030` (22 / 10; 240 statement orders of the first point stay at 19 or worse).
## SDK and NitroSystem functions redrafted from the SDK source (main, round mainmid5)

Three new matches. Drafts and tools are in the scratchpad under `mainmid5/` (`d/<tag>/`, `t/`, `perm/`).

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `CARDi_RequestStreamCommandCore` (`0200ca48`, 0x198) | 14 / 0 (permuter draft in `nnslib/pm/p_cb`) | The SDK body, but with every top-level local declared without an initialiser, in the order `buf, req_type, req_mode, retry_count, size`, followed by the assignments in the SDK order (`req_type`, `req_mode`, `retry_count`, `size`). Prepended to `libs/nitro/card/card_backup.c`, whose `.text` range now starts at `0x0200ca48`. |
| `getMdlRot_` (`0202b048`, 0x180) | 14 / 0 | The SDK body with `A`, `B` and `pNd` as trailing parameters (`void getMdlRot_(NNSG3dJntAnmResult *pResult, fx32 A, fx32 B, const NNSG3dResNodeData *pNd)`), and the node lookup stored through a non-`const` block temporary: `{ NNSG3dResNodeData *nd = NNS_G3dGetNodeDataByIdx(...); pNd = nd; }`. Appended to `libs/nnsys/g3d/nsbca.c` (now ends at `0x0202b1c8`); `nsbca_2.c` still calls it with one argument. |
| `func_0204205c` (`0204205c`, 0x5cc, OAM cell copy) | 8 / 0 | In both cell loops, read `attr0` into a block-scoped temporary before `fmt`, then store `attr1`, `attr2` and the temporary: `u16 attr0 = cell->attrs[i].attr0; fmt = img->fmt; a.attr1 = ...; a.attr2 = ...; a.attr0 = attr0;`. Appended to `src/Main/Gfx/TexObj_2.cpp`, whose `.text` range now ends at `0x02042628`. |

- **Initialised declarations are a lever of their own.** In `CARDi_RequestStreamCommandCore` the three SDK locals are `const int req_type = p->req_type;` and so on. All 120 orders of those initialised declarations stay at 9 or worse, because each order also moves the load. Declaring the locals bare and assigning them afterwards decouples the two: the loads stay in the target's order, and the declaration order alone picks the callee-saved registers (5 of 240 orders give 0, `t/` grid in `d/cb/dq/`). Removing `const` from the initialised form, by contrast, made it worse (21). Try this whenever the target's loads are in source order but the registers of those locals rotate.
- **A temporary of a different type splits a live range.** In `getMdlRot_`, neither the parameters alone nor the temporary alone changes anything (both 14, with every declaration order, statement order, one-element array and comma form). Together they give 0. The permuter found it as a `__typeof__` temporary whose type was the inline's non-`const` return type. When a cxxperm result matches, cut it down one rewrite at a time (`d/rot/m*/`): here only the temporary and the three parameters were needed; the arrays and extra parameters it also added were noise.
- **A temporary pins one load, the next statement pins the next.** Earlier rounds tried temporaries for the three OAM attributes of `func_0204205c` in every load, store and declaration order and stayed at 8. What they missed was the unrelated `fmt = img->fmt;` read: the target loads `attr0`, then `fmt`, then `attr1`/`attr2`. Only a temporary for `attr0` placed before the `fmt` read gives that order. cxxperm found it (3,300 compiles, `perm/205c/`), and the cut-down was done by hand (`d/205c/min/`). When temporaries alone do not move a load, look at the statements next to them.
- **NitroSystem code may want `dsi/1.1`.** `getScaleData_` (`0202b7c0`) is 7 / 2 under `dsi/1.1` and 15 or worse under `dsi/1.3` (`d/sc/e0.c`, the mainlow14 draft). Under `dsi/1.3` the fx32 element reads fold the index into the load (`ldr rX, [r1, r2, lsl #3]`), while the target and `dsi/1.1` compute the element address first (`add ip, r1, r2, lsl #3; ldr rX, [ip]`). Every function of `nsbca_3.c`, which would take it, also matches under `dsi/1.1`, so the file can be switched in `tools/mwcc_versions.py` once the last 7 diffs close. They are the 3:1 fx16 interpolation block (the target computes both element addresses before the first `ldrsh` and loads `s` into a fresh register) and the operand order of one `adds` in the fx32 block. Locals, `* 3`, struct and `fx16 *` indexing, volatile reads (`volsweep.py`), locals as parameters (pp3 K=2), 9,500 cxxperm compiles and all 1,440 bare-declaration orders of the six top locals with two assignment orders (`d/sc/dq/`, best 7 / 2) did not move it.
- **`NextAction` (`02008a20`) needs one more stack word.** The mainsm draft (`d/na/a.c`, with today's symbol names) is right except the frame: the target has `sub sp, #0xa0` (pushing `r3`), ours `#0x9c`, and every slot above `sp+0x2c` is 4 bytes higher. The hole is at `sp+0x30`, between the locals of action 16 (`sp+0x24..0x2c`) and action 15 (`sp+0x34..0x3c`). An unused or address-taken dummy scalar in the action-15 inline is removed (no change). A struct local for action 15 (`{ u8 action; u32 specs; u32 pcOffset; s32 cinfoRef; }`) gives the right frame size but MWCC places aggregates above all scalar locals; making every action's locals a struct sorts them by size instead (48). Case 14 jumps to the default return.
- **No change:** `func_0200d36c` (5 / 0: `word`/`data` in `r1`/`r0`, target `r3`/`r1`) with 184 pragma combinations at levels 1 to 4, function-scope or split `word`/`data`, `word++` in the store, and 400 random declaration orders of the whole function with every local declared bare. `02056e74` (4 / 0) with 11 more spellings of the `-0x80000` multiply. `func_0203b4ac` (56 / 14, `d/b4ac/ip/030.cpp`, the `h` load moved into the init block) and `func_02068e68` (26 / 1) had 20-minute cxxperm runs (`perm/b4ac`, `perm/8e68`) with no match.
- **Tools** (`scratchpad/mainmid5/t/`): `sc.py` now looks up the address of non-`func_` names in `symbols.txt` and takes `MWCC=<compiler>`. `par.sh func mod files...` scores drafts 8 at a time; `pard.sh func mod dir files...` copies each into `dir` first, for drafts that include a private header (`card_private.h`, `ExceptionHandler.h`). `vr.py base func mod spec.py outdir` scores the text replacements listed in a spec file (`VRDIR=dir` to use `pard.sh`). `cxxperm.py` needs the `{` on the function's definition line; a `setsid` without `-w` returns at once in a background shell, so wrap background permuter runs in `setsid -w` or `... & wait`.

## Undoing type hacks before the parameter lever, and a load that must cross a merged bitfield store (ov000 0x021a0000+ and ov009–ov015, round ovhimid6)

One new match, confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build whose ROM checks pass. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovhimid6/d/<tag>/`. Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_021c7038` (0x3d4, a gauge draw with five optional layers; new file `src/ov000/Gauge_021c7038.cpp`) | 56 / 12 (`ovmid2/b/021c7038/base.cpp`) | Each step came from a small generated grid (`d/7038/`). (1) `s16 w; long h;` to `s32` (70 / 12). The target computes `w = unk_50 << 12` as a full word (`lsl r7, r1, #0xc`). (2) `h` into the unused fourth parameter and `w` as a new trailing parameter (22 / 7). (3) The last block's `s16 a` to `s32`, as the target's `asrs r5, r1, #0xc` shows (16 / 0). (4) The loop's `i` and `step` as two more trailing parameters, `(..., s32 scale, s32 w, s32 i, s32 step)` (9 / 0). (5) `a` in the unused second parameter (0). |

- **Fix wrong types before you sweep registers.** The old draft's `s16 w` had the shape of a register-coloring trick, and every earlier sweep started from it. Where the target never narrows a value (no `lsl #16; asr #16` pair), declare it `s32` first, then use the parameter lever.
- **Two at once, and the unused parameters.** `ppa.sh` only tries one local at a time, and the loop fix here needed `i` and `step` together. The last step reuses a parameter the function already has and never reads, which no sweep tries. `ovhimid6/t/k2.sh src func mod tag` runs `pp3.py` with `K=2` and scores every result in parallel (about 15 s for 50 variants).

- **`func_ov013_021fee10` (0x3dc, the actor set-up after `func_ov013_021fed64`): 44 / 22 to 0 / 0 by seqdiff, but one pool word too many (`d/fee10/v5.cpp`).** The fresh draft appends to `ov013_021fea8c.cpp` and uses the file's idioms. What fixed the structure:
  - the area object's fields in an inline `Ov013Area_Set(ar, self->id)`. The argument is read before the inline body, so `ldrh [self, #0xc]` comes before the bitfield `strb`, as in the target;
  - the position as three `s32` locals read once (`ar->pos.x = x = FLD(s32, data_ov013_0220aedc, 0x4c);` and so on), with `o2`'s `x` read again from the table (the target reloads only `x` there) and `o3` using the locals;
  - `o3`'s `b0 = 0; b1 = 0; ... b0 = 1;` as plain bitfield stores, with nothing that reads memory between them. MWCC then keeps the byte in a register and emits one `ldrb` and one `strb`.
- **The blocker is the `anim` load in `o3`.** The target loads `data_ov013_0220aedc[4]` before the final `strb` of `o3`'s flags, and still emits only one `strb`. In our builds:
  - a load placed in the source between the bitfield stores, `const` or not, keeps the earlier `strb` (two stores, 10 / 10 or worse);
  - a load placed after `o3->b0 = 1` comes after the `strb` when the table is non-`const` (110 / 10, `v2.cpp`), because the scheduler won't move a load above a byte store to an unknown pointer;
  - with the table read through `namespace Kee10 { extern "C" const u8 data_ov013_0220aedc[]; }` for that one load, the scheduler hoists it above the `strb`, and every instruction matches (`v5.cpp`). But the namespace declaration gets its own pool entry, so the pool has 14 words where the target has 13.
  - Reading all of the function's table accesses through the `const` declaration gives one pool entry (18 / 16, `v6.cpp`). But then the area block's position loads and the table address are scheduled before the constant stores, where the target has them after. Volatile reads of `x`/`y`/`z` (15 combinations), a pointer local, inline accessors, block wrappers (`do {} while (0)`, `for (;;) break`, labels) and eight `opt_*`/`peephole` pragmas don't move them. Neither do a `const` member in a struct-typed declaration, a cast to `const` (the object's own declaration decides), or a byte local for the flags (the masks fold into `and #0xfc`). A 15-minute `cxxperm` run on `v6.cpp` (15,569 compiles) took its own score from 43 to 33.
  - Only a `const` **array** declaration makes loads movable. With `extern const Prm data_ov013_0220aedc;` (a struct object, `d/fee10/st_*.cpp`), member loads stay in place but are CSE'd across calls (the `o2` reload of `x` disappears), and `FLD` casts on the struct's address behave exactly like the non-`const` table. A `mutable` member, a `const` array member inside the struct, and a `const` array of structs (`ca_*.cpp`) don't give the mixed behaviour either. A block-scope `extern const u8 data_ov013_0220aedc[];` is a redeclaration error. All `dsi/*`, `1.2/*` and `2.0/*` compilers give the same counts.
  - The table is in `.rodata`, so the original declaration was probably `const`. Some other form of the area block must keep its loads late. Try that next, starting from `v6.cpp`.
- **`q - 0x1000` into a fresh register (`func_ov000_021be5dc`, 3 / 0; also in `021c14f4`).** Twenty-eight more forms of the last step (`d/e5dc/g_*.cpp`, `h_*.cpp`, `vol_*.cpp`) all give the same code: `-=` and right-operand splits, `u32`, `(0x2000, q)` operand order, the result in `w`/`q`/`m`, a one-element array, `s64` products, `ToDepth2`/`ToDepth3`, and `volatile` table reads. The target writes `z` into the register that held the hoisted `0x800` (`r4`), and we write it in place.
- **No change:** `ov015_022113e8` (90 / 6): 240 random orders of the ten locals stay at 83 / 6 or worse. `ov009_021ff908` (38 / 4): every order of `v`/`p`/`d`, `d` and `&p` as parameters, a `&p` pointer local, a chained zero store and a one-element `d` stay at 38 / 4 or worse. `ov010_0220640c` (6 / 2): the target hoists the `0` argument of `func_0203cb78` above the two `ldrh`. Eleven forms of the `+= (u16)(... + 0x7fff)` statement compile the same (`d/640c/v_*.cpp`).
- **Tool:** `ovhimid6/t/rperm.py src func mod outdir N "decl1|decl2|..."` scores random (or, for up to 6 lines, all) orders of the given declaration lines in parallel with `sc.py`.
- **Two-local parameter sweep over every near draft.** `k2.sh` ran on the best draft of 37 unmatched functions in scope, from 4 to 436 variants each. None improved the masked score. Two improved only the raw score: `ov011_021fd638` went from 55 / 8 to 50 / 8 (`k2/ov011_021fd638/pp/p_ang_next.cpp`), and `ov015_022113e8` went from 90 / 6 to 65 / 6 (`k2/ov015_022113e8/pp/p_id_start.cpp`). The 7038 result needed the type fix first, so on an old draft the parameter lever mostly reproduces what earlier sweeps already tried.
- **Read `match.py` columns the right way round.** The left column is the draft and the right column is the target. To check, edit the draft and see which column changes.
- **`ov010_02200a00` (2 / 2, `ovhimid1/pr/0a00/g_97.cpp`).** The only real diff is where `mov r8, #0` goes. The target materialises the shared zero first, before the `ldr` of `NNS_G3dGlb` and the `bic ..., #0xa4` flag clear, and stores the three zeros of `v` after the flag store. We materialise it just before the stores. None of these moved it (`d/0a00/`):
  - plain, chained or split `v.x = 0` stores;
  - a `zero` local assigned before or after the clear, or in a block;
  - an `Ov010_SetBaseRot` inline;
  - `s32`/`u32` flag types, a `volatile` flag, a pointer to `NNS_G3dGlb`;
  - the `VecSet` placed earlier;
  - 200 declaration orders.

  `Ov010Vec v = {0, 0, 0};` declared after the clear schedules the zero exactly as the target does (`d/0a00/o.cpp`, `q.cpp`). But it moves `v` to its own stack slot, and the `[r4]` stores replace `[sp, #0x80]`.
- **`ov010_021fbda0` (28 / 20, `bigsweep1/d/ov010_021fbda0/base.cpp`).** In case `0x15` the target loads `x` and `y` of `data_ov010_0220fa2c + 0x38`, computes and stores both, and only then loads `z`. We load all three first. These compile the same or worse (`d/bda0/`):
  - a `const` table;
  - pointer or `s32 *` destinations, `FLD` on `&dv`;
  - three locals, an `Ov010Vec` source pointer;
  - inline `VecScale` helpers in three argument orders, a `VEC_Set` inline, a loop, the explicit macro form;
  - 120 orders of the five vector locals.
- **No change:** `ov000_021c3158` (4 / 0): `res_0` in the `t` parameter or at case or function scope, `name` hoisted or passed to the inline. `ov010_0220441c` (8 / 0): `1ll`, `(s64)1`, `(u32)n`, a `u64 one` local, a `u32` shift local, a `u64 b` temporary.
## Hand-written SDK asm with its constants in front, and switch clustering (main, round mainmid6)

One new match, confirmed with a full `ninja` build (0 FAILED, every ROM check OK). Drafts and tools are in the scratchpad under `mainmid6/` (`d/<tag>/`, `t/`, `perm/`).

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `MATHi_SHA1ProcessBlock` (`0201609c`, 0x31c) | gap | Hand-written ARM in the TwlSDK. New file `libs/nitro/math/math_sha1_arm.c`, made with `tools/asm2mw/mkfile.sh ... main 0201608c 020163b8 --split MATHi_SHA1K@0201608c --data 0201608c-0201609c`. The four SHA-1 round constants sit just before the function and are read with `ldr rX, [pc, #-N]`, so they are a separate `asm` block `MATHi_SHA1K` (four `dcd`s, kept by `#pragma force_active on`, listed as `kind:data(any)` in `symbols.txt`). `func_020163b8` (a TWL-only caller) stays in the gap after it, so that gap still has code. |

- **Spotting SDK asm.** `push` including `ip`, `ldr rX, [pc, #-N]` (a pool *before* the function) and a hand-scheduled `ldm`/`stm` byte-swap loop mean the function was written in asm. `asm2mw` handles the pool in front when the range starts at the pool (`--split NAME@ADDR --data A-B`).
- **The other hand-written main asm stays in gaps.** `func_02075810` and `func_02075d4c`/`0207670c` sit between `.text` rodata tables, and `func_02076f3c`/`02077118` are followed by rodata up to the end of `.text`. Claiming them leaves gaps with only data, which objdiff cannot report (see round mainbig5).
- **How MWCC builds a `switch` tree (`func_02015510`, `GX_SetBankForSubBG`, still 1 diff).** Before building the binary tree it groups the sorted case values into dense clusters: a run of `n` cases with `max - min < 2n` is one cluster (`{1, 4}` and `{0, 4, 5}` are clusters, `{0, 4}` is not). The pivot is the middle cluster; a cluster is lowered as `cmp max; bgt def; cmp min; blt def; ...`, a single case whose target is the default is dropped, and a trailing cluster case that goes to the default loses its compare but keeps the bound. The target's `{0, 4}` range check (`cmp #4; bgt; cmp #0; blt; beq end`) therefore needs a third case in `1..3` whose compare disappears, and every placement tried (fake cases with default, `break` or their own body, `goto`, dead statements, a distinct case-0 body) emits a `cmp`/`cmpne` for it. Without case 0 and with a fake `case 1:` on `default` the only diff is `cmp #1` for `cmp #0` (`d/5510/v4/top_1.c`).
- **`getScaleData_` (`0202b7c0`) is down to 1 diff under `dsi/1.1`** (`d/sc/b2.c`, was 7 / 2). The 3:1 fx16 interpolation is `(p[idx].s + (p[idx].s << 1) + p[idx_sub].s) >> 2`, written inline with no locals; that gives the target's two element addresses and the fresh register for `s`. Locals for the four values, `* 3` and `fx16 *` indexing are all worse. The diff left is `adds r7, r8, r7` for `adds r7, r7, r8` in the fx32 block: `(fx64)v * 3 + v_sub` (and the old comma form) gives the target's registers with the operands swapped, the add chain `((fx64)v + v + v) + v_sub` gives the target's operand order with `r5`–`r8` rotated (7 / 0, `d/sc/v5/x00.c`). 128 comma placements, 60 add-tree shapes and casts, 64 pointer/struct spellings 720 bare-declaration orders and cxxperm under `dsi/1.1` on both drafts (27,205 compiles on `b2.c`, 21,967 on `x00.c`, `perm/sc/`) did not give both.
- **No change:** `NextAction` (a case-14 body with a dead address-taken local gives its own return block, the local is still removed), `func_0206e858` (pragmas `opt_*` off one or two at a time and a two-iteration loop do not stop the merge of the two `data_0214b105` pool loads), `func_020459cc` (eight forms of the `sysState` store inline), `func_0203d298` (160 type/declaration/expression forms of the trigger/release pair and every position of `repeat = 0`).
## `tools/splitmod.py`: an automatic split-then-modify search (all modules but ov004, round splitsweep1)

Three new matches. `tools/splitmod.py draft func --module ovNNN` applies the split-then-modify idiom from the shared1 round mechanically. It works in the target function and in every function of the same file that the target calls. Each variant is scored with `tools/match.py` (raw and register-masked sequence diff).

- **split**: `T v = A op B;` or `v = A op B;` becomes `T v = A; v op= B;`, and also `T v = B; v op= A;` when `op` is commutative. The second statement goes either right after the first or after the declarations that follow it (`@run`).
- **extract**: a side-effect-free `A op B` inside a statement, an argument, or an `if`/`switch` condition becomes `s32 sm_tN = A; sm_tN op= B;` before the statement. For `>>`, `/` and `%` it also tries `u32`.
- **search**: all singles are scored, then pairs of the best ten singles, then "every site at once". The two best states are expanded again, for up to three rounds. A last sweep moves one declaration inside its run, or swaps two adjacent plain statements.

The sweep covered 134 unmatched functions whose best draft was masked ≤ 10 (from permsweep2 `best.txt`/`scores.txt`, `bigfresh7/best_scan*.txt`, `ppasweep1/cand.txt`, and smallsweep1). It ran on the best draft and on the second-best draft of each. A run takes 1–5 minutes.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_02161994` (new `Lo_02161994.cpp`) | 10 / 0 | Three extractions: `s32 py = y - 0x60000; py -= off + 0x4000;` as the `Model_SetPos` argument in the first block, `x = pos[0]; x -= 0x9c000;` in the second, and `s32 py2 = y; py2 += off + 0x4000;`. The ppa one-element array `x_1[1]` that the draft carried turned out to be unneeded. |
| `func_ov000_021c6d64` (new `Menu_021c6d64.cpp`) | 11 / 6 | First block `s32 x = (pos->x >> 12) - b4 / 2 - b0; x -= c0 * 6;`. Second block `s32 cx = pos->x >> 12; cx += b4 / 2; x = cx + b0; x += c0 * 6;`. The sweep stopped at 7 / 4 on a permuter draft that had the `unk_150` store and `Model_SetPos` swapped. Swapping them back and rerunning splitmod reached 0 / 0, and the same body also matches in the cleaner `perm/p_021c6d64/orig.c` header context. |
| `func_ov009_0220b8a0` (appended to `ov009_0220b70c.cpp`) | 6 / 0 | `s32 dy = v.y + 0x1800; dy -= self->pos.y; self->pos.y += Ov009b_Mul(dy, 0x19a);` in place of `Ov009b_Mul(v.y + 0x1800 - self->pos.y, 0x19a)`. |

- **Extraction mattered more than splitting existing locals.** Most near-miss drafts have no `T v = A op B;` local at all: 18 of the first 30 had no split site. All three matches came from extracting a subexpression that was written inline (an argument or a right-hand side) into a split local.
- **Adjacent-statement swaps are a cheap companion.** `func_ov000_021bd474` went from 26 / 4 to 2 / 0 with one `+=` split (`d = FXM(dx, dx); d += FXM(dy, dy);`) and a swap of `scr.x <<= 12` / `scr.y <<= 12`. `func_ov000_0217a1f8` went from 8 / 8 to 1 / 1 with a swap alone. Both are still open:
  - `bd474`: the two `asr #31` sign extensions for the final `FXM(d?, k)` are in the opposite order. cxxperm (16k compiles), ppa, decl orders, `s64` temporaries and swapped stores did not move them.
  - `a1f8`: the target passes `-1` (`mvn r2, #0`) where the draft passes the `u8` 0xff. Every `s32` parameter type makes MWCC derive it as `sub r2, r1, #0x79` from the `0x78` beside it.
- **Partial gains still worth finishing:** `ov012_021ffd34` 27 / 3 to 23 / 0, `ov015_02211b2c` 6 / 6 to 2 / 2 (only one `ldr`/`add` pair is out of order), `ov003_021e6274` 8 / 5 to 7 / 2, and `ov000_0219763c` 93 / 8 to 48 / 4. A second splitmod pass on every improved output found nothing new.
- **Rename the result before you land it.** Rename `sm_tN` and put each statement on its own line. The tool scores lines with `function:line:op` labels, so its output is meant to be read.

## `s64` copies assigned in the target's order fix hoisted sign words (round shared3)

One new match, confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build (0 FAILED, every ROM check OK). Drafts and tools are in the scratchpad under `shared3/` (`t/sc.py` and `t/par.sh` are the mainmid6 scorers pointed at this clone; `sc.py` also prints `match.py`'s own count as `m=N`, which includes pool-offset diffs that the seqdiff score hides).

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_021bd474` (0x1b0; new file `src/ov000/Scr_021bd474.cpp`) | 2 / 0 (`splitsweep1/sw/func_ov000_021bd474_r34.cpp`) | Declare `s64 ex; s64 ey;` in the `else` block and assign `ey = dy; ex = dx;` (in that order) right after the two differences, before `d = FXM(dx, dx)`. The final steps become `self->unk_78 += (s32)((ex * k + 0x800) >> 12);` and the same with `ey`. |

- **What it fixes.** For `(s64)dx * k` with a non-constant `k`, MWCC computes the high word of `dx` (`asr rX, rY, #0x1f`) once and hoists it above the `if` that picks `k`. Two such sign words come out in the order of the multiplies that need them. The target has `dy`'s first. Swapping the two final statements, `s64` temporaries for the products, a `FXM(k, dx)` operand swap and declaration orders all rearrange other code. Widened copies assigned in the target's order move only the two `asr`s. Assigned `ex` first, or placed after `k = self->unk_4c`, they change nothing. Try this whenever the only diff left is a pair of `asr #31` in the wrong order.
- **Read `match.py`'s count, not only seqdiff.** `func_ov013_021fee10` (`ovhimid6/d/fee10/v5.cpp`) is 0 / 0 by seqdiff but 5 in `match.py` (the extra pool word shifts five `ldr [pc, #N]`). `ov010_02200a00` (`g_97.cpp`) is 2 / 2 by seqdiff and 9 in `match.py`, `ov003_021e83d0` and `ov014_02205634` are 3 / 3 and 8.
- **Not matched, with what was tried this round:**
  - `func_0206e858` (1): MWCC keeps one constant address in a callee-saved register whenever two uses are in one basic block. In a test file this held for plain globals, `&g.name[0]`, `(char *)&g + 1`, casts through `void *`/`u8 *`/`u32`, `volatile` casts, every `opt_*`, `optimization_level` 0–4, `optimize_for_size`, `scheduling`/`peephole`/`register_coloring` off (180 variants in `shared3/e858/`). Two different symbols for the same address (`data_0214b104.name` and `data_0214b105`) give the target's code but two pool words where the target has one. A null-checked local (`char *d = g.name; if (d) ...`) is not folded here, unlike the string locals of round ovhimid5. The call in each switch arm is not tail-merged (28 diffs).
  - `func_ov000_0217a1f8` (1): 384 prototype type pairs with 6 spellings of `-1`, and 54 casts of both arguments. A `u8` third parameter gives `mov r2, #0xff`, `u16` builds `0xffff`, and every 32-bit type gives `sub r2, r1, #0x79`. The round ovhi21 two-definition `kq = 0x78` at all 18 placements, with plain, comma and cast arguments, stays at 2 / 2 (`mov r1, #0x78` before `mov r0, r4`).
  - `func_ov015_02211b2c` (2): eight inline wrappers for the last call (index, name or model pointer as the argument, either order), a flags pointer local (plain and `volatile`), a model pointer local, `*(tbl + i)`, `&self[0x14c]` and `const` namespace views of the two globals all compile to the same code or worse.
  - `getScaleData_` (1 under `dsi/1.1`): 168 `fx64` local grids (each of the four values widened, every assignment order, `* 3`, add chain and reversed forms), 64 comma placements on either operand, and 81 split forms (`fx64 t = ...; t += ...;`, `t *= 3`, `t += t << 1`). Each gives the 1 / 0 (operands swapped) or the 7 / 0 (registers rotated) build.
  - `func_ov000_021be5dc` (3 / 0): an `s64` copy of `z` or of the multiplier, an `s64` parameter for `ToDepth` and a split `s64` product all give the same code.
  - `ov010_02200a00`: a zero local before any of the first four statements changes nothing. With a second definition under `if ((u32)self & 3)` the test is not removed here (33 or worse). In a test file the three stack stores stay on whichever side of the `NNS_G3dGlb` flag store the source puts them, and the `mov #0` stays with them. The target has the `mov` first and the stores after the flag store, which no plain, `static const` copy or pointer form reproduced.
  - `ov014_02205634` (3 / 3): all 1,260 orders of the nine statements of the distance block that keep `x`, `y`, `z` stores in that order score 6 / 7 or worse.

## Sibling-inline redrafts, a C function that only gets close as C++, and trailing parameters on a big register mess (ov000 below 0x021a0000 and ov001–ov008, round ovlo12 second pass)

- **Match: `func_ov000_0219763c`** (`src/ov000/Robo_0219763c.cpp`). The best old draft was 93 diffs after many permuter rounds. A fresh draft that copies the idioms of the matched sibling `Robo_02196258.cpp` matches: the `SetNibble`/`GetNibble` inlines, a death inline that ends in `obj->vfunc_08()`, an `s32 zero = 0;` local used for both `e = (Ent *)zero` and the last argument of `func_ov000_0219b400`, and `p = *(Vec *)(self + 0x70)`. The last fix was passing the scale to the spawn inline as a pointer (`Spawn0219763c(u8 *self, s16 id, const s16 *scale, Vec *p)`) and reading `*scale` after `func_02065f88`. If a draft has been permuted for a long time without progress, start again from the nearest matched sibling file before trying more permutations.
- **Compile a `.c` draft as C++ when the function sits in a gap.** `func_ov003_021ea588` follows `ov003_021ea3d0.c`, but no file covers it, so it can go in a file of its own. The same source wrapped in `extern "C" {}` and compiled as `.cpp` scores 17 / 9 (`scratchpad/ovlo12/d/a588/c1.cpp`), where the `.c` scores 42 / 26 (`c1.c`). Five other `.c` drafts (`ov002_021e41cc`, `021e3dac`, `ov000_0214b1a0`, `0215d4b4`, `ov003_021d36fc`) give the same score either way, so check each one. In the same function, copying `pos` into `unk_84` through a pointer inline (`VecCopy_a588(&self->pos, &self->unk_84)`) gives the target's second load of `pos.y` for the call argument. A plain field-by-field copy merges the two loads. What is left is allocation in the `func_ov000_0219aae8` argument block: the target loads `offset.x` late, keeps `a` in `sl` and pushes `r10` instead of `r3`. None of the 120 orders of the five statements in that block, nor struct copies or a `param` local, fixed it. A 15-minute `cxxperm` run did not either.
- **Trailing parameters on a big register mess: `func_ov000_02164c50`** (0x694 bytes, 234 / 11). Its callee-saved registers were rotated almost everywhere. `k2.sh` with `K=1` found that making the first `sz` local a parameter takes it to 114 / 11 (`scratchpad/ovlo12/d/4c50/sz.cpp`). Two `cxxperm` rounds from there reached 60 / 11 (`scratchpad/ovlo12/d/4c50/p83.cpp`). A second `K=1` pass on that output found nothing more. A single function-scope `sz` gives 136. On a large function whose structural diff is already small, try `K=1` before anything else.
- **`func_ov000_021512b4`** went from 35 / 3 to 11 / 0. Here `x` and `h` are trailing parameters (`d/12b4/xh.cpp`, 23 / 3), and two `cxxperm` rounds added a one-element `y` array (`perm/12b4b/out-011-0-70.cpp`). What is left is an `x`/`h` register swap (`r5`/`r7`). None of these fixed it: every placement of `x`/`h` among the parameters, `x` as an array, other types for `h`/`x`, or other forms of the `h` expression. Without the `y` array, the score falls back to 67.
- **`func_ov002_021e0488`** (OAM digit writer). A clean redraft with real locals and an `s16` power-of-ten loop (`d/0488/c.cpp`, 66 / 32) gets the instructions close. But the target frame has one more 4-byte spill slot at `sp+0x18` (`0x78` plus a pushed `r3`, against our `0x74`). The old 61 / 29 draft only got there by moving locals into extra parameters. Declaring `val` at function scope gives 51 / 32 (`d/0488/v.cpp`), and a `cxxperm` round from there reached 37 / 28 (`d/0488/p65.cpp`), helped by extra trailing parameters. Neither unused nor dead-stored 4-byte locals, address-taken locals, nor function-scope `m`/`v`/`out` add the missing slot. Changing `out` to `s32 out[3]` fixes the frame size but moves `out` above `tmpl`. So stack objects seem to be ordered by size, and the missing slot is a 4-byte addressable local.
- **`func_ov000_0214dd3c`** went from 55 / 0 to 28 / 0 (`d/dd3c/m2.cpp`) with:
  - a C++ node class with `static operator new` calling `func_0203dd9c`;
  - a constructor that calls `func_ov000_02153f90(this)` and zeroes `unk_30..36`;
  - `new` declared after the duplicate-id loop;
  - a one-element `g` array in the key inline.

  The key compare still has a register rotation, and a 15,875-compile `cxxperm` run left it at 28.
- **`func_ov000_0219b808`** is at 4 / 0 again (`d/b808/f.cpp`), reached by declaring both `VecFx32` temporaries at function scope ahead of `m` (`p1`, `p2`, `m`). Every other scope and order is 14 or worse. What is left is the registers of the three `ldrh`/`strh` of the `rot` copy in the `Send` inline: the target uses `r1`/`r3`/`lr` and we use `r2`/`lr`/`lr`. None of these fixed it: field-wise and reversed copies, the copy outside the inline, by-value `rot`, all 120 parameter orders, or a 7,264-compile `cxxperm` run.
- **Pooled G3 constants** (`0215b2e4`, `0215b598`, `0215d4b4`, `021535a8`) are still open. These all still give `mov`, not a literal-pool load:
  - every optimization pragma, and `optimize_for_size`;
  - absolute-address objects;
  - casts to pointer, `long long`, enum, `float` and `short`;
  - `const` reference parameters of an inline, which become real memory temporaries;
  - `auto_inline`;
  - high register pressure;
  - `static const` objects in C and C++.

  The target loads a pooled constant again at each use, without CSE, even though it derives other constants from the same values (`rsb r0, r7, #0xe40`). So the constants probably came through some macro or inline form we have not found yet.
## Calls into TWL-only code: absolute symbols in the `.lcf` (main, round mainmid7)

46 new matches in main, all confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build (0 FAILED, every ROM check OK). Drafts are in the scratchpad under `mainmid7/d/`.

About 70 relocations in main (and more in the overlays) point into the TWL-only region (`0x0221xxxx`, `0x0228xxxx`). `relocs.txt` marks them `module:none`, because no module of the NDS ROM defines those addresses. Until now, every function with such a `bl` stayed in a gap: a C call needs a symbol, and the linker had none.

- **The lever.** `config/usa/arm9/abs_symbols.txt` lists `name addr` pairs. The `lcf` rule now runs `tools/lcf_abs.py` after `dsd lcf`, which writes `name = addr;` next to `__OVERLAY_COUNT` in `build/usa/arm9.lcf`. Declare the callee in C under that name (`void func_0228de80(u32 ndmaNo, ...);`) and the linker resolves the `bl`. `dsd check symbols` ignores the extra absolute symbols. Add a line for every new TWL callee you use. TWL data works the same way (`data_022919dc`, an `OSTitleId` in TWL `.bss`).
- **Thumb callers.** Give a Thumb target its address with bit 0 set (`func_0228caa0 0x0228caa1`), and the linker emits a Thumb `bl`. An ARM target called from Thumb needs `blx`, and an absolute symbol always gets `bl`; mark such a target `arm` in a third column (round twl2, below).
- **Unreferenced TWL functions get stripped.** Nothing in the NDS build calls the TWL-only paths (`func_0204a598`..`0204a7dc`, the GX loaders, the TWL SVC wrappers), so once they leave the gap `-dead` removes them and everything after them shifts. Put them under `#pragma force_active on`. This also fixes the old note on `func_0204a3b8`, which is now placed.
- **The SDK structure transfers directly.** Most of these are TwlSDK/NitroSystem functions whose NITRO versions were already matched. The TWL versions add an `OS_IsRunOnTwl()` branch or a `dmaNo > 3` NDMA branch:
  - `gx_load.c` is now one file from `0x02014304` to `0x02014e30` (21 functions, the old `gx_load_2/3/4.c` merged in). The `GXi_DmaCopy16/32`, `GXi_DmaCopy32Async` and `GXi_WaitDma` inlines are `if (dmaNo != -1) { if (dmaNo > 3) MI_NDma...(dmaNo - 4, ...) else MIi_Dma...(..., TRUE); } else MI_CpuCopy...`. Only the 16-bit one checks alignment before the NDMA copy. All matched first time.
  - `GX_Init` and `G3X_InitTable` test `GXi_DmaId > 3` once, then make both fill calls in each arm, reloading `GXi_DmaId` for the second. Two calls to one inline (one test each) do not match.
  - `NNS_G3dGeSendDL`: the TWL wrapper reads `GXi_DmaId` after `OS_IsRunOnTwl()`, inside the wrapper, not as an argument.
  - `RtcCommonCallback` is the NitroSDK body plus an `OS_IsRunOnTwl() == TRUE` default case, with explicit empty cases 3–5 and 11–15 (needed for the 16-entry jump table). In `RTC_SEQ_GET_DATETIME` the target masks the whole word for the year (`and #0xff`, written `*(u32 *)pSrc & 0xff`), while `RTC_SEQ_GET_DATE` extracts the bitfield (`lsl`/`lsr`).
  - `func_0201d648` (`PMi_CommonCallback`): the jump tables needed the empty cases (`0x62`, inner `1`..`3`), and case `0x61` written before `0x60`. Case `0x60` then becomes a branch into `0x61`'s tail.
  - `func_0201dca4` (PM DMA stop): a `switch` on the DMA mode with case `0x28000000` first, and `if (!(cnt & 0x02000000)) MI_WaitDma(i); else MI_StopDma(i);` in `default`.
- **Game code calling TWL SDK.** `func_0203d5c4` (system init), `func_0203d8b0`, `func_0203c638` (TWL graphics reset, with the `GfxSys_5.cpp` inlines and `(int)(DISPCNT & 0x300010) >> 20` for the `asr`), `func_02049344` (sound init) and `func_0204a598`..`0204a7dc` (camera-shutter sound loading, `SndMgr_6.cpp`).
- **Overlays.** The same `abs_symbols.txt` is shared by every module (there is one `.lcf`), so the overlay TWL callers (`func_ov000_0214b438`..`0214b810` and others in the old notes) can use it too.
- **Renames:** `func_02013428` `GX_Init`, `func_02014304` `GX_LoadBGPltt`, `func_0201439c` `GXS_LoadBGPltt`, `func_020147bc` `GXS_LoadBG3Scr`, `func_020194a0` `OS_InitArenaEx`.

## Narrow stack parameters, a NULL-handling inline and a reused `u8` counter (ov000 above 0x021a0000 and ov009–ov015, round ovhimid7)

- **Match: `func_ov000_021b70c0`** (`src/ov000/Mdl_021b70c0.cpp`, 0x520 bytes). It needed four changes to the old bigsweep1 draft:
  - **Stack parameters typed `u8`.** The target's prologue copies its stack arguments (`ldr r4, [sp, #0x28]; str r4, [sp, #0x28]` ...) in a fixed order. Declaring `needMat` and `nsub` as `u8` instead of `BOOL`/`u32` moved those copies into the target's order.
  - **The inline handles a NULL set itself.** `GetMdlByIdx(set, i)` starts with `if (set != NULL)` and returns NULL otherwise, in place of the caller-side `if (set) m = Get(set, i); else m = NULL;`. Both NULL paths then merge, giving the target's `addne; bne join; mov rX, #0`.
  - **A reused function-scope `u8 i`.** `self->unk_bb = 0; i = self->unk_bb;` gives the target's unmasked index register.
  - **A trailing unused `void *tex` parameter.** It fixes the register order of `mdl` and `tex`.
- **New tool:** `$S/ovhimid7/t/ptype.py src func module outdir` scores each parameter retyped one at a time (`u8`/`s8`/`u16`/`s16`/`int`/`u32`/`BOOL`/pointer), plus pairs of the stack parameters. Run on eight other drafts, it found nothing.
- **Partial gains:**
  - `func_ov014_0220f3f0`: 67 / 12 → 28 / 12 (`d/f3f0/b2.cpp`), from `s32 dx, dy, dz` difference locals in the axis inline after `VEC_Normalize`. Still open:
    - a register swap in block 1;
    - the target computes `&ax` late into `r6`, but any pointer variable we use for it is hoisted.
  - `func_ov010_021ff21c`: 20 / 10 → 15 / 12 (`d/f21c/c2.cpp`). Reading the rot struct through a `namespace D { extern "C" const u8 data_...[]; }` view hoists the table load. The `ldrh` order (0x2c, 0x2a, 0x2e) still differs.
  - `func_ov000_021ad130`: 21 / 9 → 21 / 6 (`d/ad130/h/A1.cpp`). It now uses the `CmdQueue` loop of the matched siblings `Lo_02166be8` and `Widget_02182590` (`for (i = q->unk_90; i != q->index;)`). Six-byte `ldrh/ldrh/strh/strh/ldrh/strh` runs are a struct copy of `u16[3]`. The target still loads the queue pointer after the copies.
- **No progress (attempts in the round report):** `021b3e90` (5 / 1, `movne` against an unconditional `mov`), `021aa978`, `ov010_02205444`, `021b209c`, `ov013_02205c74`, `021b628c`, `ov015_021fc40c`, `021c8408` (5 / 2) and `ov015_02211b2c` (2 / 2).
## TWL callers in ov000, a typed `blx` target, and the arena constants (round twl2)

16 new matches, each confirmed with a full `ninja` build (0 FAILED, every module check OK) and, for the ARM ones, `tools/match.py`.

- **Every `module:none` caller is now placed.** A scan of all `relocs.txt` files for `module:none` relocations inside unmatched functions found only `func_0200c118` in main and eleven functions in ov000; no other overlay has any (every overlay ends below `0x022183c0`, so nothing there is mistaken for TWL code). The ov000 ones, with their TWL callees added to `abs_symbols.txt`, all matched first time under `#pragma force_active on`:
  - `src/ov000/Twl_0214b438.cpp` (`0214b438`..`0214b540`): a TWL-only singleton in `data_ov000_021ccd20`. Create is `new(0x14)` plus a TWL constructor behind `if (!OS_IsRunOnTwl()) return; if (!OS_IsRunOnTwl()) OS_Terminate();`; delete is `HEAP_DELETE_FN` from `ov000/HeapScope.h`.
  - `src/ov000/Twl_0214b598.cpp` (`0214b598`, `0214b5bc`), `src/ov000/Twl_0214b670.cpp` (`0214b670`, `0214b68c`): the destructor, update and size/ready queries of the TWL object at `+0x20` of the loader in `Loader_0214b88c.cpp` (its constructor is `Twl_0214b6d8.cpp`). `0214b68c` is now kept by `force_active`, so the old problem of splitting `0214b670` (the stripped `0214b68c`) is gone.
  - `src/ov000/Twl_0214b724.cpp` (`0214b724` destructor, `0214b810` update) of that loader; the sub-object calls are the two inlines above.
- **ARM targets called from Thumb (`func_0200c118`).** MWLD picks `bl`/`blx` from the target's `$a`/`$t` mapping symbol, which an absolute symbol does not have (an `SHN_ABS` `STT_FUNC` symbol in an object, even with an absolute `$a` beside it, still gets `bl`; a zero-size section is dropped). `abs_symbols.txt` now takes a third column, `name addr arm`. For those, `tools/lcf_abs.py` writes `build/usa/abs_arm.o` with one 4-byte ARM section `.abs.<name>` per entry (holding `$a` and the global function), adds a memory region `ABS_<name> : ORIGIN = addr > build/abs_<name>.bin` and a section placing it there, and appends the object to `objects.txt` (the `lcf` rule now passes the objects file). The stub's bytes go to their own `.bin`, never into the ROM, and `dsd check symbols` accepts the extra symbol. `func_0200c118` is appended to `libs/nitro/svc/svc_twl.c`.
  - `tools/match.py` cannot check Thumb calls (it shows the unrelocated `bl` halves, 6 lines for `func_0200c118` and for the matched `func_0200c0fc` alike), and `objdiff` scores `func_0200c118` at 96%, because dsd delinks a `module:none` call as raw bytes (`blx`) while our object has a relocated `bl`. The module check is byte-exact.
- **Linker constants (`OS_GetInitArenaHi`, `OS_GetInitArenaLo`).** The same list holds the SDK's link-time constants: `SDK_SYS_STACKSIZE 0x1000`, `SDK_IRQ_STACKSIZE 0x800` and `SDK_SECTION_ARENA_EX_START 0x0d000000`, used as `extern char X[];` and `(s32)X`. Only values whose use the compiler would otherwise fold need a symbol: the stack sizes (tested against 0 at run time) and `0x0d000000` (a pool load in the target although it would fit a `mov`). The other pool words (`0x022183c0`, `0x02298140`, `0x01ffcac0`, `0x02fe15c0`, `0x02fff000`, `0x02fff680`, `0x023e0000`) are plain constants, which keeps their objdiff rows clean. `HW_DTCM` is dsd's `__DTCM_LO`. Both functions follow the TwlSDK `os_arena.c` switch (cases `MAIN`, `MAINEX`, `ITCM`, `DTCM`, `SHARED`, `WRAM_MAIN`; `MAINEX` tests the console size inside the `OS_IsRunOnTwl()` branches), and both matched first time. Appended to `libs/nitro/os/os_arena_2.c`, which now ends at `0x02019704`, directly before `os_arena_3.c`.

## Twin search, statement-order climbing and vfunc arity (ov000 below 0x021a0000 and ov001–ov008, round ovlo13)

- **Twin search.** Disassemble every unmatched function and every matched function in all modules (main and the overlays) with capstone. Replace the registers with `R` and the branch targets and pool offsets with placeholders. Then rank the matched functions by `difflib` ratio, using a 4-gram prefilter to find candidates. Below about 0.6, the matches came from shared inlines such as `PlayPart` or the heap-scope delete, and are not useful. The script is `scratchpad/ovlo13/t/twins2.py <list>`, with environment variables `K` (n-gram size), `CF` (prefilter fraction) and `TH` (ratio). It found:
  - `ov006_021e0b80` ↔ `ov006_021e1bb4`;
  - `ov000_021597dc` ↔ `ov000_02155770`.

  Both are now matched.
- **Start from the twin's inline and climb the statement order.** For `func_ov006_021e0b80`, the caption draw inline from `ov006_021e1bb4.cpp`, written out flat, was already close. What was left was only scheduling, and source order breaks the scheduler's ties. `scratchpad/ovlo13/t/hcorder.py` does a beam hill-climb over single moves and swaps of the statements in a range. It never reorders two statements where one writes a variable the other reads or writes, and calls and braces stay fixed. It found the matching order. `hcall.py` runs the climb over every run of three or more simple statements in a draft. A queue of 20 old leftovers gained nothing from it: their diffs are register colouring, not scheduling.
- **Vfunc arity shows in the registers.** `func_ov000_021597dc` is the list handler of `Lo_02155770.cpp` without the item and sub-menu cases. Its vfunc calls set `mov r2, #0` and load the vtable into `r3` instead of `r2`. That means each call has one more argument (`vfunc_ac(1, 0)` and so on). Also, the `0x94` NULL test comes before the `b2`/`b1` checks.
- **Some old drafts are semantically wrong.** The splitsweep `021e6eb0` draft (9 / 8) stores `trig = ~old` before the `&=`. The 13 / 2 `021e96cc` drafts pass their arguments swapped. The correct forms score 30 / 15 (`ovlo13/d/6eb0`) and 13 / 4 (`ovlo13/d/96cc`). Check the semantics before you build on a low score.
- **Still open** (best drafts in `scratchpad/ovlo13/d/`, scored under dsi/1.3):
  - `0215e6f0`: 11 / 0, `x`/`mask`/`buf0` register rotation.
  - `021708ec`: 11 / 0, `mq`/`end` swap.
  - `ov003_021cd744`: 28 / 0, `self` should be in `r4`.
  - `021688c4`: 20 / 0.
  - `0217e560`: 6 / 6 and `0218a670`: 12 / 6. Both are scheduling-only, and no source change alters the hash.
  - `0219badc`: 5 / 4.
  - `ov003_021e83d0`: 6 / 5.
  - `ov006_021e7004`: 21 / 10.
  - `ov002_021d3e14`: 18 / 0. The zero register `r5`/`r6` is swapped with `g`/`base`. Its twin is `Lo_021782e0`. Moving `g`, using a `zero` local, declaration orders and scoping all leave the code unchanged.
  - `0218e12c`: 18 / 0 (`ovmid8/pa/0218e12c/base.cpp`). A flat form with the right inner registers is 35 / 3 (`ovlo13/d/e12c`).
  - `ov006_021e2490`: 17 / 0. It does not change across 72 type/declaration-order variants.
  - `0214b724`/`0214b810` are TWL-only (`module:none`).
## splitmod under dsi/1.3, the right-operand split, and a draft that tested the wrong variable (all modules but ov004, round splitsweep2)

Two new matches, each confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build (0 FAILED, every ROM check OK). Lists, logs and drafts are in the scratchpad under `splitsweep2/` (`unm.txt`, `cands_all.tsv`, `resc.tsv`, `best12.tsv`, `sweep_res.txt`, `sw/`, `ch/`, `d/`). Scores are raw / register-masked seqdiff.

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `func_ov000_0214bcc4` (0x164, item-id lookup; new file `src/ov000/Lo_0214bcc4.c`) | 11 / 0 (`ovmid1/b/0214bcc4/base.c`) | splitmod took it to 2 / 0 with two extractions: `s32 hidden = fl; hidden &= 0x100; if (hidden)` and the first `func_02059544` argument as `s32 slot = 0x30; slot += (s32)base;`. The last 2 were a bug in the draft: in the `id` chain it tested `got & 4` and `got & 8` (always 0 there, since `got == 0`), where the target tests `fl`. With `fl & 4` / `fl & 8` it matches. The `fl` fix alone gives 13 / 0, so both are needed. |
| `func_ov015_022113e8` (0x43c, shop item roll; new file `src/ov015/ov015_022113e8.cpp`) | 37 / 9 (`ovhi1/13e8_b.cpp`) | Five chained steps (`ch/`): splitmod (a declaration move and the `price` read swapped below `found = FALSE`, 24 / 7); `ppa.sh` (`j` as a trailing parameter, `BOOL found[1]`, 10 / 7); splitmod again (the first `found = FALSE` after the `price` read, 7 / 7); the stack parameter as `volatile s32 budget`, copied into a local (88 / 2); `ppa.sh` again (that local as `s32 rest[1]`, 2 / 2). The last 2 were a tail merge: write `sub = price * num;` at the end of each of the three `num` arms instead of once after them. |

- **Candidates.** `t/unm.py` lists 327 unmatched functions (main, ITCM, DTCM, ov000–ov015 without ov004). `t/collect.py` finds their drafts in the earlier rounds' `best*`/`scores*` lists, the splitsweep1 tables, the ppasweep1 `res.txt` files and (by grepping for the definition) in `ovhimid4`–`6/d`, `mainmid5`–`6/d`, `vol1/r`, `shared2/best` and `splitsweep1/sw`, `d`: 9,397 drafts of 172 functions. Up to five per function with the best old scores, plus the three newest unscored ones (1,146 drafts), were rescored under dsi/1.3 (`t/rescore.py`, `resc.tsv`). 131 functions have a draft at masked ≤ 12 (`best12.tsv`); 9 of those are 0 / 0 and are the known string-pool, gap or asm blockers (`0204c158`, `02058d10`, `02058f2c`, `020163b8`, `02004ffc`, `02077118`, `ov000_0214b670`, `ov003_021e44dc`, `ov006_021d3f04`). No draft scored much better under dsi/1.3 than its old score.
- **Sweep.** `splitmod.py` ran on the best draft of the other 122 (two at a time, `t/drive.sh`, `t/sw1.sh`; the second half with `--iters 2 --pairs 6`). Only eight changed: `0214bcc4` (above), `ov015_022113e8` 37 / 9 to 24 / 7 (a declaration move and a statement swap), and raw-only or worse-masked changes on `ov010_021ff21c` (14 / 12), `0203c2bc`, `ov000_0219e998` (14 / 5), `ov010_02201064`, `ov000_0216c310` (65 / 9) and `ov000_02164c50`. Most of these drafts had been through splitmod (round splitsweep1) or were products of earlier lever sweeps, so on old drafts the compiler change did not open anything new.
- **When a register-only diff sits on a test, read the test.** `0214bcc4`'s last diff was `tst r6, #4` against `tst r8, #4`, which looks like allocation. It was the draft testing a different variable. Check that each source variable in a differing instruction is the one the target uses before sweeping registers.
- **Chaining levers on `ov015_022113e8`.** Each lever moved it only after the one before had run. The stack parameter `b` was loaded into `sb` in the prologue; the target loads it from `[sp, #0x28]` straight into `r0` for `func_02040528`, after `MI_CpuFill8`. Round lo12's `volatile` stack parameter, copied into a plain local, fixed the load and broke the registers (masked 7 to 2, raw 7 to 88), and the one-element array lever then brought raw back to 2. A diff that is a `mul` before a `b` in the target and after a join in ours is a tail merge: repeat the statement in each arm.
- **Other changes, no match:** `ov010_021ff21c` 15 / 12 to 14 / 12 came from the new right-operand split inside `Ov010_VecSub` (`sw/func_ov010_021ff21c_b1.cpp`). `ov000_0219e998` 15 / 6 to 14 / 5 (a `u32` division extraction; `ppa.sh` on it found nothing). `ov000_0216c310` 67 / 11 to 65 / 9. `k2.sh` and `volsweep.py` found nothing on the `022113e8` intermediates.
- **Tool changes (`tools/splitmod.py`).** For `-`, the split and extract steps also write the right-operand split from round ovhimid5 (`T v = B; v = A - v;`, labels ending in `r`). `return A op B;` is now an extraction site (it used to produce `s32 x = return a; ...`, which did not compile). Extraction skips operands that are empty or a type name, so `FLD(u8 *, p, 4)` no longer yields a `u8 * ...` "multiplication".

## The MSL unwinder: one struct for every decoded number, `opt_propagation off` and `exception_terminate` (main, round mainmid8)

Three new matches, each confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build (0 FAILED, every ROM check OK). Drafts are in the scratchpad under `mainmid8/d/` (`us/`, `na/`, `hu/`).

| Function | Before | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `UnwindStack` (`02008cdc`, now 0x834) | no draft | New file `libs/runtime/ExceptionHandler_unwind.cpp`. The old `func_02009508` (8 bytes, `add sp, sp, #0xa4; pop`) was only the unreachable epilogue of the `for (;;)` loop, so it is merged into `UnwindStack` in `symbols.txt`. |
| `NextAction` (`02008a20`, 0x2bc) | 1 frame word short (rounds mainsm, mainmid5, mainmid6) | Same file, written flat (no `SkipActionN` inlines). |
| `HandleUnexpected` (`020096e4`, 0x60) | 2 / 0 (smallsweep1) | New file `libs/runtime/ExceptionHandler_unexp.cpp`, the old draft plus the two pragmas below. |

- **Every decoded number goes in one struct local.** Both `UnwindStack` and `NextAction` keep the out-parameters of `DecodeSignedNumber`/`DecodeUnsignedNumber` in one `Ops o;` local, with one member per action field. The members run from action 19 up to action 1 in address order, and there is one unused word between action 16 and action 15. That unused member is the "hole at `sp+0x30`" that rounds mainmid5 and mainmid6 could not reproduce with scalars. A struct keeps it, a scalar is removed. `NextAction` uses the same layout without the action-1 member, and its branch offset is a separate scalar at `sp+0`. Scalars gave the right instructions but the wrong order of stack slots inside each action, and one word too few.
- **`#pragma opt_propagation off` keeps early computations early.** With propagation on, MWCC folds `isReg = action & 0x20;` into its use and computes `p + 4` after the call. The target computes both before the `DecodeSignedNumber` call, into callee-saved registers. Putting the pragma at the top of the file fixes this. A sweep of the pragma over the best draft of every other unmatched main function improved nothing.
- **`#pragma exceptions on` plus `#pragma exception_terminate` in the function body stop cross-jumping of calls.** In the target, each action keeps its own `blx` to the destructor, and only the `str rX, [info, #8]` stores are tail-merged, into the *first* case that has them. Without the pragma, MWCC merges the calls too and keeps the later copy, which costs about 20 instructions. The file must be C++ (`extern "C"` around everything). `exception_terminate` alone, or `exceptions on` alone, changes nothing. In `HandleUnexpected` the same pair moves the parameter copies (`mov r4, r3; mov r5, r2`) ahead of the `r0` save, which is what its last 2 diffs were. Try this pair on any C++ runtime function whose calls are not tail-merged where you expect them to be.
- **The structure came from other DS decompilations.** A GitHub code search for `UnwindStack DecodeSignedNumber` finds MWCC ARM unwinders in other DS decompilation projects. The search snippet of one of them shows `#pragma opt_propagation off`, `#pragma exception_terminate` and `Ops o;`. When an SDK or runtime function resists, search there for other projects' versions.
- **Twin search over main.** `twins2.py` (retargeted, `MI=12 MU=15` so that 15–40 instruction functions are searched too) found only twins that earlier rounds already used: `0205c234` ↔ `Slot_Push` in `0205de88`, `0204f524` ↔ `0205592c`, and `020701d4` ↔ `0207011c`. None of them gave a new match. `020701d4` (18 / 0) is still an `r2`/`r3` swap between `cat` and `ranges`.
## Old near-misses that store the wrong value, a `const` pointer that fixed a load order, and a two-definition hack that hid a register-only draft (ov000 below 0x021a0000 and ov001–ov008, round ovlo14)

One new match, `func_ov000_0214b1a0` (0x298, a textured quad draw; new file `src/ov000/Twl_0214b1a0.c`), confirmed with `tools/match.py` and a full `ninja` build (0 FAILED, every ROM check OK). Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovlo14/d/<addr>/`; `ovlo14/t/tdis.py <module> <func>` prints the target from the ROM with relocation names (`ninja dis` currently stops with "Inline tables must have a known size").

- **`func_ov003_021e6274`: the 7 / 2 and 6 / 4 drafts are semantically wrong.** In case 2 the target is `sub r0, r5, #1; lsl r1, r5, #0x1d; ...; and r5, r0, #0xff`: the bit-field gets the old `k`, and only the call and the `1 << n` test use `k - 1`. The splitsweep1 drafts store `k - 1`. Written correctly (`self->unk_93_1 = k; n = k - 1;`) the function is at 9 / 5 with a wider `k` (`d/6274/g1.cpp`, `nu16*.cpp`) or 12 / 5 with `s32 n` and a `u8 k` local in case 2 (`d/6274/w1.cpp`).
  - With a 16- or 32-bit `k`, MWCC adds `and r1, r5, #0xff` before the store, because the store converts `k` to `u8`.
  - A `u8 k` drops that `and`, but then any `u8`-truncated `k - 1` in case 2 swaps `self` and `t` (`r6`/`r7`) for the whole function. That holds for `n = (u8)(k - 1)`, `k--`, a block `u8 m`, `(u8)(k - 1)` written into both uses, every statement order, `me`/`t` as parameters or one-element arrays, and declaration orders (46 / 6 every time).
  - A function-scope `s32 n` fixes `self`/`t`, but then `n = k - 1` in case 2 loses the target's `and`.
  - A 15-minute `cxxperm` run did not get below 9 / 5.
- **`func_ov003_021d36fc`: drop the `kq` two-definition hack.** The old 20 / 2 draft used `kq = 0x50; if ((u32)self & 3) kq = 0x50;`. Without it the draft is 31 / 0, register-only (`d/36fc/nh.c`). The only problem left is that the hoisted zero and `t` swap registers (target: `t` in `r4`, zero in `r5`). None of these fixed it: `ppa.sh` (every local as a trailing parameter or a one-element array), a `z = 0` local for the zero stores (propagated away), or declaration orders.
- **`func_ov006_021cf420` (15 / 4) also leans on a `kq = 7` hack.** Without it, case 5 tail-merges with case 4 completely, from the `strb` onwards (60 / 47). The target merges only the last two stores, and case 4 jumps into case 5's tail where the target has case 5 jump into case 4's. Changing the case order, moving the hack into case 4 and function-scope declarations for case 4 did not change which way it merges. The zero register is also swapped with `blk` (`r6`/`r7`).
- **`func_ov000_0216c310`: a fresh draft with real locals** (`d/c310/q1.cpp`: no `y`/`next`/`n` parameters, an `s32` product and an `IsCurNode` inline) is 103 / 11. A `cxxperm` round took it to 65 / 9 (`ovlo14/perm/c310/`), close to the old parameter-hack draft (67 / 11). In the target the `0x24000 - y` temporary goes in `sl`, the register `prev` uses, and the `-1` and `-0x10` constants come from the hoisted `-0x400` (`asr #10`, `add #0x3f0`).
- **Register-only drafts that no change moved:**
  - `02152f8c` (13 / 0): moving the `LO_FX_MUL` statement, writing it as an `s64` block or as a copy then multiply all give the same code.
  - `02153804` (17 / 0): `sy` and `t` swap registers. All 49 single-declaration moves give the same code, and statement orders around the `FX_Div` call are worse.
- **`func_ov000_0214b1a0`: a wrong constant, then `const` on the pointer parameters.** The old draft wrote the third quad vertex as `0x07ff0800` and masked `scale[1] & 0xFF`; the target pools `0x08000800` and passes `scale[1]` whole (16 / 16 after fixing both). What was left was the order of the loads for the translate and scale vectors. The target is `ldmib r8, {r0, r1}; str r1, [sp, #0x68]; ldr r1, [r8]; str r0, [sp, #0x64]; str r1, [sp, #0x60]`: `y` and `z` first, `z` stored at once, and `x` loaded into `z`'s freed register. With `s32 *pos, s32 *scale` MWCC loads `x` first into a fresh register. Store-order permutations inside the `G3_Vec` inline (best 13 / 10), pointer, struct-copy and by-value struct inlines, macros, caller temporaries and `s32` typing were all the same or worse. Declaring the parameters `const s32 *pos, const s32 *scale` matches with the plain `v[0] = x; v[1] = y; v[2] = z;` inline. If loads from a pointer parameter come out in the wrong order and with an extra register, try `const` on the pointee.
- **`func_ov003_021d8fc0` (0x628, no earlier draft):** a fresh sequencer draft on the `Ov003Sequence` struct from `src/ov003/ov003_021d95e8.c` (`d/8fc0/f2.c` 307 / 194, `d2.c` 286 / 207). The logic is all there (the `0x81` random and `0x82` jump commands in the loop, then a 0–5 jump table). What is left: the target reloads the bit-field byte and `index` after every join, uses a 0x5c frame (15 six-byte entry copies from inlines, placed from `0x54` down in source order), and writes the `IsCmd` test two ways (`r = FALSE; if (...) r = TRUE;` in the loop, `if (...) return TRUE; return FALSE;` after it). `volatile u16 index` gets 209 / 145 but is not plausible source.
## Twin search over every overlay (ov000–ov015 without ov004, round twins1)

The ovlo13 twin search is now `tools/twins.py`. It reads `extract/usa` and `config/` from the repository root, builds its own list of unmatched ARM functions (those outside every `.text` range in `delinks.txt`), runs in parallel (`-j`), and writes a TSV. Two scores are reported: `cov` (matched instructions over the unmatched function's length, the ovlo13 score) and `sym` (difflib `ratio()`, which is low when a short function only matches part of a long one). `--mask-imm` also masks immediates and field offsets, which finds twins that use the same code shape on a different struct.

```shell
python3 tools/twins.py -j 4 -K 3 --cf 0.1 --th 0.5 --min-len 12 -o twins.tsv
python3 tools/twins.py -j 4 -K 3 --cf 0.1 --th 0.5 --min-len 12 --mask-imm -o twins_imm.tsv
```

The ranked list, with a note on each entry's known state, is `scratchpad/twins1/twins.tsv` (39 entries with `cov` ≥ 0.5, `__sinit_*` left out).

- **The pool is mined out.** There are 187 unmatched overlay functions. Apart from the `__sinit_*` stubs and four large functions with no twin (`ov000_021b5578`, `ov003_021cd91c`, `ov010_02209948` and `ov012_021fd708`, all with `cov` < 0.21), every function with a twin at `cov` ≥ 0.5 is already a documented leftover. The only pairs with both `cov` and `sym` above 0.6 are `ov009_021ff908` ↔ `ov009_0220487c` (0.77 / 0.71), `ov002_021d3e14` ↔ `ov000_021782e0`, `ov000_021be5dc` ↔ `ov000_021be220`, and `ov003_021e83d0` ↔ `ov003_021e7ee4`. With `--mask-imm`, `ov006_021e309c` ↔ `ov006_021e1bb4` (sym 0.65), `ov006_021e51f8` ↔ `021e5604` and `ov000_021535a8` ↔ `0215deb4` also show up. All of these are known blockers.
- **Redrafts from twins** (drafts in `scratchpad/twins1/d/`, permuter output in `perm/`):
  - `ov009_021ff908`, written from `0220487c` (the early return removed, `021987b8` moved up, `0219a2f4` given `pos.y + 0x2000`, `+ 0x3000` and the multiplier `0xcd`), is 49 at the start. cxxperm takes it to 18 (`perm/f908/out-018-*.cpp`; `self` in `r4` and `&p` in `r6`, as in the target). That equals the earlier best. `ppa.sh` found nothing more.
