- **`func_ov000_02191f18` is still at 84 masked lines (sc2 count, `ovbig8/d/1f18_best.cpp` unchanged).** Two-definition locals cannot remove the hoisted `0`/`0x1000`. A two-definition `kk = 0x1000` / `kz = 0` before each of the six hit calls still gets the hoisted register (85), because the `kk = 0x1000` definitions are constant uses too and are merged into the one hoisted value. Other findings:
  - The hoist is not set by single sites. Replacing any one `0`/`NULL`/`FALSE` literal with a variable (`t2/abl.py`, every site) never changes the prologue.
  - The `0x1000` hoist depends on the count. With the six `0x1000` arguments split between `0x1000` and `0x1001`, 6+0 and 5+1 (and 1+5) hoist, but 2+4, 3+3 and 4+2 do not (push `{r3-sb}` like the target, masked 73–75, `d/th_*.cpp`). Removing both the `0` height arguments and the `NULL` box arguments also stops both hoists. The target materialises `0x1000` six times without hoisting, so it must differ elsewhere.
  - These did not move it: `u16`/`s16`/`u32`/`int`/`long` types for the last or height parameters of both prototypes; four forms of `FindActor` at entry (if/else in either order, ternaries, a two-definition `NULL`); a `BOOL hit` local for the call result; an `s32 one` local; nesting `if (actor) { if (live) ... }`.
- **Tools.** `scratchpad/ovbig8/t2/allk.py` (per-case two-definition `0`/`0x1000` hit arguments), `t2/abl.py src regex repl` (replace each match in the function body in turn and print the prologue and score), and `t2/tv.py` (a `tv.score` that uses `sc2`'s masked count, for `tdc.py`).

## Three near-miss redrafts: a byte-pointer queue, depth-first inline slots, and a dead frame slot (ov000, ov003, round nearmix1)

`func_ov000_021ad130` matches as its own file, `src/ov000/Robo_021ad130.cpp` (`.text 0x021ad130..0x021ad8ac`, `.rodata 0x021c9fe8..0x021c9ff8`, new symbols `data_ov000_021c9feb`, `021c9fee` and `021c9ff2`). Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/nearmix1/` (`d/`, `t/`; `t/slots.py draft func mod` lists the 6-byte stack slots in store order for both sides).

- **`021ad130`: the queue pointer is a `u8 *`.** Round datadl1 left a rotation of three registers inside the `QHas` inline. Twenty-odd more forms (a `k` local, `p` declared outside the loop, `q` as a parameter, by-value ids, a flag with `goto`, `while` for `for`, a list inline, nested `if`s, a wrapper inline) all stay at 9 or get worse. Declaring `u8 *q = data_ov000_021ccd54` and reading `LFLD(s32, q, 0x90)`, `LFLD(s32, q, 0x94)` and `((s32 *)(q + 0x10))[i]` instead of a `CmdQueue *` matches: the address arithmetic gets different virtual registers, and `q` lands in `r8`.
- **The size sort reverses equal-sized objects.** With `021c9fe8`, `9feb` and `9fee` (all `u8[3]`) defined in address order, the linked file puts `9fee` first and `9fe8` last (overlay 0's checksum fails and `check symbols` swaps the two names). Defining them in the order `9fee`, `9feb`, `9fe8` gives the right layout. The `u16[3]` at `9ff2` is larger, so it stays last.
- **`ov003_021d8fc0`: writing out the nested inlines by hand gives depth-first slots, but no gain.** Replacing `Seq_Goto` in `Wait`/`Exec` and `Seq_Rand` in `Exec` with block locals (`SeqEntry ge = e;`, `SeqEntry re = e;`) puts each copy straight after its parent's parameter, as in the target. Inside `Exec` the three locals still come out bottom-up (`re` lowest), and swapping the case order or moving declarations doesn't change that. Declaring them at the inline's top scope removes the copies entirely. Raw went from 292 to 312 (`d/m1.cpp`), and masked-plus-stack stays at 61. The bigger diffs come first: the target materialises the `ok` flag (`mov r2, #0 … movne r2, #1; cmp r2, #0`) and reloads the flags byte a third time before the `b6` test, and the push set is different. Fix those before the slot order.
- **`ov000_021652e4`: what gives the two extra frame words.** The target's frame is `0x44`, with `tmpl` at `0x3c/0x40` and `0x34/0x38` never touched. Unused locals, a `Pos` for the row base, an `s64` product local and a by-value `Oam` return inline are all optimised away (the frame stays `0x3c`). A second `Oam` copy (`Oam tmpl2 = tmpl`, a block-local `Oam tt = tmpl` in the row loop, or a by-value `PutOam(Oam *, Oam)` inline) gives `0x44`, but MWCC stores into the copy, and the target doesn't. The best of these is `d/v12_rowloc.cpp` (161 / 61 against 156 / 56). None landed.
## A two-definition result keeps a 64-bit product from merging across branches (ov000, ov012, round ovbig9)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovbig9/d/`.

- **`func_ov000_021b9b14` went from 123 / 39 to 107 / 16 (`ovbig9/d/9b14_best16.cpp`), and the size now matches (521 words).** In block 1, ours compiled the `kind == 0xd` path (`FXC(0x1000 - t, 0x16000)`) and the `else` path (`FXG(t, 0x16000)`, after `t = 0x1000 - t` when `kind == 0xd`) as one product: the `0xd` arm did only the `rsb` and branched into the `else` arm. The target keeps two products. The `0xd` arm uses immediates (`adc rX, rY, #0`), and the `else` arm uses the zero register for both high words (`mla rX, rY, r4, rX`, `adc rX, rY, r4`). Only the `>> 12` tail is shared. Giving the `else` arm's result a second, identical definition stops the merge:
  ```c
  h = FXG(t, 0x16000) >> 12;
  if ((u32)self & 3) { h = FXG(t, 0x16000) >> 12; }
  o = 0;
  ```
  The `if` compiles away. The second definition must come before `o = 0` (after it: 39). Its condition matters a little: `self->kind & 3` gives 17, `t & 3` 18, `(u32)g & 3` 16. A two-definition `s64 k` constant, or `s64 r = 0x800`, fixes the `mla` but not the `adc` (19–36). The same line in the `kind != 0xd` arm, or in the `0xd` arm, makes it worse (27, 35).
  - Left: the `0x16000` is in `r0` across block 1 and then materialised again into `sb`. The target keeps it in `sb` from entry. Ours also sets `mov r5, #1` at entry, where the target sets it after block 1. In the last block, the target computes `FXG(t, k)` (with `k` from `movne/moveq #0x2e000/#0x1f000`) with the zero-register high words, and ours uses immediates. `s32`/`u32`/`s64` `k`, a two-definition `a`, a ternary constant, `(s64)(u32)k`, and one product per arm don't change that (16–26). Block 3's `mov r8, #0x68` comes before the `kind` branch in the target. Writing `q = 0x68` before the `if` or in each arm gives 23–24.
- **`func_ov012_02200cd4`: the `j = hit - 1` shape is found, but the total count doesn't improve.** In the target, `j` is not a folded `-1`. It is `sub r7, r5, #1` from `hit`'s zero, computed straight after the `func_ov000_02199e94` call and kept in `r7` across the hit block. Ours materialised `-1` at the call, as `sub r0, r1, #0x21` from the `0x20` mask argument. A two-definition `hit` (`hit = FALSE; if ((u32)self & 3) { hit = FALSE; }`) with `j = hit - 1;` moved directly after `x = func_ov000_02199e94(...)` gives the target's `sub` and its position. A two-definition `j = -1` gives the same. The two-definition zero for `re` changes nothing. With the permuter's `s16 dz` replaced by `s32` (which removes its extra `lsl`/`asr`), the draft is 93 / 33 (`ovbig9/d/0cd4_j_33.cpp`), against 72 / 27 for the saved best. What is left is scheduling: the `unk_a8 = 0` store comes after the sqrt in ours, the sin/cos table load order, the `Ov012_Flash` stores, and the tail `re = FALSE` copy (`mov r5, r4`). A clean redraft of the aim block (no `__typeof__` temporaries, all four zero stores together) is worse (55). The permuter's noise is holding the rest of the function's allocation in place, so this one needs a fresh draft from the target.
- **`func_ov000_02191f18` is still 84.** The per-prototype lever doesn't help: an `s32`/`u16`/`s16`/`u32` last parameter on `func_ov000_0219aae8` only (a 3+3 split by type) keeps the hoist, because the arguments are promoted back to `int`. `k = sc != 0 && sc != 0x1000`, a ternary, and an if/else for `k` all compile the same. The target also hoists `1` into `r8`, with only two uses (`strb r8` in case 24 and `orr r1, r0, r8, lsl r4` at the end), so hoisting isn't just a use-count threshold.

## The new levers on the closest register near-misses: one big gain, no matches (round nearmix2)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `nearmix2/d/<func>/`. `nearmix2/t/v.py` is ovbig7's variant scorer, retargeted, and it now keeps the base file's extension (`.c` drafts compile as C).

- **`func_02049c80`: 53 / 22 to 22 / 5 (`nearmix2/d/9c80/best_22_5.cpp`). The two-definition `-1` worked.** Declare `s32 none = -1;` as the first local, then `if ((u32)mgr & 3) { none = -1; }`. Use `none` for the first return, the three `!= none` tests and both clamps (`v < none - 0x7f`, `v = none - 0x7f`). That keeps `-1` in a callee-saved register and builds `-0x80` from it (`sub r0, rX, #0x7f`), as the target does. Declared after the other locals, `none` is the same code but lands in the wrong register (36 / 5). The condition variable hardly matters (`mgr`, `id`, `useSlot`, `slot` and `pitch` give 22; `pan` gives 27). Three things are left:
  - `none` and the zero swap `r4`/`r5`;
  - ours reloads `r0` from `mgr`'s copy before both first calls (`mov r0, r6`). The target keeps the incoming `r0` and loads the `useSlot`/`slot` stack parameters into `r2`, not `r0`;
  - the target's `slot >= 5` return is a fresh `mvnhs r0, #0`, where ours derives it from the zero (`subhs`).
  
  These made it worse or changed nothing: inverting the `useSlot` if/else (50), moving `result = 0` (49), a two-definition zero for `result` (51), a `BOOL useSlot`, `slot < 5` with an empty arm, `return (s32)0xffffffff`, `return none`, and the `slot` path as an inline returning `-1` (68).
- **These did not move:**
  - **`ov000_0218cfc8` (8 / 2):** two-definition `ret`, `g2` or `busy`, `busy` set in both arms, `g2 = (ActorGroup *)ret`, a `u8 *` manager pointer loaded before `ret = NULL`, and `ret = NULL` set in an `else`.
  - **`02068e68` (26 / 1):** `rotated` set in both arms, a two-definition `rotated = FALSE`, `shift` or `pop`, `rotated` as `u32` or `u8`, `rotated = pop = 1`, and inverting the `rx` test. None of them turns the `rx` branch's `mov r6, #1` into the target's copy of the hoisted 1.
  - **`ov003_021cd91c` (84 / 18):** a two-definition zero `z` in place of `self->pos.z + 0`, at the head, before the `pos` stores or in `case 0`. All are worse (112–245). The `if` merges into the entry chain, so the zero never gets the target's separate `sb` copy on the `state <= 0x1f` side.
  - **`ov002_021e7ea8` (28 / 0):** a block-local `b`, a two-definition `b = 0`, a `u8 *q` base for `g`, and a two-definition register pointer in `GX_SetMasterBrightness` (117).
  - **`0200d36c` (5 / 0):** a two-definition `word` index, and scalar `word`/`ctrl` in place of the permuter's array. The target keeps `r0` out of the read loop.
  - **`0203d298` (7 / 2):** a two-definition `repeat` zero, a two-definition `cur`, a `~cur` local, and a `u8 *` pad pointer.
  - **`ov009_0220acb8` (6 / 6):** without the `u16` cast, a precomputed `y + 0x1333` (as a local, inside the inline, or with a two-definition `0x1333`) stays at 15 / 11.
  - **`ov003_021d112c` (14 / 2):** `u8 *` source and destination pointers for the `08`/`0a` copy, in four orders, and a `u32` copy (18).
- **Not tried:** `ov003_021ead50`, `ov003_021cd744` and `ov012_021fbec8`.
- **Lesson.** A two-definition constant fixes a target that keeps a constant in a callee-saved register and builds other constants from it (`02049c80`, like `0220182c`). It does not help where the target copies a constant into a second register (`02068e68`, `021cd91c`). There the second definition is merged into the entry block chain, and the constant is still propagated.
## A clean redraft of `func_ov012_02200cd4` gets to 2 masked lines; `func_ov000_021b9b14` at 14 (round ovbig10)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovbig10/d/`. `ovbig10/t/vv.py base func mod spec.py` scores a dict of `(old, new)` replacement lists, 3 at a time.

- **`func_ov012_02200cd4` went from 93 / 33 (raw / masked) to 19 / 2 (`ovbig10/d/0cd4_best_19_2.cpp`, a from-scratch draft with no permuter temporaries). The size and push set now match.** In order of gain:
  - **`gm = NULL;` just before `func_ov000_0218dbe0(self, 0)`** (52 to 12). Without it, `gm` (the second part's `g2->unk_48`) is live from function entry on the `re` path, takes a callee-saved register across the whole hit block, and pushes `fp`. Initialised there, it shares the target's `mov r4, #0` with the `0` argument and the `re = FALSE` copy (`mov r5, r4`). Initialising it at its declaration is worse (25).
  - **`hit`, `x` and `j` declared at the `case 1:` scope, not in the hit block** (41 to 26 raw). This gives the target's `r5`/`r6`/`r7`/`r8` for `hit`/`x`/`j`/`g`. ovbig9's two-definition `hit` with `j = hit - 1;` straight after the `x = func_ov000_02199e94(...)` call is kept.
  - **The hit vector `hv` and case 11's `t` both declared at function scope, `t` first** (frame slots `0x30` and `0x24`), with **`hv.y` stored before `hv.x`/`hv.z`** (12 to 2).
  - **The aim distance is `sq = (s64)dx * dx; ... sq += (s64)dz * dz;`, with `oy = o->pos.y` read between the two terms.** That gives the target's `smull` on `dz` first and `smlal` on `dx`. `(s64)dz * dz + (s64)dx * dx` in one expression gives the opposite order, and `Ov012_DistXZ` is worse.
  - **Left (19 raw, 2 masked):** the `dz`/`dx` operand loads (the target loads `o->pos.z` before `self->pos.z`; ours loads `self` first, which is 6 register lines), and in the `Ov012_Flash` call a rotation of `r0`–`r3` with the `+ b` operand order (`add r0, r0, r2` against `add r1, r3, r1`). None of these changed either one: negated or reordered operands, `o->pos` read into locals or through a pointer, a `Ov012Vec` for the difference; `c` as `u8`/`s32`/array, six operand orders and `b * 3`; the Flash written out by hand, a `g` parameter, an inline taking `b`. A line-move hill climb over the aim and Flash lines found nothing.
- **`func_ov000_021b9b14` went from 16 to 14 masked (`ovbig10/d/9b14_best14.cpp`).** `tdc.py` (two-definition constants, within 4 lines of the use) found a two-definition `0x120` in block 3 (`kq = 0x120; if ((u32)self & 3) kq = 0x120;` before `p = (FXC(t, -0x104000) >> 12) + kq;`). It fixes the `adds`/`mla` order there. No other literal helps: every `1`, `0`, `0x16000` and `0x68` site at every earlier position stays at 14 or worse.
  - **A `K = 0x16000` local (plain, `const`, `s64`, declared first) compiles to the same code as the literal (16).** A two-definition `K` is much worse (55).
  - **Why `0x16000` is split, as far as can be seen:** the target keeps `1` out of block 1 (it materialises `mov r5, #1` after the block, in the slot ours uses for `mov sb, #0x16000`), and its block 1 uses `r5` as a product temporary. Ours keeps `1` in `r5` from entry, so block 1 is one register short, and `0x16000` goes to `r0` and is materialised again after the block. Fixing where the `1` lives should fix both. Most of its uses are the count argument inside the `G3Cmd1` inline, which `tdc.py` cannot reach. That is the next thing to try: a count parameter on `G3Cmd1`, or a two-definition `1` after block 1.

## Two operand-order levers finish `func_ov012_02200cd4` (round nearmix3)

- **`func_ov012_02200cd4` matched (19 / 2 to 0), in `src/ov012/ov012_02200200.cpp`.** The TU's `.text` now ends at `0x022015a0`. Two fixes:
  - **Aim block (19 to 13).** The target loads `o->pos.z` before `self->pos.z`, and `o->pos.x` before `self->pos.x`. Writing both differences as `dz = o->pos.z; dz = self->pos.z - dz;` (and the same for `dx`) gives that order, as in round ovbig4. Fixing only one of them gives 16.
  - **`Ov012_Flash` block (13 to 0).** The inline now takes only the byte `b` (as `s32`), and works out the colour inside, before the bit-field stores: `u8 c = (b << 1) + 0x14 + b; ... g[0x18d] = b * 2 + 6; g[0x18e] = c; g[0x18f] = c;`. Moving the `c` computation into the inline fixed the r0–r3 rotation and the schedule (1 diff left). `(b << 1)` in place of `b * 2` then fixed the `add r0, r0, r2` operand order. `b + (b * 2 + 0x14)` and `0x14 + b * 2 + b` also leave 1 diff, and the other orders are worse (3–5). With a `u8` parameter, or with the call passing three values, it stays at 13 or gets worse (22–36). A `g` parameter with `|= 0x1c` (the `ov014` `Shake` shape) is worse too (18–40).
  - **Lesson.** When `x * 2` is shared with another use, `x << 1` can change the operand order of the following `add`.
- **`func_02049c80` stays at 22 / 5 (`nearmix3/d/9c80/`).** These did not change it (all 22 / 5 unless noted): `volatile`, `bool`, `u8` or `s32` on `useSlot`/`slot` (22–25 / 5–9); `SndMgr *const mgr`; a `SndMgrX::Play` member function with `this`, which compiles to the same code; a `void *p` parameter, or a `SndMgr *` parameter copied into `mgr`, with the calls taking `p`; `&mgr->seqArcSlots[slot]` taken before the call, and other forms of the slot store; and `slot > 4`, `~0` or `result = -1` for the `slot >= 5` return. A two-definition `zero` local fixes the `none`/zero registers, but every `-1` then becomes a fresh `mvn` (39 / 9). `result = 0` moved into the `!useSlot` arm, or the slot check hoisted above it, is worse (34–51). Taking out the `id < 0` test still gives `mov r0, r6`, so the early return is not what clobbers `r0`. A scan of the built objects shows that keeping the incoming `r0` across a branch up to the first call is common in matched code (`func_020498ac` and about 30 others). So the `ldr r0, [sp, #0x2c]` here is a source-shape difference that we haven't found yet. The permuter (about 10 minutes, 2 jobs) found only noise (locals turned into parameters).
## A count parameter on `G3Cmd1` doesn't move the `1`; `021fbec8` goes from 24 to 17 (ov000, ov003, ov012, round ovbig11)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovbig11/d/`.

- **`func_ov012_021fbec8` went from 24 / 0 to 17 / 0 (`ovbig11/d/bec8/best_17_0.cpp`, the whole `ov012_0220a8d8` TU).** The timer/`unk_04` swap in `Bec8_S11` is gone if `ent->unk_04` is read inside the test and not through a `u16 w` local: `s->timer++; if (s->timer <= ent->unk_04 + 0x28) return;`. A `u16 t = s->timer` copy, or `if (s->timer > ...) {} else return;`, gives the same 17. `s32 lim = ent->unk_04 + 0x28`, `++s->timer` in the test and `!(... > ...)` stay at 24.
  - **Left: the `px`/`lim` swap in `Bec8_S1`.** The target has `lim` in `r4` (which it shares with the `0` after the compare) and `px` in `r6` (shared with `o` and `d1.z`). Ours has them the other way round. `lim` is live from function entry, because the `switch` has no default, so it is a long-lived value like `px`. These all stay at 17 or get worse: all 24 orders of the outer locals (17 or 170), all 120 orders of `S1`'s scalar locals (17–53), `lim` as an outer local passed by value (17) or by reference (27–182), `S1` written out in `case 1:` with `lim` at function scope (42–188), `px` set after the `idx` chain (26), `pl` passed in place of `px` (52), a `px_` copy at each position, a local `u16 t` in place of the parameter (17–43), a two-definition zero for `S1`'s four zero arguments (26–99), and `int`/`u32`/`u16`/`s16` types for `lim` and `px`.
- **`func_ov000_021b9b14` is still 14 masked.**
  - **The `1` is a live-range split, not a hoist.** The target also materialises `1` again in block 3 (`mov fp, #1` just before `if (a != 0)`) once `r5` is reused. So both builds hoist the constants to entry, and the allocator splits one of them across block 1. Ours splits `0x16000` (to `r0` in block 1, then `sb`); the target splits `1`.
  - **A count parameter doesn't change which one is split.** A `G3CmdN(cmd, value, n)` inline with `n = one` (plain at either end of block 1: 14; two-definition at function scope: 51–60; two-definition inside the first `if (h != 0)`: 16–37). Neither does renaming `t`, the order of the `h`/`o` locals and statements, or where `g` and `t` are declared (14–39).
  - **One product per arm in the last block (`a = FXG(t, 0x2e000) >> 12;` / `t = 0x1000 - t; a = FXG(t, 0x1f000) >> 12;`) gives the target's `movne`/`moveq` and its zero-register high words (`mla rX, rY, r4`, `adc rX, rY, r4`). That is ovbig9's open item (`ovbig11/d/9b14_arm_101_16.cpp`, 101 raw against 105).** It is 16 masked, not 14. The draft is one word longer: the sprite tail loads `-0x81a` from the pool twice, where the target keeps it in `r5`, and `x`/`w` move to `r7`/`sb`. Moving any one local declaration doesn't fix that (99–143 raw, 16–22 masked). Two-definition and other-type `k` forms of the old shape stay at 14–24.
  - Block 3's `mov r8, #0x68` is still merged into the tail: `q = 0x68` before the `kind` test (20–22), or a two-definition `q` in either arm (16).
- **`func_ov003_021ead50` (2) and `func_ov003_021cd744` (28 / 0) did not move.** For `ead50`: split `if (tbl != NULL)` tests, `goto`, and an empty `if` arm all stay at 2. For `cd744`: no new lever beyond the many already listed above.

## A two-definition sprite depth takes `func_ov000_021b9b14` to 11; `021fbec8`'s `px`/`lim` swap holds (ov000, ov012, round nearmix4)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `nearmix4/d/`. `nearmix4/t/vr.py` is `vv.py` with the replacements limited to one region of the file (`start` and `end` markers), for TUs where the same text appears in several functions.

That puts the size back to 521 words. Declared just before `x` it gives 104 raw. Other positions give 116–130 raw, all 11 masked. A plain `dp` makes no change (16). Setting `dp` before `sa = self->sprA` gives 31, `sprB->depth = sa->depth` gives 20, and a chained store gives 22. The same `dp` on the old 14 draft is worse (17).

The two-definition form keeps one hoisted `1` (in `fp`) and is worse (39–52). Extra dummy `0x16000` uses in blocks 1 and 2 still split `0x16000` (20–27). So the choice does not follow use counts. In the target, `flags.b19 = 1` is an `orr`, and the second `1` (`mov fp, #1`) is set just before `if (a != 0)`.
- **`func_ov012_021fbec8` stays at 17 / 0.** These all stay at 17: `default: break;`, case 1 before case 0, a `limc` copy, `Bec8_Lim(self, &lim)`, a shared `o`/`e` local in four positions, `u16`/`s32` casts in the compare, and `px` set in the same statement as `pl`. These are worse: an if-chain (23), `lim >= t` (18), an inverted test (46), `s->timer` in the test (120), `y`/`z` before `x` in the `unk_b8` stores (18–21), a `Bec8_Lim` inline that returns the value (53 raw / 0 masked: `lim` goes to `sb`), `S1` written out in `case 1:` with `lim` at five caller positions (42–188, the frame changes), and passing `pl->pos.x` straight in (176). Use-count probes (one fewer or more `o` calls or zero stores) never swap the pair. The only probe that moves `lim` to `r4` is `s32 lim = 0;` (`nearmix4/d/bec8/probe_liminit_48_7.cpp`, 48 / 7). Then `lim` is no longer live from entry, but `px` goes to `r5` and `t` to `r6`, and the extra `mov` is wrong. So the swap depends on `lim` being live from entry, and the target still gives it `r4`.
## An `IsValid` inline fixes the `8fc0` prologue; the `1f18` zero "hoist" is the entry `NULL` (ov000, ov003, round ovbig12)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovbig12/d/`.

- **`func_ov003_021d8fc0` went from 292 / 154 (61 with stack offsets masked) to 274 / 139 / 46 (`ovbig12/d/8fc0_best_274_139.cpp`).** ovbig6's `ok` flag with a `volatile` cast is replaced by bigfresh7's inline, called after the `if (!self->active) return;`:
  ```c
  static inline BOOL Seq_IsValid(const Seq *self) {
      BOOL r = FALSE;
      if (self->active) { r = self->entries != NULL; }
      return r;
  }
  ```
  This gives the target's whole prologue: the second and third reloads of the flags byte, `mov r1, #0 … movne r1, #1; cmp r1, #0`, and the `b6` test on a fresh `ldrb`. Passing both `SeqEntry e` and the pointer to `Seq_Run` (`Seq_Run(self, SEQ_CUR(self), &self->entries[self->index], ctl)`, `d/s2_d.cpp`) gives 270 raw, with the same masked counts.
  - **Left: the push set (`r3–r8` against `r4–r7`).** In the `Wait` block ours keeps the `index` it loaded for the entry copy (`add r0, r0, #1` in case 3). The target loads `index` again at the start of case 3. So ours needs one more register, and the first entry byte goes to `r8`, where the target uses `ip`. These don't fix it: a `Seq_Cur` inline that returns the struct, used in any combination of the four places it can go (`d/s3_*.cpp`, 257–280 raw, 137–215 masked); `Next` before `timer = 0` in case 3 (worse, 158); and `self->entries + self->index`, a `SeqEntry *q` local, or nested `if`s for the `Run` call (all give the same code).
  - **Slot order (`t/slots2.py txt`, which maps each slot to its first store).** The target lays the slots out depth-first in source order: `Wait.e` 0x54, its `Goto` 0x4e, `Exec.e` 0x48, `Rand` 0x42/0x3c, `Goto` 0x36, the copy at the bottom of the loop 0x30, the `Run` path 0x2a–0x12, then the outer locals `e` 0xc, `cur` 6 and the `Run` argument 0. Ours puts the loop's `cur` first (0x54) and the depth-2 copies after all the depth-1 copies.
- **`func_ov000_02191f18` is still 84 masked. The zero register is the `NULL` that `FindActor` returns at entry, CSE'd with the later zero arguments.** Once the 0x1000 hoist is gone (ovbig8's 3+3 split), `sb` holds zero only from `mov sb, #0; … moveq r5, sb` at entry to the case-8/11 height arguments and the `NULL` box arguments, and is reused for other values in the other switch arms. The target does `moveq r5, #0`. The ideas tried this round:
  - **A function-pointer local for each hit function stops the 0x1000 hoist** (the pointers take `r4`/`r5`, and `mov r3, #0x1000` appears in each case), but the calls become `blx` (`d/i2.cpp`), so it is no use as source. It does show that the 0x1000 hoist depends on there being free callee-saved registers, not only on the use count.
  - **Hit calls wrapped in inlines with the constants inside** (`d/i1.cpp`) compile exactly as before (84).
  - **`k = FALSE;` placed straight after `sc = (s16)m->unk_ce;`, before `if (h < 0)`, with the `k` test after it, gives the target's materialised flag** (`mov r0, #0 … movne r0, #1`, `d/1f18_kearly_87.cpp`), but masked goes up to 87, and with the 3+3 split to 75. An `IsSc` inline in four forms, a nested `if`, and `BOOL k = FALSE` at its declaration all still give `movne`/`moveq`.
  - Five more forms of `FindActor` (local with if, if/else, both ternaries) compile the same. Removing all four `NULL` boxes, or both zero heights, does not stop the zero CSE on its own.
  - `#pragma opt_common_subs off` (which one matched file uses) is worse (93) and keeps all three hoists. `opt_propagation`, `opt_loop_invariants`, `opt_lifetimes`, `opt_dead_assignments` and `opt_strength_reduction` change nothing.

The list, the scores and the variant specs are in the scratchpad under `fresh5/` (`left.txt`, `rescore.txt`, `d/<func>/s*.py`, which `t/vv.py` scores).

- **`ov003_021e1204` (2 / 0).** The target loads `unk_16` into the dead `e` register (`ldrh r1, [r1, #0x16]`), and ours loads it straight into `kind`'s `r0`. - **`ov010_02200a00` (2 / 2, plus two `blx` lines that resolve at link).** The target sets the zero `r8` before the `unk_fc &= ~0xa4` load. A zero local in four positions, direct field stores and moving the `VecSet` all stay at 2 or get worse. A two-definition zero gives 94.

- **`ov000_021b3e90` (5 / 1).** Writing the compare as `cam->unk_d6 - 1 != cam->unk_d4` fixes the target's `cmp r1, r0` operand order, but the registers then shift (7 / 1). The target uses `r0` for the global load and sets `r0 = ret` unconditionally before `popne`. Moving `ret = FALSE` and `return FALSE` around gives 5–85.
- **`ov010_0220441c` (8 / 0).** In the `flags |= 1ull << n` expansion, the target has `1` in `r1` and `0x20 - n` in `r2`, and ours has them the other way round. A `u64` temporary, `(u64)1`, `1ll`, a `Sub40 *` local, a `u64 *` field pointer, `u32`/`int`/`long` for `n` and a two-definition `1` all stay at 8 or get worse.

- **Lesson.** The overlay leftovers that nobody had touched today are single-register or single-scheduling misses that many rounds have already tried. Today's levers don't move them, so each needs a source shape no one has found yet.

## Splitting a shared pool at an aligned string, and the pools that stay blocked (round strpool5)

- **`func_ov006_021d3f04` matched by merging the files that use the second half of the ov006 pool.** The listed pool `0x021ebbb0..0x021ebc65` is two objects. `" "` ends exactly at the aligned `0x021ebc04`, and every string from `"nut00"` on is loaded only by functions in `0x021d3498..0x021d54b0`, so that part is a separate `@stringBase0`. The seven files there (`ov006_021d3498`, `3794`, `37d0`, `40a8`, `4118`, `4880`, `4f64`) and the literal draft of `021d3f04` (`scratchpad/ovlow2/d3f04.cpp`) went into `src/ov006/ov006_021d3498.cpp`, each in `namespace m_<addr> {}` with the includes hoisted to the top. `strrefs.py --literalize` then turned the `extern` strings into literals (delete the `extern ... "..."[];` lines it leaves behind). All 15 functions stay at 0, and the ROM check passes, so the pool order comes out right. The entry is `.text 0x021d3498..0x021d54b0`, `.rodata 0x021eb6fc..0x021eb708` (from the old `4118` entry; keep every section of the merged entries) and `.data 0x021ebc04..0x021ebc68`, followed by `strrefs.py ov006 --claim 0x021ebc04 0x021ebc68`. The merge script is `scratchpad/strpool5/t/merge.py out.cpp files...` (address order; `.c` files get `extern "C" {}`, `#define`s are `#undef`ed after each part).
- **A non-inline call's literal arguments were pooled left to right here.** `func_02035acc("ac/c1dum1", "c1dum1", 6)` puts `"ac/c1dum1"` first, which is the target's order. The right-to-left rule above was seen with inlined calls, so check the ROM rather than reorder by hand.
- **`relchk.py` reports every `@stringBase0` load as BAD**, because it resolves the name to a main-module symbol. Compare the offsets with the `add:` values that `--claim` wrote; the ROM check is what decides.
- **Still blocked.** The first half of the ov006 pool (`0x021ebbac..0x021ebc04`, with the `.data` word at `0x021ebbac`) needs `021cf414`/`021cf420`. The ov003 pool `0x021f8564..0x021f85c1` cannot be split (`"nut00"` is used from `021e1a68` to `021e6fb4`), so it still needs `021e1204` (2) and `021e6274` (9) besides `021e44dc` (0 with literals). Main TU1 (`0x0208b9dc`, needed for `0204c158`) still has `0204e5d4`, `0204f524`, `0204f5bc`, `0204ffdc` and `020508f4` unmatched in its span. TU2 (`0x0208bdec`, needed for `02058d10` and `02058f2c`) still has `0205c234`, `0205e29c`, `0205e5c4`, `02060a08` and `02060b18`.
## Sequencer and fx-block redrafts: `ov003_021d8fc0` and `ov012_021fbec8` match (round bigd7)

Drafts and scores are under `bigd7/d/` in the scratchpad.

- **`ov003_021d8fc0` (274 / 139 to 0).** This is a full redraft from the asm, now `src/ov003/ov003_021d8fc0.cpp`. In order, the steps that moved it:
  1. **Test the real flag.** The old draft's `ctl` local was really `!self->b2`. Dropping it fixed the push set.
  2. **Write Wait and Exec as blocks.** Writing them out as blocks inside the main function, instead of as inlines, fixed the frame.
  3. **Add volatile views.** The target re-reads `index` and `entries` where ours had CSE'd them. `*(volatile u16 *)&s->index` and `*(SeqEntry *volatile *)&s->entries` (the `VIDX`/`VENT` macros) reproduce the re-reads. Use them only at the reads that the target re-reads.
  4. **Reuse `*p`.** Using `*p` twice as a struct value (`SeqEntry e = *p;` and then `Seq_Count(self, *p)`) makes CSE create a late temp, which gives the target's last stack slot.
  5. **Merge the common tail.** Writing the `if`/`else` with the shared `timer = 0; ClearFlags` after it reproduces the target's merged tail.
  6. **Fix statement order.** `Seq_Next(self); self->timer = 0;`, with Next first, fixed the last register swaps.
- **`ov012_021fbec8` (17 / 0 to 0).** The `S1` inline is now written out as a block in `case 1:`. The outer locals are declared `pl, idx, ent, lim, t, px`, and the block's vectors are declared `d0, t0, d1, v1, v2`. That fixed the `px`/`lim` swap. The TU's `.text` now starts at `0x021fbec8`. `ov012_021fab7c` cannot fold into this TU because other files cover `021fb060`–`021fbec8`.
- **Stack slot order (mini tests, every compiler version):**
  1. Function-scope locals come first, then block locals, in declaration order from the top of the frame.
  2. Inline params and locals come next, breadth-first by inline depth. Locals inside one inline are allocated in reverse declaration order.
  3. CSE temps are allocated last.
  
  `#pragma inline_bottom_up`, `inline_depth` and `always_inline` have no effect on this order.
- **`relchk.py` false positive.** It reports `UNK <section>` for a pool load of the TU's own `.rodata` (`data_ov012_0220a3c4`), because MWCC relocates against the section symbol. The function `021fc688`, which was already matched, shows the same thing.
- **No movement:** `ov000_021b9b14` stays at 104 / 11. Reading `data_02097d48` directly instead of through `g` gives 265 / 75. `ov000_02191f18` stays at 84 masked. Other compiler versions and the inline/parameter variants gave nothing.

## The ov003 pool `0x021f8564` claimed: a missing call argument and a `u32` bit-test inline (ov003, round pool3)

- **`func_ov003_021e1204` (2 to 0): both callees take the entry's id as a second argument.** The target loads `unk_16` into `r1` because `r1` is still the id when `func_ov000_02193e5c` / `func_ov003_021d0a2c` are called: their matched definitions are `(res, s32 id)` and `(res, u16 id)`. The draft declared them with one parameter, so no prototype, inline or type change could move the load. `u16 id = e->unk_16; u16 kind = (u16)(id >> 12);` and `func_...(&self->unk_d0, id)` match. When a load lands in an odd argument register and stays there across a call, check the callee's real prototype before anything else.
- **`func_ov003_021e6274` (9 to 0): one `u8 n`, `n--`, and the `1 << n` test in an inline with a `u32` parameter.** Case 2 is `n = self->unk_18; ...; self->unk_93_1 = n; n--; func_ov000_02190e98(self->model, n, 0x1000, 0); if (Bit6274(self, n)) ...` with `static inline s32 Bit6274(Ov003Obj *o, u32 i) { return o->unk_1a & (1 << i); }`. With the test written in place (or the inline taking `u8`/`u16`/`s32`), MWCC computes `n - 1`'s `and #0xff` before the bit-field insert reads the old `n`, so the new `n` gets another register and `self`/`t` rotate (46). Small tests show the same thing: the order flips only when the old `n`'s last use is not a shift amount, and `n--` behaves differently from `n = n - 1`. Statement order makes no difference; only how the value is used later does.
- **The merge.** `src/ov003/ov003_021e0e8c.cpp` now holds the 16 files from `0x021e0e8c` to `0x021e7274` (built with `scratchpad/strpool5/t/merge.py`, one `namespace m_<addr>` each) plus the three drafts. The entry is `.text 0x021e0e8c..0x021e7274`, `.data 0x021f8564..0x021f85c0`. The pool ends at `0x021f85c0`, not `0x021f85c1`: `data_ov003_021f85c0` is a pointer table whose first word is zero, and `strrefs.py` reads that zero as `""`. `--literalize` turns `data_ov003_021f85c0[i]` into `""[i]`, so put those lines back by hand. It also turns the `extern "C" char data_...[]` declarations into `extern "C" char "..."[]` lines; delete those. All 62 functions match, and the ROM check passes. `021e219c`, `2360`, `2b50`, `2f54`, `45dc` and `51f8` show 5–10 `bl`/`blx` differences in `match.py`, as they did as separate files.

## The TU1/TU2 pool blockers have no wrong callee prototypes (main, round mainpool)

No new matches, so neither main pool is claimed. Drafts and tools are in the scratchpad under `mainpool/` (`d/<func>/`, `t/`).

- **Prototype check (the round pool3 lever).** `t/proto.py draft func` lists every call in the function's body with the draft's parameter count and the matched definition's. Run on the best drafts of `0204e5d4`, `0204ffdc`, `0204f5bc`, `020508f4`, `0205e29c` and `0205e5c4`, it finds no real mismatch; the flagged ones are regex misreads, checked by hand. The call sites in the ROM (`t/mdis.py addr` disassembles a main function and names its `bl` targets) agree with the drafts' argument registers. `0204f524`'s callees (`MIi_CpuClearFast`, `func_02039828`, `func_0204f49c`) are also right. `0205e29c`'s two unmatched ov000 callees take three arguments, as the draft says (`func_ov000_0218e6d0` does its own `& 0xf000`). `0205c234`, `02060a08` and `02060b18` make no calls. `0204ffdc` calls `func_0204c4fc`, whose matched definition ends in a C++ `bool` where the draft passes a `BOOL` ternary. The old draft no longer compiles against today's headers, and at 52 it was not worth porting.
- **Pool splits.** Neither pool can shrink. In TU1 (`0x0208b9dc`) the only aligned string inside the span is `"GREEN"` (`0x0208ba0c`), and `func_0204d040` loads it together with the colour names before it. In TU2 (`0x0208bdec`) the aligned `".cctmp"` belongs to `func_0205a840` along with `"."`/`".ccmes"`. The aligned `"MissionNameTbl"` (`0x0208be34`) would split the pool after `func_02061d64`, but every blocker comes before that. `0x0208be05` holds an unnamed `"%s_%.3d"`.
- **No movement:**
  - **`0204f524` (2 / 2):** `G2_GetOBJCharPtr`/`G2S_GetOBJCharPtr`-style inlines for the two VRAM bases (in the ternary, in a `base` local or in a clear inline), two ternary orientations, five types for `i` and three loop-init forms (90 variants) give 12 / 1 at best. That is the natural form's rotation.
  - **`02060a08` / `02060b18`:** the matched `Flags_AddSlot`/`Flags_ClearSlot` inlines from `StageCmd_11.cpp`, which take a `flags` pointer, give 108 and 26. Slot-pointer inlines for `b18`'s branch 2 (the permuter's "second slot pointer" hint from round mainpool11) give 23–40.
  - **`0205c234` (2):** `s32` parameters (as the ov000/ov003 callers declare them) change nothing. The header's `Slot_Alloc` + `StageRes_Last` form gives 48.
## A sprite-update giant redrafted from the target: `ov000_02166180` from 392 / 116 to 128 / 30 (round ovbig13)

No new matches. Drafts are in the scratchpad under `ovbig13/d/<addr>/` (`6180/wz2.cpp` is the best `02166180` draft). `ovbig13/t/dmove.py base func mod start end` is a greedy search that moves one declaration line at a time. Scores are raw / register-masked / register-and-`sp`-masked (`t/sc2.py`).

- **`ov000_02166180` (0xa68 bytes), in order of gain:**
  1. **Write the on-screen test out at each call site.** The old draft had a `Put(o, x, y)` inline. The target computes `&oams[j]` separately in each arm of the `x < 8 || x > 0xf8 || ...` test, and CSE then reuses that address for the later palette stores. A `PUTM(o, X, Y)` macro (`if (...) SetXY(o, 0, 0xc0); else SetXY(o, X, Y);`) reproduces this: 392 / 116 to 300 / 86.
  2. **Use `s32` coordinate temporaries.** Use `xx = ps->x + ex; yy = ps->y + ey;` in place of `s16` locals or arguments passed straight through, and the same for the two `nd->0x18` tails. That gives 278 / 77.
  3. **Compute `yy` before `xx`.** The target loads `pos[j].y` before `pos[j].x`. Computing `yy` first gives 215 / 47.
  4. **Put `ex - 4` and `ey - 4` in temporaries before the inner `PUTM`.** This reproduces the target's spilled `ex - 4`: a store to `[sp, #0x1c]` that is reloaded at once, while `ey - 4` stays in `r1` across the loop. That gives 159 / 34.
  5. **Declare `u8 sec;` and mask it in its own statement.** With `sec = (a + 0x1000) / 0x2000; sec &= 7;`, the struct index `&tbl[sec]` (`add r0, r0, r1, lsl #2`) stays separate from the `s8` index `sec * 4`, as in the target. Every other type or form folds both indexes into one shared `lsl #29` (142 / 30). Our build still emits an extra `and #0xff`.
  6. **Register-only gains (raw 146 to 128):** declaring `ex`, `ey` and `pos` inside the `i` loop, `r` after `p`, and writing the compare as `r.a == g[0xcb]`.
- **Still open in `02166180`.**
  - **Prologue.** The target loads `data_ov000_021ccd54` into `ip` before the busy test. Moving the `g` statement does not change the schedule.
  - **The `dy` load needs `data_ov000_021c9b41`.** The target loads `dy` from its own pool word. With that symbol, MWCC sinks the `dy` load to the `y` insert after the first store to `[p, #0x14]`, which scores 161 / 53. The 128 draft reads `dy` as `((s8 *)data_ov000_021c9b40)[sec * 4 + 1]`, which is the wrong relocation, so it can't land as it is.
  - **Register rotation** remains in the busy and `tbl2` blocks (`c`/`d`, `n`/`off`).
- **Looked at, not redrafted.** None of these drafts is structurally far off:
  - `ov000_021a7af0` (160 / 66): the diffs are where `0x200` and `0x52` are materialised.
  - `ov000_02164c50` (234 / 11): a register rotation from the first instruction.
  - `ov009_02204a10`: the best draft is `giants1/d/4a10/lp/pdz.cpp` at 49 / 25.
  - `ov000_021652e4` (106 / 46 / 23 `sp`-masked): the target's frame has 8 unused bytes at `[sp, #0x34]`. Unused `Oam` or `Pos` locals are dropped, and a `tmpl = src` copy is worse (152).

## The wrong-prototype check on every overlay near-miss: two matches (ov000, ov010, round proto8)

Tools are in the scratchpad under `proto8/t/`. `proto2.py cands` compares every callee declaration in a draft (and in the draft's own inlines) with the matched definition's parameter count, scalar widths and return type. `narrow.py` keeps only the differences that involve a count or a narrow type. `romargs.py cands` reads each callee's ROM code and reports a call where the callee reads `r1`–`r3` before writing them but the draft passes fewer arguments. `selfsig.py` compares a draft's own signature with how matched callers declare it. `fixsig.py draft callee out` rewrites one declaration to the matched widths. The candidates were 55 unmatched overlay functions at raw ≤ 20, plus 39 above that (ov004 and `ov000_02166180` left out).

- **`func_ov010_0220441c` (8 / 0 to 0).** The draft declared `func_ov003_021d982c(void *p)`. Its matched definition is `(Ov003Sequence *self, u8 v)`, and the ROM passes `1` in `r1`: the `1` of the `1ull << n` expansion is reused as the argument. `func_ov003_021d982c(self + 0x160, 1)` moves the `1` from `r2` to `r1`, and the `1 << n` operand swap that round fresh5 chased goes away. This is the same lever as round pool3's: a constant or load that lands in an odd argument register before a call is an argument of that call.
- **`func_ov000_021b628c` (16 / 0 to 0), in two steps.**
  1. **The callee prototypes.** `func_0206e230`/`func_0206e264` take the `LangBlockB *` (`self->unk_14`), and the draft declared them `(void)`. The target keeps `unk_14` in `r0` and tests its bytes through `r1`. Passing `self->unk_14` gives 12 / 0.
  2. **A copy and two compound subtractions.** The rest was the sine/cosine offset block. `t = pos; t.x -= FX_SinCosTable_[...]; t.z -= FX_SinCosTable_[... + 1];` matches. `t.y = pos.y; t.x = pos.x - ...;` gives 12. Other forms tried: `z` first (23), `y` last (14–16), an index local, a table-pointer local or negated operands (12), and a `&table[i]` pointer (19).
- **Checked, no gain.**
  - **Width-only differences (118 single-callee rewrites with `fixsig.py`).** None scored better. Most compile the same, and the rest are worse or fail to build.
  - **`ov000_021512b4`.** `func_ov000_0214bf2c` is defined `(void)`, but the ROM caller loads a `u16` into `r0` first, so the draft's argument is correct. The callee just ignores it.
  - **`ov010_02205444` (24 / 15).** The same `func_ov003_021d969c(self + 0x160, 1)` fix applies (`r1` still holds the entry's `1`), but it compiles to identical code.
  - **`ov006_021e51f8`.** The `r1` that holds the `1` is not an argument of `func_02045ea4`.
  - **`ov000_021c4460`.** Its `r1`/`r2` difference is the `VOLR` reload of `unk_e4`, not a prototype. Dropping it gives 10 / 0.
  - **`ov008_021ce518`.** The remaining 1 / 1 is the switch pivot.
  - **Other reports.** The other `romargs.py` reports are struct returns (hidden `r0`) or regex misreads of call sites.
- **New files:**
  - `src/ov010/ov010_0220441c.cpp` (`.text 0x0220441c..0x022046ac`);
  - `src/ov000/Cam_021b628c.cpp` (`.text 0x021b628c..0x021b65ec`).
